+param(
+    [string]$JavaHome='D:\jdk-17.0.19.10-hotspot',
+    [string]$Maven='C:\Program Files\apache-maven-3.9.12\bin\mvn.cmd',
+    [string]$OutputRoot=('target/wd007-checks-'+(Get-Date -Format 'yyyyMMdd-HHmmss')),
+    [string]$BridgeExecutable,
+    [string]$KimiConsumer=(Join-Path $env:APPDATA 'uv/tools/kimi-cli/Lib/site-packages/kimi_cli/soul/approval.py')
+)
+$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
+if(Test-Path -LiteralPath $OutputRoot){throw 'Use a new output directory; evidence is never overwritten.'}
+New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $OutputRoot | Out-Null
+$env:JAVA_HOME=$JavaHome
+& $Maven -o '-Dmaven.repo.local=.m2repo' -DskipTests test-compile *> (Join-Path $OutputRoot 'compile.log')
+if($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0){throw 'Compilation failed; see compile.log'}
+& python (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'wd007/run-checks.py') --java (Join-Path $JavaHome 'bin/java.exe') --output (Join-Path $OutputRoot 'behavior')
+$behaviorExit=$LASTEXITCODE
+& pwsh -NoProfile -File (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'wd007/test-managed-selection.ps1') *> (Join-Path $OutputRoot 'managed-selection.log')
+if($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0){throw 'Managed selection checks failed'}
+if($BridgeExecutable){
+    & python (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'wd007/test-managed-ipc.py') --bridge $BridgeExecutable *> (Join-Path $OutputRoot 'managed-ipc.log')
+    if($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0){throw 'Managed IPC process checks failed'}
+}
+if(Test-Path -LiteralPath $KimiConsumer){
+    & python (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'wd007/test-kimi-consumer.py') --java (Join-Path $JavaHome 'bin/java.exe') --consumer $KimiConsumer *> (Join-Path $OutputRoot 'kimi-consumer.log')
+    if($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0){throw 'Installed Kimi consumer replay failed'}
+} else {throw 'Supply the read-only installed Kimi consumer with -KimiConsumer'}
+if($behaviorExit -ne 0){throw 'One or more behavior suites failed; see behavior/RESULTS.json'}
+Write-Output "PASS WD-007 machine checks: $OutputRoot (not hardware/UAT or package admission)"

+using System;
+using System.Collections.Generic;
+using System.IO;
+using System.Linq;
+using System.Net;
+using System.Net.Sockets;
+using System.Text;
+using System.Threading;
+using System.Threading.Tasks;
+using System.Web.Script.Serialization;
+using System.Windows.Forms;
+using System.Drawing;
+using Windows.Devices.Bluetooth;
+using Windows.Devices.Bluetooth.GenericAttributeProfile;
+using Windows.Devices.Enumeration;
+
+namespace BLE_tcp_driver {
+    // UI owns only user intent. No BleCore, TCP listener, or firmware writer exists here.
+    internal sealed class ManagedBridgeWindow : Form {
+        private readonly ListBox devices = new ListBox { Dock=DockStyle.Fill };
+        private readonly Label status = new Label { Dock=DockStyle.Bottom,Height=65,Text="正在连接 AhaKey Studio…",AutoEllipsis=true };
+        private readonly NotifyIcon tray;
+        private readonly Action<string,Dictionary<string,object>> intent;
+        private bool exiting;
+        private string selectionAuthority="";
+        private Button connect;
+        private sealed class Choice {
+            public string Id,Name,Address;
+            public override string ToString() { return Name+"  ["+Address+"]"; }
+        }
+        public ManagedBridgeWindow(Action<string,Dictionary<string,object>> intent) {
+            this.intent=intent;Text="AhaKey 蓝牙桥";Size=new Size(550,350);MinimumSize=new Size(420,260);
+            var actions=new FlowLayoutPanel { Dock=DockStyle.Top,Height=40 };
+            var refresh=new Button { Text="刷新设备",AutoSize=true };
+            connect=new Button { Text="连接所选键盘",AutoSize=true,Enabled=false };
+            var disconnect=new Button { Text="断开配置连接",AutoSize=true };
+            refresh.Click+=(s,e)=>intent("refreshRequested",new Dictionary<string,object>());
+            connect.Click+=(s,e)=>{
+                var item=devices.SelectedItem as Choice;if(item==null){status.Text="请先选择键盘。";return;}
+                status.Text="已请求连接 "+item.Name+"…";
+                intent("selectionRequested",new Dictionary<string,object>{{"selectedTargetId",item.Id},{"address",item.Address},{"selectionAuthority",selectionAuthority}});
+            };
+            disconnect.Click+=(s,e)=>intent("disconnectRequested",new Dictionary<string,object>());
+            actions.Controls.AddRange(new Control[]{refresh,connect,disconnect});Controls.Add(devices);Controls.Add(status);Controls.Add(actions);
+            tray=new NotifyIcon { Icon=SystemIcons.Application,Text="AhaKey 蓝牙桥",Visible=true };
+            var menu=new ContextMenuStrip();menu.Items.Add("显示蓝牙桥",null,(s,e)=>Reveal());
+            menu.Items.Add("隐藏窗口",null,(s,e)=>Hide());tray.ContextMenuStrip=menu;tray.DoubleClick+=(s,e)=>Reveal();
+            FormClosing+=(s,e)=>{if(!exiting && e.CloseReason==CloseReason.UserClosing){e.Cancel=true;Hide();}};
+            FormClosed+=(s,e)=>{tray.Visible=false;tray.Dispose();};
+        }
+        public void Reveal() { Show();WindowState=FormWindowState.Normal;Activate(); }
+        public void SetStatus(string text) { status.Text=text; }
+        public void SetAuthority(string value) { selectionAuthority=value;connect.Enabled=value.Length>0; }
+        public void SetChoices(List<Dictionary<string,object>> choices) {
+            string selected=(devices.SelectedItem as Choice)?.Id;
+            devices.BeginUpdate();devices.Items.Clear();
+            foreach(var row in choices){var item=new Choice {Id=(string)row["targetId"],Name=(string)row["name"],Address=(string)row["address"]};devices.Items.Add(item);if(item.Id==selected)devices.SelectedItem=item;}
+            devices.EndUpdate();
+        }
+        public void ExitOwner() { exiting=true;Close(); }
+    }
+    internal sealed class SelectionSnapshot<T> {
+        private Dictionary<string,T> candidates=new Dictionary<string,T>();
+        public string Id { get; private set; } = "";
+        public string Publish(Dictionary<string,T> found,bool observationOnly,bool sessionActive) {
+            if(!observationOnly && !sessionActive){candidates=new Dictionary<string,T>(found);Id=Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");}
+            return Id;
+        }
+        public bool Contains(string id,string target){return id==Id && candidates.ContainsKey(target);}
+    }
+    // Studio-only control plane. The legacy Form/TCP listener never starts in this mode.
+    internal sealed class ManagedBridge {
+        private readonly BleCore core = new BleCore();
+        private readonly object gate = new object(), outputGate = new object();
+        private readonly string instance = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
+        private readonly string token = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("AHAKEY_BRIDGE_TOKEN");
+        private readonly HashSet<string> requestIds = new HashSet<string>();
+        private readonly SelectionSnapshot<DeviceInformation> selection=new SelectionSnapshot<DeviceInformation>();
+        private readonly BoundedAsyncFifo<Write> writes;
+        private string session = "", target = "", clientGeneration = "";
+        private long epoch, eventSequence;
+        private TcpClient owner;
+        private CancellationTokenSource cancellation = new CancellationTokenSource();
+        private bool authenticated;
+        private int outstanding;
+        private ManagedBridgeWindow window;
+        private long uiSequence;
+        private void Ui(Action action) {
+            var current=window;if(current==null||current.IsDisposed)return;
+            try { current.BeginInvoke(action); } catch(InvalidOperationException) { }
+        }
+        private sealed class Write {
+            public string Session;
+            public long Generation;
+            public GattCharacteristic Characteristic;
+            public byte[] Bytes;
+            public CancellationToken Token;
+        }
+        public ManagedBridge() {
+            writes = new BoundedAsyncFifo<Write>(24, SendWrite, (w,e) => {});
+            core.ReceiveNotifyData += (sender, data, generation, discovery) => {
+                lock (gate) {
+                    if (session.Length == 0 || !core.IsCurrentConnection(generation)) return;
+                    Event("notify", Map("bytes", Convert.ToBase64String(data)));
+                }
+            };
+            core.DeviceDisconnected += device => {
+                long captured=Interlocked.Read(ref epoch);
+                // BleCore invokes this while holding its discovery lock. Never acquire gate on that stack.
+                _ = Task.Run(() => { lock(gate) {if(captured!=epoch || !ReferenceEquals(device,core.CurrentDevice))return;Event("sessionDisconnected",Map());Revoke();} });
+            };
+        }
+        public static void Run(string[] args) {
+            int index = Array.IndexOf(args, "--port"); int port;
+            if (index < 0 || index + 1 >= args.Length || !int.TryParse(args[index + 1], out port) || port < 1 || port > 65535) return;
+            Application.EnableVisualStyles();Application.SetCompatibleTextRenderingDefault(false);
+            var bridge=new ManagedBridge();
+            bridge.window=new ManagedBridgeWindow((name,fields)=>{lock(bridge.gate){if(bridge.authenticated)bridge.Event(name,fields);}});
+            bool started=false;
+            bridge.window.Shown+=(s,e)=>{if(started)return;started=true;Task.Run(async()=>{try{await bridge.RunAsync(port);}finally{bridge.Ui(()=>bridge.window.ExitOwner());}});};
+            Application.Run(bridge.window);
+        }
+        private async Task RunAsync(int port) {
+            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(token)) return;
+            var listener = new TcpListener(IPAddress.Loopback, port);
+            try {
+                listener.Start(1);
+                var accept = listener.AcceptTcpClientAsync();
+                if (await Task.WhenAny(accept, Task.Delay(5000)) != accept) return;
+                owner = await accept; listener.Stop(); owner.NoDelay = true; owner.SendTimeout = 2000;
+                var stream = owner.GetStream();
+                while (true) {
+                    byte[] header = authenticated ? await Read(stream,3) : await Deadline(Read(stream,3),2000);
+                    int length = header[1] | header[2] << 8;
+                    if (header[0] != 0x10 || length < 2 || length > 16384) throw new IOException("BAD_FRAME");
+                    var data = new JavaScriptSerializer().DeserializeObject(Encoding.UTF8.GetString(await Deadline(Read(stream, length),2000))) as Dictionary<string, object>;
+                    if (data == null || Int(data, "version") != 1) throw new IOException("BAD_VERSION");
+                    string id = Text(data, "requestId"), op = Text(data, "op");
+                    if (!requestIds.Add(id) || requestIds.Count > 100000) { Reply(id, false, "DUPLICATE_REQUEST"); continue; }
+                    if (!authenticated) {
+                        if (op != "hello" || Text(data, "ownerToken") != token) throw new IOException("OWNER_MISMATCH");
+                        authenticated = true;
+                        Reply(id, true, "OK", Map("capabilities", new[]{"enumerate-connected","select-target","release-session","session-writes","visible-window"}));
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    if (Text(data,"bridgeInstanceId") != instance) { Reply(id, false, "INSTANCE_CHANGED"); continue; }
+                    // Cancellation is handled here, never queued behind a GATT write.
+                    if (op == "disconnect" || op == "cancelRequest" || op == "shutdown") {
+                        lock (gate) {
+                            if (op == "disconnect" && session.Length != 0 && Text(data,"sessionId") != session) { Reply(id,false,"SESSION_CHANGED"); continue; }
+                            Revoke();
+                        }
+                        Reply(id,true,"REVOKED",Map("revoked",true));
+                        if (op == "shutdown") return;
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    if (Interlocked.Increment(ref outstanding) > 24) { Interlocked.Decrement(ref outstanding); Reply(id,false,"BUSY"); continue; }
+                    _ = Task.Run(async () => {
+                        try { await Handle(id, op, data); }
+                        catch (Exception e) { Reply(id,false,e is TimeoutException ? "TIMEOUT" : e.Message); }
+                        finally { Interlocked.Decrement(ref outstanding); }
+                    });
+                }
+            } catch (Exception) { }
+            finally { listener.Stop(); lock(gate) Revoke(); owner?.Close(); }
+        }
+        private async Task Handle(string id, string op, Dictionary<string,object> data) {
+            if(op=="showWindow") {Ui(()=>window.Reveal());Reply(id,true,"OK");return;}
+            if(op=="studioStatus") {
+                string message=Text(data,"message"),authority=Text(data,"selectionAuthority");long sequence=Convert.ToInt64(data["sequence"]);
+                lock(gate){if(sequence>uiSequence){uiSequence=sequence;Ui(()=>{if(sequence==Interlocked.Read(ref uiSequence)){window.SetStatus(message);window.SetAuthority(authority);}});}}
+                Reply(id,true,"OK");return;
+            }
+            if (op == "enumerateConnected") {
+                bool observationOnly=data.ContainsKey("observationOnly") && data["observationOnly"] is bool && (bool)data["observationOnly"];
+                long captured; lock(gate) { captured=epoch; }
+                var found = await Deadline(DeviceInformation.FindAllAsync(BluetoothLEDevice.GetDeviceSelectorFromConnectionStatus(BluetoothConnectionStatus.Connected),
+                    new[]{"System.Devices.Aep.DeviceAddress","System.Devices.Aep.IsConnected"}).AsTask(), 4500);
+                var list = new List<Dictionary<string,object>>();
+                var discovered=new Dictionary<string,DeviceInformation>();
+                lock (gate) {
+                    if (captured!=epoch) throw new IOException("CANCELLED");
+                    foreach (var d in found) {
+                        object connected;
+                        if (!d.Name.StartsWith("AhaKey",StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
+                            || !d.Properties.TryGetValue("System.Devices.Aep.IsConnected",out connected) || !(connected is bool) || !(bool)connected) continue;
+                        discovered[d.Id]=d;
+                        object address;d.Properties.TryGetValue("System.Devices.Aep.DeviceAddress",out address);
+                        list.Add(Map("targetId",d.Id,"name",d.Name,"address",address as string??"","observedConnected",true,"serviceEvidence","connect-verifies-services"));
+                    }
+                    Reply(id,true,"OK",Map("snapshotId",selection.Publish(discovered,observationOnly,session.Length!=0),"candidates",list));
+                    if(!observationOnly)Ui(()=>window.SetChoices(list));
+                }
+                return;
+            }
+            if (op == "connect") {
+                string wanted=Text(data,"targetId"), generation=Text(data,"clientGeneration"); long captured;
+                lock(gate) {
+                    if(session.Length!=0 || !selection.Contains(Text(data,"snapshotId"),wanted)) throw new IOException("TARGET_CHANGED");
+                    captured=Interlocked.Increment(ref epoch); session="connecting"; target=wanted; clientGeneration=generation;
+                }
+                BluetoothLEDevice device=null;
+                try {
+                    var opening=BluetoothLEDevice.FromIdAsync(wanted).AsTask();
+                    try { device=await Deadline(opening,4000); }
+                    catch { _ = opening.ContinueWith(t=>{if(t.Status==TaskStatus.RanToCompletion)t.Result?.Dispose();}); throw; }
+                    if(device==null || device.ConnectionStatus!=BluetoothConnectionStatus.Connected || !device.Name.StartsWith("AhaKey",StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) throw new IOException("TARGET_NOT_CONNECTED");
+                    var ready=new TaskCompletionSource<bool>(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
+                    Action<long,long,bool> callback=(g,d,ok)=>{
+                        if(ok) core.TryConfirmReady(g,d,(dev,ch)=>ready.TrySetResult(true));
+                        else ready.TrySetResult(false);
+                    };
+                    core.AllCharacteristicsDiscovered+=callback;
+                    try {
+                        lock(gate) { if(captured!=epoch)throw new IOException("CANCELLED"); core.ConnectManagedDevice(device); device=null; }
+                        if(!await Deadline(ready.Task,14000)) throw new IOException("SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE");
+                        lock(gate) {
+                            if(captured!=epoch || !core.GetBleStatusInfo().IsTargetDevice) throw new IOException("SESSION_CHANGED");
+                            session=Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
+                            Reply(id,true,"READY",Map("sessionId",session,"targetId",target,"clientGeneration",generation));
+                        }
+                    } finally { core.AllCharacteristicsDiscovered-=callback; }
+                } catch { lock(gate) {if(captured==epoch)Revoke();} throw; }
+                finally { device?.Dispose(); }
+                return;
+            }
+            if(op=="write") {
+                Write write;
+                lock(gate) {
+                    if(session.Length==0 || session=="connecting" || Text(data,"sessionId")!=session || Text(data,"targetId")!=target || Text(data,"clientGeneration")!=clientGeneration) throw new IOException("SESSION_CHANGED");
+                    byte[] bytes=Convert.FromBase64String(Text(data,"bytes"));
+                    string channel=Text(data,"channel");
+                    if(bytes.Length==0 || bytes.Length>4096 || (channel!="command" && channel!="data")) throw new IOException("BAD_WRITE");
+                    write=new Write{Session=session,Generation=core.ConnectionGeneration,Bytes=bytes,
+                        Characteristic=channel=="command"?core.CurrentWriteCharacteristic:core.CurrentDataCharacteristic,Token=cancellation.Token};
+                }
+                Task<bool> completion;
+                if(!writes.TryEnqueue(write,out completion))throw new IOException("BUSY");
+                if(!await Deadline(completion,10000))throw new IOException("WRITE_UNCONFIRMED");
+                Reply(id,true,"TRANSPORT_WRITTEN",Map("transportWritten",true)); return;
+            }
+            throw new IOException("UNKNOWN_OPERATION");
+        }
+        private async Task<bool> SendWrite(Write w) {
+            using(var timeout=CancellationTokenSource.CreateLinkedTokenSource(w.Token)) {
+                timeout.CancelAfter(5000);
+                int pdu; string source; int size=core.GetMaxGattWritePayload(w.Generation,out pdu,out source);
+                return await BridgeMtuPolicy.SendFragmentsAsync(w.Bytes,size,async(offset,bytes,index,count)=>{
+                    if(!Current(w)||timeout.IsCancellationRequested)return false;
+                    var result=await core.WriteDataToCharacteristicAsync(w.Characteristic,bytes,w.Generation,timeout.Token);
+                    return result.Success && Current(w) && !timeout.IsCancellationRequested;
+                },()=>Current(w)&&!timeout.IsCancellationRequested);
+            }
+        }
+        private bool Current(Write w) { lock(gate)return session==w.Session && !w.Token.IsCancellationRequested && core.IsCurrentConnection(w.Generation); }
+        private void Revoke() {
+            Interlocked.Increment(ref epoch); session=""; target=""; clientGeneration="";
+            cancellation.Cancel(); cancellation=new CancellationTokenSource();
+            writes.CancelWhere(w=>true); core.Dispose();
+        }
+        private void Event(string name,Dictionary<string,object> values) {
+            values["event"]=name;values["eventSeq"]=++eventSequence;values["sessionId"]=session;
+            values["targetId"]=target;values["clientGeneration"]=clientGeneration; Send(0x91,values);
+        }
+        private void Reply(string id,bool ok,string code,Dictionary<string,object> values=null) {
+            values=values??Map();values["requestId"]=id;values["ok"]=ok;values["code"]=code;Send(0x90,values);
+        }
+        private void Send(byte type,Dictionary<string,object> values) {
+            try { lock(outputGate) {
+                values["version"]=1;values["bridgeInstanceId"]=instance;
+                byte[] body=Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(new JavaScriptSerializer().Serialize(values));
+                if(body.Length>16384)throw new IOException("FRAME_TOO_LARGE");
+                var stream=owner.GetStream();byte[] packet=new byte[body.Length+3];packet[0]=type;packet[1]=(byte)body.Length;packet[2]=(byte)(body.Length>>8);Buffer.BlockCopy(body,0,packet,3,body.Length);stream.Write(packet,0,packet.Length);
+            }} catch { owner?.Close(); }
+        }
+        private static async Task<byte[]> Read(NetworkStream stream,int count) {byte[] bytes=new byte[count];int at=0;while(at<count){int read=await stream.ReadAsync(bytes,at,count-at);if(read==0)throw new EndOfStreamException();at+=read;}return bytes;}
+        private static async Task<T> Deadline<T>(Task<T> task,int ms) {if(await Task.WhenAny(task,Task.Delay(ms))!=task)throw new TimeoutException();return await task;}
+        private static string Text(Dictionary<string,object> value,string key) {object item;if(!value.TryGetValue(key,out item)||!(item is string)||((string)item).Length>12000)throw new IOException("BAD_FIELD_"+key);return (string)item;}
+        private static int Int(Dictionary<string,object> value,string key) {object item;return value.TryGetValue(key,out item)&&item is int?(int)item:-1;}
+        private static Dictionary<string,object> Map(params object[] pairs) {var value=new Dictionary<string,object>();for(int i=0;i<pairs.Length;i+=2)value[(string)pairs[i]]=pairs[i+1];return value;}
+    }
+}

### scripts/wd007/ManagedWindowSmoke.cs

+using System;
+using System.Collections.Generic;
+using System.Drawing;
+using System.Reflection;
+using System.Windows.Forms;
+
+// Loads the actual compiled window, without constructing ManagedBridge or any Bluetooth object.
+public static class ManagedWindowSmoke {
+    [STAThread] public static int Main(string[] args) {
+        Application.EnableVisualStyles();
+        var assembly=Assembly.LoadFrom(args[0]);
+        var type=assembly.GetType("BLE_tcp_driver.ManagedBridgeWindow",true);
+        int requests=0,phase=0;Exception failure=null;
+        Action<string,Dictionary<string,object>> intent=(name,fields)=>{requests++;if(name=="selectionRequested" && ((string)fields["selectedTargetId"]!="B" || (string)fields["address"]!="AA:02" || (string)fields["selectionAuthority"]!="observed-A"))throw new Exception("selection lost observed authority/address");};
+        var form=(Form)Activator.CreateInstance(type,BindingFlags.Public|BindingFlags.NonPublic|BindingFlags.Instance,null,new object[]{intent},null);
+        var timer=new Timer {Interval=120};
+        timer.Tick+=(s,e)=>{
+            try {
+                if(phase==0) {
+                    if(!form.Visible || !form.IsHandleCreated)throw new Exception("window not created and visible");
+                    type.GetMethod("SetChoices").Invoke(form,new object[]{new List<Dictionary<string,object>>{
+                        new Dictionary<string,object>{{"targetId","A"},{"name","AhaKey"},{"address","AA:01"}},
+                        new Dictionary<string,object>{{"targetId","B"},{"name","AhaKey"},{"address","AA:02"}}}});
+                    type.GetMethod("SetStatus").Invoke(form,new object[]{"离线窗口测试：同名不同地址；未访问设备。"});
+                    using(var bitmap=new Bitmap(form.Width,form.Height)){form.DrawToBitmap(bitmap,new Rectangle(0,0,bitmap.Width,bitmap.Height));bitmap.Save(args[1]);}
+                    form.Close();
+                    if(form.IsDisposed || form.Visible)throw new Exception("X did not hide live window");
+                } else if(phase==1) {
+                    type.GetMethod("Reveal").Invoke(form,null);
+                    if(!form.Visible || form.IsDisposed)throw new Exception("existing window not restored");
+                    foreach(Control control in form.Controls)if(control is FlowLayoutPanel)
+                        foreach(Control button in control.Controls)if(button.Text=="刷新设备")((Button)button).PerformClick();
+                    if(requests!=1)throw new Exception("UI did not emit exactly one refresh intent");
+                    type.GetMethod("SetAuthority").Invoke(form,new object[]{"observed-A"});
+                    foreach(Control control in form.Controls)if(control is ListBox)((ListBox)control).SelectedIndex=1;
+                    foreach(Control control in form.Controls)if(control is FlowLayoutPanel)
+                        foreach(Control button in control.Controls)if(button.Text=="连接所选键盘")((Button)button).PerformClick();
+                    if(requests!=2)throw new Exception("current window did not emit selected address/authority");
+                } else {timer.Stop();type.GetMethod("ExitOwner").Invoke(form,null);}
+                phase++;
+            }catch(Exception error){failure=error;timer.Stop();type.GetMethod("ExitOwner").Invoke(form,null);}
+        };
+        form.Shown+=(s,e)=>timer.Start();Application.Run(form);timer.Dispose();
+        if(failure!=null){Console.Error.WriteLine(failure);return 1;}
+        if(phase!=3 || !form.IsDisposed)throw new Exception("owner exit did not dispose window");
+        Console.WriteLine("PASS actual compiled WinForms: create/show, two addresses, close-hide, same-window reveal, intent, owner exit; no Bluetooth");return 0;
+    }
+}

+import argparse, concurrent.futures, hashlib, json, subprocess, time
+from pathlib import Path
+
+parser=argparse.ArgumentParser()
+parser.add_argument('--java',required=True)
+parser.add_argument('--output',required=True)
+parser.add_argument('--filter',default='')
+args=parser.parse_args()
+output=Path(args.output).resolve();output.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False)
+classes=[
+ 'service.Wd007FirmwareMatrixChecks',
+ 'service.Wd007CursorRuntimeChecks',
+ 'service.Wd007CanvasReadbackChecks','service.Wd007ReadFailureChecks','service.Wd007TransferChecks',
+ 'service.Wd007HookManagementChecks','service.Wd007HookUiChecks',
+ 'service.connection.ManagedBridgeClientChecks','service.Wd007BehaviorChecks','service.Wd007SafetyChecks','service.Wd007RecoveryControllerChecks','service.Wd007UsbTerminationChecks','service.Wd007BridgeRetirementChecks','service.Wd007WindowExitChecks','service.KimiBridgeLifecycleCheck',
+ 'displayconfig.DisplayConfigChecks','displayconfig.DisplayConfigCapabilityChecks','displayconfig.DisplayConfigIsolationChecks',
+ 'displayconfig.DisplayConfigRaceChecks','displayconfig.DisplayConfigFxChecks','displayconfig.DisplayConfigRevision3Checks',
+ 'displayconfig.DisplayConfigRevision3UiChecks','displayconfig.DisplayConfigRound5Checks',
+ 'protocol.K1RouteProtocolChecks','packaging.K1RouteEditorChecks','packaging.Wd007DraftReadbackChecks','packaging.WindowCloseChecks',
+ 'firmware.DeviceMaintenanceChecks','firmware.BackupRestoreChecks','firmware.FirmwareUpdateChecks','firmware.SimpleFlashChecks',
+ 'update.UpdateCenterChecks','update.ReleaseSignatureChecks','service.SpeechLifecycleCheck']
+if args.filter:classes=[c for c in classes if c.rsplit('.',1)[1] in args.filter.split(',')]
+def run(name):
+    log=output/(name.rsplit('.',1)[1]+'.log');home=output/(name.rsplit('.',1)[1]+'-home');home.mkdir()
+    command=[args.java,'-Dfile.encoding=UTF-8','-Duser.home='+str(home),'-Dwd007.artifacts='+str(home/'artifacts'),'-cp','target/test-classes;target/classes;target/lib/*','com.example.ahakey.'+name]
+    if name.endswith('DisplayConfigRevision3UiChecks'):command.append(str(output/'display-ui.png'))
+    if name.endswith(('SimpleFlashChecks','ReleaseSignatureChecks')):command.append(str(output/(name.rsplit('.',1)[1]+'-artifacts')))
+    started=time.monotonic()
+    with log.open('wb') as stream:
+        try:exit_code=subprocess.run(command,stdout=stream,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,timeout=180).returncode
+        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:exit_code='TIMEOUT'
+    result=dict(test=name,command=command,exit=exit_code,seconds=round(time.monotonic()-started,3),log=str(log),sha256=hashlib.sha256(log.read_bytes()).hexdigest())
+    print(name,exit_code,flush=True);return result
+with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:results=list(pool.map(run,classes))
+(output/'RESULTS.json').write_text(json.dumps(results,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+raise SystemExit(0 if results and all(r['exit']==0 for r in results) else 1)

+"""Replay the installed consumer's exact loader against Java's compatibility endpoint."""
+import argparse, hashlib, socket, struct, subprocess
+from pathlib import Path
+
+parser=argparse.ArgumentParser()
+parser.add_argument('--java',required=True)
+parser.add_argument('--consumer',required=True)
+args=parser.parse_args()
+source=Path(args.consumer).read_text(encoding='utf-8')
+loader=source[source.index('def _load_ahakey_override_uncached('):source.index('\ndef get_ahakey_approval_override(')]
+for state in ['auto','swapped-auto','custom','unknown','disconnected']:
+    child=subprocess.Popen([args.java,'-cp','target/test-classes;target/classes;target/lib/*','com.example.ahakey.service.KimiBridgeLifecycleCheck','consumer-probe',state],stdin=subprocess.PIPE,stdout=subprocess.PIPE,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,text=True,encoding='utf-8',errors='replace')
+    try:
+        port=None
+        for line in child.stdout:
+            if line.startswith('KIMI_PROBE_PORT='):
+                port=int(line.split('=')[1]);break
+        assert port is not None,'Java probe failed'
+        scope=dict(socket=socket,struct=struct,_AHAKEY_BRIDGE_HOST='127.0.0.1',_AHAKEY_BRIDGE_PORT=port)
+        exec(loader,scope)
+        result=scope['_load_ahakey_override_uncached']()
+        if state in ['auto','swapped-auto']:assert result and result['is_auto'] is True,(state,result)
+        else:assert result is None,(state,result)
+        print('PASS actual Kimi consumer:',state,repr(result))
+        child.communicate('\n',timeout=3)
+        assert child.returncode==0
+    finally:
+        if child.poll() is None:child.kill();child.wait(timeout=2)
+print('Consumer source SHA256:',hashlib.sha256(source.encode()).hexdigest())

--- start/scripts/wd007/test-managed-ipc.py
+++ current/scripts/wd007/test-managed-ipc.py

+"""Exercise only control-plane framing in our own compiled child; never enumerate/connect devices."""
+import argparse, hashlib, json, os, socket, struct, subprocess, time, uuid
+from pathlib import Path
+
+parser=argparse.ArgumentParser()
+parser.add_argument('--bridge',required=True)
+args=parser.parse_args()
+exe=Path(args.bridge).resolve()
+checks=0
+def check(value,name):
+    global checks
+    assert value,name
+    checks+=1
+def packet(peer,body):
+    raw=json.dumps(body).encode();peer.sendall(struct.pack('<BH',0x10,len(raw))+raw)
+def receive(peer):
+    def exact(n):
+        data=b''
+        while len(data)<n:
+            chunk=peer.recv(n-len(data))
+            if not chunk:raise EOFError()
+            data+=chunk
+        return data
+    kind,length=struct.unpack('<BH',exact(3));check(kind==0x90,'response envelope')
+    return json.loads(exact(length))
+def launch(token,port):
+    env=os.environ.copy();env['AHAKEY_BRIDGE_TOKEN']=token
+    return subprocess.Popen([str(exe),'--studio','--minimized','--port',str(port)],env=env,cwd=exe.parent,
+                            creationflags=subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW,stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,stderr=subprocess.PIPE)
+for scenario in ['normal','wrong-token','owner-closes','invalid-frame']:
+    token=str(uuid.uuid4())
+    with socket.socket() as reservation:
+        reservation.bind(('127.0.0.1',0));port=reservation.getsockname()[1]
+    child=launch(token,port)
+    try:
+        peer=None;end=time.monotonic()+4
+        while peer is None and time.monotonic()<end:
+            try:peer=socket.create_connection(('127.0.0.1',port),timeout=.15)
+            except OSError:time.sleep(.025)
+        check(peer is not None,'managed listener and window start without device enumeration')
+        with peer:
+            peer.settimeout(3)
+            packet(peer,dict(version=1,requestId='1',op='hello',ownerToken=token if scenario!='wrong-token' else 'wrong',versions=[1]))
+            if scenario=='wrong-token':
+                check(peer.recv(1)==b'','unauthorized owner rejected')
+            else:
+                reply=receive(peer);check(reply['ok'] and 'session-writes' in reply['capabilities'],'versioned hello')
+                instance=reply['bridgeInstanceId']
+                if scenario=='normal':
+                    check('visible-window' in reply['capabilities'],'compiled bridge advertises visible control surface')
+                    packet(peer,dict(version=1,requestId='show',op='showWindow',bridgeInstanceId=instance))
+                    check(receive(peer)['ok'],'show existing authenticated bridge window')
+                    packet(peer,dict(version=1,requestId='status',op='studioStatus',bridgeInstanceId=instance,sequence=1,selectionAuthority='1:1:',message='离线 IPC 测试，无设备访问'))
+                    check(receive(peer)['ok'],'coordinator status delivered to window')
+                    packet(peer,dict(version=1,requestId='2',op='write',bridgeInstanceId=instance,sessionId='absent',targetId='fake',clientGeneration='1',channel='command',bytes='qrsAzN0='))
+                    check(receive(peer)['code']=='SESSION_CHANGED','write without selected target rejected before GATT')
+                    packet(peer,dict(version=1,requestId='2',op='write',bridgeInstanceId=instance))
+                    check(receive(peer)['code']=='DUPLICATE_REQUEST','duplicate never replays')
+                    packet(peer,dict(version=1,requestId='3',op='write',bridgeInstanceId='old'))
+                    check(receive(peer)['code']=='INSTANCE_CHANGED','old instance fenced')
+                    packet(peer,dict(version=1,requestId='4',op='shutdown',bridgeInstanceId=instance))
+                    check(receive(peer)['revoked'],'shutdown revokes even without a device')
+                elif scenario=='invalid-frame':
+                    peer.sendall(bytes([0x10,0xff,0xff]));check(peer.recv(1)==b'','oversized frame rejected')
+        check(child.wait(timeout=3)==0,'owned process terminates after owner closes or shutdown')
+    finally:
+        if child.poll() is None:child.kill();child.wait(timeout=2)
+print(json.dumps(dict(status='PASS',checks=checks,bridge=str(exe),sha256=hashlib.sha256(exe.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),scope='private IPC only; no enumeration or GATT'),ensure_ascii=False))

+    public static void Run() {

+        string foreground=selection.Publish(sameNames,false,false);
+        selection.Publish(new Dictionary<string,string>{{"id-C","AhaKey"}},true,false);
+        if(!selection.Contains(foreground,"id-A")||!selection.Contains(foreground,"id-B")||selection.Contains(foreground,"id-C"))throw new Exception("late background overwrote user choice");
+        selection.Publish(new Dictionary<string,string>(),true,false);

+        selection.Publish(new Dictionary<string,string>(),false,true);
+        if(!selection.Contains(foreground,"id-A"))throw new Exception("active session changed selection");
+        string fresh=selection.Publish(new Dictionary<string,string>{{"id-C","AhaKey"}},false,false);
+        if(selection.Contains(foreground,"id-A")||selection.Contains(fresh,"id-A")||!selection.Contains(fresh,"id-C"))throw new Exception("fresh foreground did not replace old candidates");
+    }
+}

     public static String getName(int code) {
+        if((code & 0xFF00)!=0) {
+            var parts=new java.util.ArrayList<String>();
+            int[] bits={0x200,0x100,0x400,0x800,0x2000,0x1000,0x4000,0x8000};
+            String[] names={"Ctrl","Shift","Alt","Win","RCtrl","RShift","RAlt","RWin"};
+            for(int i=0;i<bits.length;i++)if((code&bits[i])!=0)parts.add(names[i]);
+            if((code&255)!=0)parts.add(getName(code&255));return String.join("+",parts);
+        }
         return codeToName.getOrDefault(code, String.format("0x%02X", code));
     }

+            }
+            return new AhaKeyResponseParser.SideSwitchPositionConfig(AhaKeyProtocol.SIDE_SWITCH_BINDING_DISABLED, new byte[0]);
+        }
+        return currentOther;

+public record DeviceTarget(String transport, String id, String name) {

+    public synchronized void simulateAction(com.example.ahakey.model.KeyConfig key) {
+        if (simulationThread != null && simulationThread.isAlive()) {
+            lastSimulateHint.set("正在模拟，请等待本次动作结束。"); return;
+        }
+        final com.example.ahakey.service.KeySimulationService.Action action;
+        try { action = com.example.ahakey.service.KeySimulationService.snapshot(key); }
+        catch (IllegalArgumentException e) { lastSimulateHint.set(e.getMessage()); return; }
+        simulationThread = new Thread(() -> {
+            String result;
+            try {
+                com.example.ahakey.service.KeySimulationService.execute(action, (hid, down) -> {
+                    int vk = hidBaseToVk(hid);
+                    if (vk < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("不支持的模拟键: " + hid);
+                    WinUser.INPUT input = new WinUser.INPUT();
+                    fillKey(input, vk, !down);
+                    int count = User32.INSTANCE.SendInput(new WinUser.DWORD(1), new WinUser.INPUT[]{input}, input.size()).intValue();
+                    if (count != 1) throw new IllegalStateException("系统未接受按键输入");
+                }, Thread::sleep);
+                result = "模拟输入已发送；目标应用是否执行请实际确认。";
+            } catch (Exception e) { result = "模拟未完成：" + e.getMessage(); }
+            String message = result;
+            Platform.runLater(() -> lastSimulateHint.set(message));
+        }, "key-simulation");
+        simulationThread.setDaemon(true); simulationThread.start();
     }
 
     private static int hidBaseToVk(int hid) {
+        if (hid >= 0xE0 && hid <= 0xE7) return new int[]{0xA2,0xA0,0xA4,0x5B,0xA3,0xA1,0xA5,0x5C}[hid - 0xE0];
         // 字母 A(0x04)–Z(0x1D) → VK 0x41–0x5A
         if (hid >= 0x04 && hid <= 0x1D) return 0x41 + (hid - 0x04);
         // 数字 1(0x1E)–9(0x26) → VK 0x31–0x39; 0(0x27) → 0x30

     public static boolean isDisplayCapabilityResponse(byte[] frame) {

### src/main/java/com/example/ahakey/protocol/FirmwareCapabilities.java

+package com.example.ahakey.protocol;
+
+import java.util.Set;
+
+/** Fixed offline protocol profiles, separate from the firmware installation trust catalog. */
+public record FirmwareCapabilities(boolean extended, boolean legacyWrites) {
+    private static final Set<String> FULL = Set.of(
+            "x1-c582-hw1-p1-1.0.0-r001", "x1-c582-hw1-p1-1.0.1-r001", "x1-c582-hw2-p1-1.0.1-r001");
+    public static final FirmwareCapabilities UNKNOWN = new FirmwareCapabilities(false, false);
+    public static final FirmwareCapabilities LEGACY = new FirmwareCapabilities(false, true);
+    public static FirmwareCapabilities catalog(String key) {
+        return FULL.contains(key == null ? "" : key) ? new FirmwareCapabilities(true, false) : UNKNOWN;
+    }
+    public static boolean legacyIdentityAck(byte[] frame) {
+        return AhaKeyProtocol.isValidFrame(frame) && frame.length == 6 && frame[2] == (byte) 0x9F && frame[3] == 0;
+    }
+    public boolean admits(String capability) {
+        return extended && Set.of("keys", "side", "lights", "display", "k1").contains(capability);
+    }
+}

+    public void startCursorMonitoring() {
+        synchronized (cursorLifecycleLock) {
+            if (cursorClosed || cursorMonitor != null) return;
+            cursorMonitor = java.util.concurrent.Executors.newSingleThreadScheduledExecutor(r -> {
+                Thread thread = new Thread(r, "cursor-waiting-observer"); thread.setDaemon(true); return thread;
+            });
+            cursorMonitor.scheduleWithFixedDelay(() -> {
+                try { pollCursorWaiting(); }
+                catch (Exception e) { logger.debug("Cursor waiting observation unavailable"); }
+            }, 0, 500, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
+        }
+    }
+
+    public void close() {
+        synchronized (cursorLifecycleLock) {
+            cursorClosed = true;
+            cursorOverlay = new CursorOverlay(-1, Map.of());
+            if (cursorMonitor != null) { cursorMonitor.shutdownNow(); cursorMonitor = null; }
+            if (snapshotRetry != null) { snapshotRetry.shutdownNow(); snapshotRetry = null; }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void invalidateCursorContext() {
+        cursorEpoch++; cursorTracking.clear(); cursorProcess = ""; cursorConnection = -1; cursorMaintenance = -1;
+        cursorOverlay = new CursorOverlay(-1, Map.of());
+    }
+
+    private Map<String, Long> cursorTargets() {
+        Map<String, Long> result = new HashMap<>();
+        java.util.Set<String> duplicates = new java.util.HashSet<>();
+        for (AiRuntimeSlotBinding slot : appliedSlots) {
+            if (slot != null && slot.isBound() && "Cursor".equals(slot.getProvider())) {
+                String id = slot.getStableId();
+                if (result.put(id, cursorTracking.computeIfAbsent(id, key -> new CursorTracking()).hookRevision) != null)
+                    duplicates.add(id);
+            }
+        }
+        duplicates.forEach(result::remove);
+        cursorTracking.keySet().retainAll(result.keySet());
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    // Package-visible deterministic seam: the same method is used by the production scheduler.
+    void pollCursorWaiting() {
+        try { observeCursorWaiting(); }
+        finally {
+            if (snapshotPending) {
+                try { sendAppliedSnapshot(snapshotConnection); }
+                catch (Exception e) { logger.debug("Cursor waiting snapshot remains pending"); }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void observeCursorWaiting() {
+        synchronized (snapshotSendLock) { if (cursorClosed || displayMode.get() != AiRuntimeDisplayMode.MULTI) return; }
+        var process = cursorDiscovery.runningCursor();
+        String processIdentity = process.generation() + ":" + process.version();
+        CursorRead read;
+        synchronized (snapshotSendLock) {
+            if (cursorClosed || displayMode.get() != AiRuntimeDisplayMode.MULTI) return;
+            if (!bleManager.maintenance().normalAllowed() || !isDeviceSupported()) { invalidateCursorContext(); return; }
+            long connection = bleManager.connectionGeneration();
+            long maintenance = bleManager.maintenance().epoch();
+            if (cursorConnection != connection || cursorMaintenance != maintenance) {
+                invalidateCursorContext(); cursorConnection = connection; cursorMaintenance = maintenance;
+            }
+            if (cursorClearPendingConnection == connection) { cursorClearPendingConnection = -1; sendCursorSnapshot(); }
+            else cursorClearPendingConnection = -1;
+            if (!process.supported() || !processIdentity.equals(cursorProcess)) {
+                boolean changed = false;
+                for (CursorTracking tracking : cursorTracking.values()) {
+                    if (tracking.confirmed) { tracking.unknown = true; changed = true; }
+                    if (!tracking.generation.isEmpty()) tracking.retiredGenerations.add(tracking.generation);
+                    tracking.generation = ""; tracking.pendingHooks.clear(); tracking.terminal = false;
+                    tracking.waiting = false; tracking.confirmed = false; tracking.anchored = false; tracking.lastValue = null; tracking.hookRevision++;
+                }
+                cursorEpoch++; cursorProcess = process.supported() ? processIdentity : "";
+                if (changed) sendCursorSnapshot();
+            }
+            if (!process.supported()) return;
+            Map<String, Long> hooks = cursorTargets();
+            if (hooks.isEmpty()) return;
+            read = new CursorRead(cursorEpoch, connection, maintenance, ++cursorSample, cursorProcess, cursorDataDir, Map.copyOf(hooks));
+        }
+        // No FX thread, snapshot lock or device transaction is held during the bounded SQLite read.
+        var observations = cursorDiscovery.readRuntimeStates(Path.of(read.directory()), read.hooks().keySet(), process.version());
+        var after = cursorDiscovery.runningCursor();
+        synchronized (snapshotSendLock) {
+            if (cursorClosed || read.epoch() != cursorEpoch || read.connection() != bleManager.connectionGeneration()
+                    || read.maintenance() != bleManager.maintenance().epoch()
+                    || displayMode.get() != AiRuntimeDisplayMode.MULTI || !bleManager.maintenance().normalAllowed()
+                    || !Objects.equals(read.directory(), cursorDataDir) || !after.supported()
+                    || !read.process().equals(after.generation() + ":" + after.version())) return;
+            boolean changed = false;
+            for (var entry : read.hooks().entrySet()) {
+                CursorTracking tracking = cursorTracking.get(entry.getKey());
+                if (tracking == null || tracking.hookRevision != entry.getValue() || read.sequence() <= tracking.acceptedSample) continue;
+                tracking.acceptedSample = read.sequence(); tracking.nativeIdentityContext = true;
+                boolean wasWaiting = tracking.waiting, wasUnknown = tracking.unknown;
+                var state = observations.get(entry.getKey());
+                if (state == null || state.state() == CursorSessionDiscoveryService.Waiting.UNKNOWN
+                        || state.generation().isEmpty() || tracking.retiredGenerations.contains(state.generation())) {
+                    // Unknown cannot manufacture a green/done recovery from a previous yellow.
+                    tracking.unknown |= tracking.confirmed;
+                    tracking.waiting = false;
+                    if (!tracking.confirmed) tracking.lastValue = null;
+                } else {
+                    if (!state.generation().equals(tracking.generation)) {
+                        boolean previousFalse = Boolean.FALSE.equals(tracking.lastValue) && !tracking.unknown;
+                        boolean candidateFalse = tracking.pendingStartedFromFalse;
+                        boolean hadGeneration = !tracking.generation.isEmpty();
+                        if (hadGeneration) tracking.retiredGenerations.add(tracking.generation);
+                        tracking.generation = state.generation(); tracking.terminal = false;
+                        tracking.waiting = false; tracking.confirmed = false; tracking.unknown = false;
+                        tracking.anchored = false; tracking.lastValue = null;
+                        boolean removedFact = liveFacts.remove("Cursor:" + entry.getKey()) != null;
+                        IDEState pending = tracking.pendingHooks.get(tracking.generation);
+                        tracking.pendingHooks.clear(); tracking.pendingStartedFromFalse = false;
+                        tracking.generationStartedFromFalse = pending != null ? candidateFalse : previousFalse;
+                        if (pending != null) {
+                            if (tracking.generationStartedFromFalse) tracking.lastValue = false;
+                            acceptCursorHook(tracking, pending);
+                            liveFacts.put("Cursor:" + entry.getKey(), new RuntimeFact(pending, System.nanoTime(), read.connection()));
+                        }
+                        changed |= hadGeneration || removedFact || pending != null;
+                    }
+                    if (!tracking.pendingHooks.isEmpty()) {
+                        // A different turn has arrived before its composerData save. The header
+                        // may already describe that turn: do not attach it to the older identity.
+                        tracking.unknown = true; tracking.waiting = false; tracking.confirmed = false;
+                        tracking.anchored = false; tracking.lastValue = null;
+                        changed |= !wasUnknown || wasWaiting;
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    boolean waiting = state.state() == CursorSessionDiscoveryService.Waiting.WAITING;
+                    if (!waiting) { tracking.waiting = false; tracking.confirmed = false; tracking.unknown = false; }
+                    else if (tracking.confirmed || (Boolean.FALSE.equals(tracking.lastValue) && tracking.anchored)) {
+                        tracking.waiting = true; tracking.confirmed = true; tracking.unknown = false;
+                    }
+                    // Historical first true never proves activity. An established native wait has no guessed time limit.
+                    tracking.lastValue = waiting;
+                }
+                changed |= wasWaiting != tracking.waiting || wasUnknown != tracking.unknown;
+            }
+            if (changed) sendCursorSnapshot();
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void sendCursorSnapshot() {
+        publishCursorOverlay();
+        snapshotRevision.incrementAndGet();
+        snapshotPending = true;
+        snapshotConnection = bleManager.connectionGeneration();
+    }
+
+    private void publishCursorOverlay() {
+        Map<String, String> states = new HashMap<>();
+        cursorTracking.forEach((id, tracking) -> {
+            if (tracking.unknown) states.put(id, "");
+            else if (tracking.waiting) states.put(id, IDEState.NOTIFICATION.name());
+        });
+        cursorOverlay = new CursorOverlay(cursorConnection, Map.copyOf(states));
+    }
+
+    private Delivery cursorHook(String id, IDEState state, JsonNode payload) {
+        CursorTracking tracking = cursorTracking.computeIfAbsent(id, key -> new CursorTracking());
+        JsonNode identity = payload == null ? null : payload.get("generation_id");
+        if (identity == null && payload != null) identity = payload.path("hookInput").get("generation_id");
+        String generation = identity != null && identity.isTextual() ? identity.textValue() : "";
+        if ((identity == null || identity.isNull()) && !tracking.nativeIdentityContext
+                && tracking.generation.isEmpty() && tracking.pendingHooks.isEmpty()
+                && !tracking.confirmed && !tracking.unknown && !tracking.waiting) {
+            // Preserve ordinary legacy Hook behavior only before this context enters native attribution.
+            // This path deliberately never anchors a native wait.
+            RuntimeFact legacy = liveFacts.get("Cursor:" + id);
+            if (!isTerminal(state) || (legacy != null && legacy.fresh(bleManager.connectionGeneration())
+                    && !isTerminal(legacy.state()))) return Delivery.SENT;
+            return Delivery.IGNORED;
+        }
+        if (!generation.isBlank()) tracking.nativeIdentityContext = true;
+        if (generation.isBlank() || generation.length() > 128 || cursorProcess.isBlank()
+                || tracking.retiredGenerations.contains(generation)) return Delivery.IGNORED;
+        if (!generation.equals(tracking.generation)) {
+            // UUIDs have no ordering. An unfamiliar Hook cannot replace the native current run.
+            // Retain only bounded candidates until the exact native identity independently matches.
+            if (isTerminal(state) && !tracking.pendingHooks.containsKey(generation)) return Delivery.IGNORED;
+            tracking.hookRevision++;
+            if (tracking.pendingHooks.isEmpty())
+                tracking.pendingStartedFromFalse = Boolean.FALSE.equals(tracking.lastValue) && !tracking.unknown;
+            tracking.pendingHooks.put(generation, state);
+            while (tracking.pendingHooks.size() > 8) tracking.pendingHooks.remove(tracking.pendingHooks.keySet().iterator().next());
+            return Delivery.PENDING;
+        }
+        if (tracking.terminal) return Delivery.IGNORED;
+        if (!tracking.pendingHooks.isEmpty()) return Delivery.PENDING;
+        tracking.hookRevision++;
+        acceptCursorHook(tracking, state);
+        publishCursorOverlay();
+        return Delivery.SENT;
+    }
+
+    private boolean isTerminal(IDEState state) {
+        return state == IDEState.STOP || state == IDEState.SESSION_END || state == IDEState.TASK_COMPLETED;
+    }
+
+    private void acceptCursorHook(CursorTracking tracking, IDEState state) {
+        if (isTerminal(state)) {
+            tracking.terminal = true;
+            tracking.anchored = false; tracking.confirmed = false; tracking.waiting = false;
+            tracking.unknown = false; tracking.lastValue = null;
+        } else {
+            tracking.anchored = !cursorProcess.isBlank() && cursorConnection == bleManager.connectionGeneration();
+            if (tracking.anchored && tracking.generationStartedFromFalse && Boolean.TRUE.equals(tracking.lastValue)) {
+                tracking.waiting = true; tracking.confirmed = true; tracking.unknown = false;
+            }
+            tracking.generationStartedFromFalse = false;
+            if (tracking.unknown && !tracking.confirmed) { tracking.unknown = false; tracking.lastValue = null; }
+        }
+    }

+        }
+        boolean sent = false;
+        boolean ownsSender = true;
+        try {
+            while (true) {
+                // Both this check and the send below can wait for the device command lock.
+                // Concurrent Hooks only update intent and return PENDING while this sender owns I/O.
+                if (bleManager.isDisplayBusy() || bleManager.isDisplayWriteUncertain()) return sent;

+                            || connection != bleManager.connectionGeneration()
+                            || displayMode.get() != AiRuntimeDisplayMode.MULTI
+                            || !bleManager.maintenance().normalAllowed()) return sent;

+                    for (AiRuntimeSlotBinding slot : snapshot) applyLiveFact(slot);

+                                && epoch == cursorEpoch && revision == snapshotRevision.get()
+                                && maintenance == bleManager.maintenance().epoch()
+                                && bleManager.maintenance().normalAllowed());
+                sent |= delivered;

+                    if (delivered && epoch == cursorEpoch && revision == snapshotRevision.get()) {

+                        // Release ownership atomically with observing no new intent: no lost wake-up.
+                        snapshotSending = false; ownsSender = false;
+                        return true;
+                    }
+                    // An in-flight frame cannot be recalled. Rebuild from current bindings and facts
+                    // after ACK, never replay the captured snapshot across an invalidation boundary.
+                    if (!snapshotPending || snapshotConnection != connection) return sent;
+                }
+            }
+        } finally {
+            if (ownsSender) synchronized (snapshotSendLock) { snapshotSending = false; }
+            scheduleSnapshotRetry();
+        }
+    }

+    private void scheduleSnapshotRetry() {
+        // A device-idle callback may race the sender's release. A bounded retry avoids losing
+        // the final intent, including on connection changes while the previous ACK is pending.
+        synchronized (cursorLifecycleLock) {
+            if (cursorClosed || !snapshotPending || snapshotRetryQueued
+                    || displayMode.get() != AiRuntimeDisplayMode.MULTI
+                    || snapshotConnection != bleManager.connectionGeneration()
+                    || !bleManager.maintenance().normalAllowed()) return;
+            if (snapshotRetry == null) snapshotRetry = java.util.concurrent.Executors.newSingleThreadScheduledExecutor(r -> {
+                Thread thread = new Thread(r, "runtime-snapshot-retry"); thread.setDaemon(true); return thread;
+            });
+            snapshotRetryQueued = true;
+            snapshotRetry.schedule(() -> {
+                synchronized (cursorLifecycleLock) { snapshotRetryQueued = false; }
+                if (!cursorClosed && snapshotPending) {
+                    try { sendAppliedSnapshot(snapshotConnection); }
+                    catch (Exception e) { logger.debug("Runtime snapshot remains pending"); }
+                }
+            }, 250, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
         }

+    private void managedEvent(com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode event) {

+                String requestedSession=event.path("sessionId").asText();

+                        && Long.toString(connectionGeneration).equals(event.path("clientGeneration").asText())

+    private int wireExpected=-1;

+    private boolean hasUnconfirmedWrite() {
+        return (displayLease!=null && displayLease.writing) || k1RouteWriteAttempted
+                || (wireExpected>=0 && wireMayMutate);
+    }
+    // Unknown commands and raw data remain writes. Reads inside unfinished write
+    // transactions must still preserve those transactions' recovery barriers.
+    private static boolean readOnlyRequest(byte[] frame, boolean data) {
+        if(data || frame==null || frame.length<5)return false;
+        return switch(frame[2]&255) {
+            case 0x00,0x95,0x97,0x9F -> frame.length==5;
+            case 0x83 -> frame.length==6;
+            case 0x87 -> frame.length==7;
+            case 0x88 -> frame.length>=6 && frame.length<=8;
+            case 0x9E -> frame[3]==1 && ((frame[4]==0 && frame.length==11)
+                    || (frame[4]==0x10 && frame.length==14));
+            default -> false;
+        };
+    }

+    }

+package com.example.ahakey.service;
+
+import com.example.ahakey.model.AiRuntimeSession;
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
+import org.slf4j.Logger;
+import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
+
+import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
+import java.nio.file.Files;
+import java.nio.file.Path;
+import java.sql.Connection;
+import java.sql.DriverManager;
+import java.sql.ResultSet;
+import java.sql.ResultSetMetaData;
+import java.sql.Statement;
+import java.time.Instant;
+import java.util.ArrayList;
+import java.util.Comparator;
+import java.util.HashMap;
