+public class CursorSessionDiscoveryService {
+    private static final Logger logger = LoggerFactory.getLogger(CursorSessionDiscoveryService.class);
+    private static final int MAX_SCAN_FILES = 80;
+    // Installed source verified: 3.27.22 additionally propagates child user waits to the exact parent header.
+    private static final Set<String> WAITING_SCHEMA_VERSIONS = Set.of("3.14.7", "3.27.22");
+    private final ObjectMapper mapper = new ObjectMapper();
+
+    public enum Waiting { WAITING, NOT_WAITING, UNKNOWN }
+    public record WaitingState(Waiting state, String reason, String generation) {
+        public WaitingState(Waiting state, String reason) { this(state, reason, ""); }
+    }
+    public record CursorProcess(String generation, String version) {
+        public boolean supported() { return !generation.isBlank() && WAITING_SCHEMA_VERSIONS.contains(version); }
+    }
+
+    /** Windows ProcessHandle.info().command() can be absent even for same-user processes.
+     * Toolhelp + QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION reads only executable identity and creation time.
+     */
+    public CursorProcess runningCursor() {
+        if (!System.getProperty("os.name", "").startsWith("Windows")) return new CursorProcess("", "");
+        try {
+            var kernel = com.sun.jna.platform.win32.Kernel32.INSTANCE;
+            var snapshot = kernel.CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(com.sun.jna.platform.win32.Tlhelp32.TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS,
+                new com.sun.jna.platform.win32.WinDef.DWORD(0));
+            if (snapshot == null || com.sun.jna.platform.win32.WinBase.INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE.equals(snapshot))
+                return new CursorProcess("", "");
+            Map<Integer, Integer> cursorParents = new HashMap<>();
+            try {
+                var entry = new com.sun.jna.platform.win32.Tlhelp32.PROCESSENTRY32();
+                boolean available = kernel.Process32First(snapshot, entry);
+                int count = 0;
+                while (available) {
+                    if (++count > 65536) return new CursorProcess("", "");
+                    if ("Cursor.exe".equalsIgnoreCase(com.sun.jna.Native.toString(entry.szExeFile)))
+                        cursorParents.put(entry.th32ProcessID.intValue(), entry.th32ParentProcessID.intValue());
+                    available = kernel.Process32Next(snapshot, entry);
+                }
+            } finally { kernel.CloseHandle(snapshot); }
+            List<Integer> roots = cursorParents.keySet().stream()
+                .filter(pid -> !cursorParents.containsKey(cursorParents.get(pid))).toList();
+            if (roots.size() != 1) return new CursorProcess("", "");
+            int pid = roots.get(0);
+            var process = kernel.OpenProcess(0x1000, false, pid); // PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION
+            if (process == null) return new CursorProcess("", "");
+            try {
+                char[] path = new char[32768];
+                var length = new com.sun.jna.ptr.IntByReference(path.length);
+                var created = new com.sun.jna.platform.win32.WinBase.FILETIME();
+                if (!kernel.QueryFullProcessImageName(process, 0, path, length)
+                        || !kernel.GetProcessTimes(process, created, new com.sun.jna.platform.win32.WinBase.FILETIME(),
+                            new com.sun.jna.platform.win32.WinBase.FILETIME(), new com.sun.jna.platform.win32.WinBase.FILETIME()))
+                    return new CursorProcess("", "");
+                Path executable = Path.of(new String(path, 0, length.getValue())).toRealPath();
+                if (!"Cursor.exe".equalsIgnoreCase(executable.getFileName().toString())) return new CursorProcess("", "");
+                JsonNode identity = mapper.readTree(executable.getParent().resolve("resources/app/package.json").toFile());
+                if (!"Cursor".equals(text(identity, "name"))) return new CursorProcess("", "");
+                String started = Integer.toUnsignedString(created.dwHighDateTime) + ":" + Integer.toUnsignedString(created.dwLowDateTime);
+                return new CursorProcess(pid + ":" + started + ":" + executable, text(identity, "version"));
+            } finally { kernel.CloseHandle(process); }
+        } catch (Exception | LinkageError e) { return new CursorProcess("", ""); }
+    }
+
+    /** Raw observation, not proof of a live wait. The runtime service supplies process/Hook/epoch guards.
+     * Only the canonical global header table is supported; old ItemTable discovery is not a live source.
+     */
+    public Map<String, WaitingState> readWaitingStates(Path dataDir, Set<String> composerIds, String cursorVersion) {
+        return readWaitingStates(dataDir, composerIds, cursorVersion, false);
+    }
+
+    public Map<String, WaitingState> readRuntimeStates(Path dataDir, Set<String> composerIds, String cursorVersion) {
+        return readWaitingStates(dataDir, composerIds, cursorVersion, true);
+    }
+
+    private Map<String, WaitingState> readWaitingStates(Path dataDir, Set<String> composerIds, String cursorVersion,
+                                                       boolean requireGeneration) {
+        Map<String, WaitingState> result = new HashMap<>();
+        for (String id : composerIds) result.put(id, new WaitingState(Waiting.UNKNOWN, "unavailable"));
+        if (!WAITING_SCHEMA_VERSIONS.contains(cursorVersion)
+                || (requireGeneration && !"3.27.22".equals(cursorVersion))) {
+            result.replaceAll((id, value) -> new WaitingState(Waiting.UNKNOWN, "unsupported-version"));
+            return Map.copyOf(result);
+        }
+        if (dataDir == null || composerIds.isEmpty() || composerIds.size() > 4) return Map.copyOf(result);
+        Path db = dataDir.resolve("globalStorage/state.vscdb");
+        try (Connection connection = openReadOnly(db)) {
+            // Bound VM work as well as lock waits. SQLite busy_timeout alone is not a query deadline.
+            long deadline = System.nanoTime() + java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS.toNanos(250);
+            org.sqlite.ProgressHandler.setHandler(connection, 1000, new org.sqlite.ProgressHandler() {
+                @Override protected int progress() {
+                    return Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted() || System.nanoTime() >= deadline ? 1 : 0;
+                }
+            });
+            // A read transaction is consistent, but independently saved header/identity rows may lag.
+            connection.setAutoCommit(false);
+            try (var statement = connection.prepareStatement(
+                    "select composerId,isArchived,isSubagent,value from composerHeaders where composerId=? limit 2")) {
+                statement.setQueryTimeout(1);
+                for (String id : composerIds) {
+                    if (id == null || id.isBlank() || id.length() > 128) continue;
+                    statement.setString(1, id);
+                    try (ResultSet rs = statement.executeQuery()) {
+                        if (!rs.next()) { result.put(id, new WaitingState(Waiting.UNKNOWN, "missing-header")); continue; }
+                        Object archived = rs.getObject("isArchived"), subagent = rs.getObject("isSubagent");
+                        String value = rs.getString("value");
+                        WaitingState state = new WaitingState(Waiting.UNKNOWN, "invalid-header");
+                        if (Integer.valueOf(0).equals(archived) && Integer.valueOf(0).equals(subagent)
+                                && value != null && value.length() <= 262144) {
+                            try {
+                            JsonNode header = mapper.reader().with(com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.DeserializationFeature.FAIL_ON_TRAILING_TOKENS)
+                                .with(com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonParser.Feature.STRICT_DUPLICATE_DETECTION).readTree(value);
+                            JsonNode pending = header == null ? null : header.get("hasBlockingPendingActions");
+                            if (header != null && header.isObject() && header.path("composerId").isTextual()
+                                    && id.equals(header.path("composerId").textValue())
+                                    && absentOrFalse(header, "isArchived") && absentOrFalse(header, "archived")
+                                    && absentOrFalse(header, "isSubAgent") && absentOrFalse(header, "isSubagent")
+                                    && !header.hasNonNull("subagentInfo")
+                                    && pending != null && pending.isBoolean()) {
+                                state = new WaitingState(pending.booleanValue() ? Waiting.WAITING : Waiting.NOT_WAITING, "header-boolean");
+                            }
+                            } catch (com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonProcessingException ignored) {
+                                state = new WaitingState(Waiting.UNKNOWN, "invalid-json");
+                            }
+                        }
+                        if (rs.next()) state = new WaitingState(Waiting.UNKNOWN, "duplicate-header");
+                        result.put(id, state);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            if (requireGeneration) {
+                for (String id : composerIds) {
+                    WaitingState waiting = result.get(id);
+                    if (waiting.state() == Waiting.UNKNOWN) continue;
+                    try {
+                        String generation = readGeneration(connection, id);
+                        result.put(id, generation.isEmpty() ? new WaitingState(Waiting.UNKNOWN, "unconfirmed-generation")
+                                : new WaitingState(waiting.state(), waiting.reason(), generation));
+                    } catch (java.sql.SQLException invalidIdentity) {
+                        result.put(id, new WaitingState(Waiting.UNKNOWN, "identity-read-failed"));
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            connection.rollback();
+        } catch (Exception e) {
+            // Do not expose paths, headers or SQLite errors containing user data in logs.
+            result.replaceAll((id, value) -> new WaitingState(Waiting.UNKNOWN, "read-failed"));
+        }
+        return Map.copyOf(result);
+    }
+
+    private String readGeneration(Connection connection, String id) throws Exception {
+        // Project only identity scalars from the exact bound key. Never return the composer value,
+        // conversation, prompt, tool data or credentials to Java. Duplicate selected keys are invalid.
+        String sql = "select j.key,j.type,j.atom from cursorDiskKV d, "
+                + "json_each(case when length(cast(d.value as blob))<=1048576 then d.value else '{}' end) j "
+                + "where d.key=? and j.key in ('composerId','chatGenerationUUID','latestChatGenerationUUID') limit 7";
+        Map<String, String> fields = new HashMap<>();
+        try (var statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {
+            statement.setQueryTimeout(1); statement.setString(1, "composerData:" + id);
+            try (ResultSet rs = statement.executeQuery()) {
+                while (rs.next()) {
+                    String key = rs.getString(1), type = rs.getString(2), value = rs.getString(3);
+                    // chatGenerationUUID is removed at completion; latest persists for valid stop attribution.
+                    if ("chatGenerationUUID".equals(key) && "null".equals(type)) value = "";
+                    else if (!"text".equals(type) || value == null || value.isBlank() || value.length() > 128) return "";
+                    if (fields.put(key, value) != null) return "";
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        String latest = fields.getOrDefault("latestChatGenerationUUID", "");
+        String active = fields.getOrDefault("chatGenerationUUID", "");
+        return id.equals(fields.get("composerId")) && !latest.isEmpty()
+                && (active.isEmpty() || active.equals(latest)) ? latest : "";
+    }
+
+    private boolean absentOrFalse(JsonNode header, String field) {
+        // The canonical table's required flags remain authoritative when an optional JSON flag is absent.
+        JsonNode value = header.get(field);
+        return value == null || (value.isBoolean() && !value.booleanValue());
+    }
+
+    private Connection openReadOnly(Path db) throws Exception {
+        if (!Files.isRegularFile(db)) throw new java.io.IOException("Missing Cursor database");
+        Class.forName("org.sqlite.JDBC");
+        var config = new org.sqlite.SQLiteConfig();
+        config.setReadOnly(true);
+        config.setBusyTimeout(250);
+        // mode=ro honors WAL; immutable=1 would silently ignore live WAL updates.
+        return DriverManager.getConnection("jdbc:sqlite:" + db.toAbsolutePath().toUri() + "?mode=ro", config.toProperties());
+    }
+
+    public List<AiRuntimeSession> discover(Path dataDir) {
+        if (dataDir == null || !Files.isDirectory(dataDir)) {
+            return List.of();
+        }
+        Map<String, TitleRecord> titles = readConversationTitles(dataDir);
+        Map<String, WorkspaceRecord> workspaces = readWorkspaceRecords(dataDir.resolve("workspaceStorage"));
+        ComposerTableResult tableResult = readComposerHeadersTable(dataDir, titles, workspaces);
+        List<AiRuntimeSession> sessions = tableResult.tableFound()
+            ? tableResult.sessions()
+            : readComposerHeaders(dataDir, titles, workspaces);
+        sessions.sort(Comparator.comparing(
+            AiRuntimeSession::updatedAt,
+            Comparator.nullsLast(Comparator.reverseOrder())
+        ));
+        return sessions;
+    }
+
+    private Map<String, TitleRecord> readConversationTitles(Path dataDir) {
+        Map<String, TitleRecord> titles = new HashMap<>();
+        for (Path db : findFiles(dataDir, "conversation-search.db", 6)) {
+            try {
+                Class.forName("org.sqlite.JDBC");
+                try (Connection connection = openReadOnly(db);
+                     Statement statement = connection.createStatement();
+                     ResultSet rs = statement.executeQuery("select * from conversations limit 1000")) {
+                    statement.setQueryTimeout(2);
+                    ResultSetMetaData meta = rs.getMetaData();
+                    Set<String> columns = columns(meta);
+                    while (rs.next()) {
+                        String id = firstColumn(rs, columns, "id", "conversation_id", "conversationId", "composerId", "composer_id");
+                        if (id.isBlank()) {
+                            continue;
+                        }
+                        String title = firstColumn(rs, columns, "title", "name", "summary");
+                        long updated = longColumn(rs, columns, "lastUpdatedAt", "updated_at", "updatedAt", "timestamp", "createdAt");
+                        titles.put(id, new TitleRecord(title, toInstant(updated)));
+                    }
+                }
+            } catch (Exception e) {
+                logger.info("Cursor conversation title discovery skipped for {}: {}", db, e.getMessage());
+            }
+        }
+        return titles;
+    }
+
+    private Map<String, WorkspaceRecord> readWorkspaceRecords(Path workspaceStorage) {
+        Map<String, WorkspaceRecord> records = new HashMap<>();
+        if (!Files.isDirectory(workspaceStorage)) {
+            return records;
+        }
+        try (Stream<Path> stream = Files.list(workspaceStorage)) {
+            stream.filter(Files::isDirectory).forEach(dir -> {
+                Path workspaceJson = dir.resolve("workspace.json");
+                if (!Files.isRegularFile(workspaceJson)) {
+                    return;
+                }
+                try {
+                    JsonNode node = mapper.readTree(Files.readString(workspaceJson, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
+                    String folder = firstNonBlank(
+                        text(node, "folder"),
+                        text(node, "workspace"),
+                        text(node, "workspaceFolder"),
+                        text(node, "path"),
+                        text(node.path("configuration"), "folder")
+                    );
+                    String project = AiRuntimeSession.projectFromCwd(folder);
+                    records.put(dir.getFileName().toString(), new WorkspaceRecord(project, folder));
+                } catch (Exception e) {
+                    logger.info("Cursor workspace mapping skipped for {}: {}", workspaceJson, e.getMessage());
+                }
+            });
+        } catch (Exception e) {
+            logger.info("Cursor workspaceStorage discovery skipped: {}", e.getMessage());
+        }
+        return records;
+    }
+
+    private ComposerTableResult readComposerHeadersTable(
+        Path dataDir,
+        Map<String, TitleRecord> titles,
+        Map<String, WorkspaceRecord> workspaces
+    ) {
+        Map<String, AiRuntimeSession> sessions = new HashMap<>();
+        boolean tableFound = false;
+        for (Path db : findFiles(dataDir, "state.vscdb", 5)) {
+            try {
+                Class.forName("org.sqlite.JDBC");
+                try (Connection connection = openReadOnly(db)) {
+                    if (!hasTable(connection, "composerHeaders")) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    tableFound = true;
+                    try (Statement statement = connection.createStatement()) {
+                        statement.setQueryTimeout(2);
+                        try (ResultSet rs = statement.executeQuery("select * from composerHeaders limit 2000")) {
+                            ResultSetMetaData meta = rs.getMetaData();
+                            Set<String> columns = columns(meta);
+                            while (rs.next()) {
+                                addComposerHeaderRow(rs, columns, titles, workspaces, sessions);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            } catch (Exception e) {
+                logger.info("Cursor composerHeaders table discovery skipped for {}: {}", db, e.getMessage());
+            }
+        }
+        return new ComposerTableResult(tableFound, new ArrayList<>(sessions.values()));
+    }
+
+    private boolean hasTable(Connection connection, String tableName) throws Exception {
+        try (Statement statement = connection.createStatement()) {
+            statement.setQueryTimeout(2);
+            try (ResultSet rs = statement.executeQuery(
+                "select name from sqlite_master where type='table' and name='" + tableName + "' limit 1"
+            )) {
+                return rs.next();
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void addComposerHeaderRow(
+        ResultSet rs,
+        Set<String> columns,
+        Map<String, TitleRecord> titles,
+        Map<String, WorkspaceRecord> workspaces,
+        Map<String, AiRuntimeSession> sessions
+    ) throws Exception {
+        String id = firstColumn(rs, columns, "composerId", "composer_id", "id");
+        if (id.isBlank()
+            || booleanColumn(rs, columns, "isArchived", "archived")
+            || booleanColumn(rs, columns, "isSubagent", "isSubAgent", "subagent")) {
+            return;
+        }
+        JsonNode value = parseJson(firstColumn(rs, columns, "value"));
+        String kind = firstNonBlank(
+            firstColumn(rs, columns, "type", "composerType"),
+            text(value, "type"),
+            text(value, "composerType")
+        );
+        if (kind.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).contains("subagent")) {
+            return;
+        }
+        String workspaceId = firstNonBlank(
+            firstColumn(rs, columns, "workspaceId", "workspace_id"),
+            text(value, "workspaceId"),
+            text(value, "workspace_id")
+        );
+        WorkspaceRecord workspace = workspaces.getOrDefault(workspaceId, new WorkspaceRecord("未归类", ""));
+        TitleRecord title = titles.getOrDefault(id, new TitleRecord("", Instant.EPOCH));
+        String displayTitle = firstNonBlank(
+            title.title(),
+            firstColumn(rs, columns, "title", "name", "summary"),
+            text(value, "title"),
+            text(value, "name"),
+            text(value, "summary")
+        );
+        Instant updatedAt = maxInstant(
+            title.updatedAt(),
+            toInstant(longColumn(rs, columns, "lastUpdatedAt", "updated_at", "updatedAt")),
+            toInstant(longColumn(rs, columns, "recency")),
+            toInstant(longColumn(rs, columns, "checkpointAt")),
+            toInstant(longColumn(rs, columns, "createdAt")),
+            toInstant(longValue(value, "lastUpdatedAt")),
+            toInstant(longValue(value, "updatedAt")),
+            toInstant(longValue(value, "createdAt"))
+        );
+        sessions.put(id, new AiRuntimeSession(
+            "Cursor",
+            id,
+            displayTitle,
+            workspace.projectName(),
+            workspace.cwd(),
+            "",
+            updatedAt
+        ));
+    }
+
+    private List<AiRuntimeSession> readComposerHeaders(
+        Path dataDir,
+        Map<String, TitleRecord> titles,
+        Map<String, WorkspaceRecord> workspaces
+    ) {
+        Map<String, AiRuntimeSession> sessions = new HashMap<>();
+        for (Path db : findFiles(dataDir, "state.vscdb", 5)) {
+            try {
+                Class.forName("org.sqlite.JDBC");
+                try (Connection connection = openReadOnly(db);
+                     Statement statement = connection.createStatement();
+                     ResultSet rs = statement.executeQuery("select key, value from ItemTable")) {
+                    statement.setQueryTimeout(2);
+                    int read = 0;
+                    while (rs.next() && read++ < 1200) {
+                        String key = rs.getString("key");
+                        if (key == null || !key.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).contains("composer")) {
+                            continue;
+                        }
+                        String value = rs.getString("value");
+                        if (value == null || !value.contains("composerHeaders")) {
+                            continue;
+                        }
+                        collectComposerHeaders(mapper.readTree(value), titles, workspaces, sessions);
+                    }
+                }
+            } catch (Exception e) {
+                logger.info("Cursor composer metadata skipped for {}: {}", db, e.getMessage());
+            }
+        }
+        return new ArrayList<>(sessions.values());
+    }
+
+    private void collectComposerHeaders(
+        JsonNode node,
+        Map<String, TitleRecord> titles,
+        Map<String, WorkspaceRecord> workspaces,
+        Map<String, AiRuntimeSession> sessions
+    ) {
+        if (node == null || node.isNull()) {
+            return;
+        }
+        JsonNode headers = node.get("composerHeaders");
+        if (headers != null && headers.isObject()) {
+            addHeader(headers, titles, workspaces, sessions);
+        } else if (headers != null && headers.isArray()) {
+            for (JsonNode header : headers) {
+                addHeader(header, titles, workspaces, sessions);
+            }
+        }
+        if (node.isObject()) {
+            node.fields().forEachRemaining(entry -> {
+                String key = entry.getKey().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
+                if (!"messages".equals(key) && !"conversation".equals(key) && !"chat".equals(key)) {
+                    collectComposerHeaders(entry.getValue(), titles, workspaces, sessions);
+                }
+            });
+        } else if (node.isArray()) {
+            for (JsonNode item : node) {
+                collectComposerHeaders(item, titles, workspaces, sessions);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void addHeader(
+        JsonNode header,
+        Map<String, TitleRecord> titles,
+        Map<String, WorkspaceRecord> workspaces,
+        Map<String, AiRuntimeSession> sessions
+    ) {
+        String id = firstNonBlank(text(header, "composerId"), text(header, "composer_id"));
+        if (id.isBlank() || booleanValue(header, "isArchived") || booleanValue(header, "archived") || booleanValue(header, "isSubAgent")) {
+            return;
+        }
+        String kind = firstNonBlank(text(header, "type"), text(header, "composerType"));
+        if (kind.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).contains("subagent")) {
+            return;
+        }
+        String workspaceId = firstNonBlank(text(header, "workspaceId"), text(header, "workspace_id"));
+        WorkspaceRecord workspace = workspaces.getOrDefault(workspaceId, new WorkspaceRecord("未归类", ""));
+        TitleRecord title = titles.getOrDefault(id, new TitleRecord("", Instant.EPOCH));
+        String displayTitle = firstNonBlank(title.title(), text(header, "title"), text(header, "name"), "未命名会话");
+        Instant updatedAt = maxInstant(
+            title.updatedAt(),
+            toInstant(longValue(header, "lastUpdatedAt")),
+            toInstant(longValue(header, "updated_at")),
+            toInstant(longValue(header, "updatedAt"))
+        );
+        sessions.put(id, new AiRuntimeSession(
+            "Cursor",
+            id,
+            displayTitle,
+            workspace.projectName(),
+            workspace.cwd(),
+            "",
+            updatedAt
+        ));
+    }
+
+    private List<Path> findFiles(Path root, String fileName, int maxDepth) {
+        List<Path> files = new ArrayList<>();
+        try (Stream<Path> stream = Files.walk(root, maxDepth)) {
+            stream.filter(Files::isRegularFile)
+                .filter(path -> fileName.equalsIgnoreCase(path.getFileName().toString()))
+                .limit(MAX_SCAN_FILES)
+                .forEach(files::add);
+        } catch (Exception e) {
+            logger.info("Cursor file scan skipped: {}", e.getMessage());
+        }
+        return files;
+    }
+
+    private Set<String> columns(ResultSetMetaData meta) throws Exception {
+        Set<String> columns = new HashSet<>();
+        for (int i = 1; i <= meta.getColumnCount(); i++) {
+            columns.add(meta.getColumnName(i));
+        }
+        return columns;
+    }
+
+    private String firstColumn(ResultSet rs, Set<String> columns, String... names) throws Exception {
+        for (String name : names) {
+            if (columns.contains(name)) {
+                String value = rs.getString(name);
+                if (value != null && !value.isBlank()) {
+                    return value.strip();
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        return "";
+    }
+
+    private long longColumn(ResultSet rs, Set<String> columns, String... names) throws Exception {
+        for (String name : names) {
+            if (columns.contains(name)) {
+                long value = rs.getLong(name);
+                if (value > 0) {
+                    return value;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        return 0L;
+    }
+
+    private String text(JsonNode node, String field) {
+        JsonNode value = node == null ? null : node.get(field);
+        return value == null || value.isNull() ? "" : value.asText("");
+    }
+
+    private JsonNode parseJson(String value) {
+        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
+            return null;
+        }
+        try {
+            return mapper.readTree(value);
+        } catch (Exception e) {
+            return null;
+        }
+    }
+
+    private boolean booleanValue(JsonNode node, String field) {
+        JsonNode value = node == null ? null : node.get(field);
+        return value != null && value.asBoolean(false);
+    }
+
+    private boolean booleanColumn(ResultSet rs, Set<String> columns, String... names) throws Exception {
+        for (String name : names) {
+            if (!columns.contains(name)) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            Object value = rs.getObject(name);
+            if (value instanceof Boolean bool) {
+                if (bool) {
+                    return true;
+                }
+                continue;
+            }
+            if (value instanceof Number number) {
+                if (number.longValue() != 0L) {
+                    return true;
+                }
+                continue;
+            }
+            if (value != null) {
+                String text = value.toString().strip().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
+                if ("1".equals(text) || "true".equals(text) || "yes".equals(text)) {
+                    return true;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    private long longValue(JsonNode node, String field) {
+        JsonNode value = node == null ? null : node.get(field);
+        return value == null || !value.canConvertToLong() ? 0L : value.asLong();
+    }
+
+    private Instant toInstant(long value) {
+        if (value <= 0) {
+            return Instant.EPOCH;
+        }
+        return value > 10_000_000_000L ? Instant.ofEpochMilli(value) : Instant.ofEpochSecond(value);
+    }
+
+    private Instant maxInstant(Instant... values) {
+        Instant result = Instant.EPOCH;
+        for (Instant value : values) {
+            if (value != null && value.isAfter(result)) {
+                result = value;
+            }
+        }
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    private String firstNonBlank(String... values) {
+        if (values != null) {
+            for (String value : values) {
+                if (value != null && !value.isBlank()) {
+                    return value.strip();
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        return "";
+    }
+
+    private record TitleRecord(String title, Instant updatedAt) {}
+    private record WorkspaceRecord(String projectName, String cwd) {}
+    private record ComposerTableResult(boolean tableFound, List<AiRuntimeSession> sessions) {}
+}

+package com.example.ahakey.service;
+
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.*;
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.node.*;
+import org.tomlj.*;
+import java.io.IOException;
+import java.nio.file.Path;
+import java.util.*;
+import java.util.regex.Pattern;
+
+/** Validated edits to AhaKey-owned registrations; unrelated entries and TOML text survive. */
+final class HookConfigEditor {
+    private static final ObjectMapper JSON=new ObjectMapper()
+            .enable(com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonParser.Feature.STRICT_DUPLICATE_DETECTION)
+            .enable(DeserializationFeature.FAIL_ON_TRAILING_TOKENS);
+    private static final Set<String> KIMI_EVENTS=Set.of("PreToolUse","PostToolUse","PostToolUseFailure",
+            "UserPromptSubmit","Stop","StopFailure","SessionStart","SessionEnd","SubagentStart",
+            "SubagentStop","PreCompact","PostCompact","Notification");
+    private static final Pattern FILE_ARGUMENT=Pattern.compile("(?i)(?:^|\\s)-File\\s+(?:\"([^\"]+)\"|'([^']+)'|(\\S+))");
+    private final Path scripts;
+    HookConfigEditor(Path scripts){this.scripts=scripts.toAbsolutePath().normalize();}
+
+    boolean owned(String command,String platform) {
+        var match=FILE_ARGUMENT.matcher(command);
+        if(!match.find())return false;
+        String value=match.group(1)!=null?match.group(1):match.group(2)!=null?match.group(2):match.group(3);
+        try {
+            Path path=Path.of(value).toAbsolutePath().normalize();
+            return path.equals(scripts.resolve("ahakey-"+platform.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)+".ps1"))
+                    || path.equals(scripts.resolve("ahakey-hook.ps1"));
+        }catch(RuntimeException invalid){return false;}
+    }
+    ObjectNode json(String text)throws IOException {
+        if(text.isBlank())return JSON.createObjectNode();
+        JsonNode value=JSON.readTree(stripBom(text));
+        if(!(value instanceof ObjectNode root))throw new IOException("配置根必须是 JSON 对象，原文件未修改。");
+        JsonNode hooks=root.get("hooks");
+        if(hooks!=null&&!hooks.isObject())throw new IOException("hooks 必须是对象，原配置未修改。");
+        return root;
+    }
+    ObjectNode mergeJson(String text,String platform,Map<String,ObjectNode> replacements,boolean nested)throws IOException {
+        ObjectNode root=json(text);ObjectNode hooks=root.has("hooks")?(ObjectNode)root.get("hooks"):JSON.createObjectNode();
+        for(String event:new ArrayList<>(java.util.stream.StreamSupport.stream(
+                Spliterators.spliteratorUnknownSize(hooks.fieldNames(),0),false).toList())) {
+            if(!hooks.get(event).isArray())throw new IOException("Hook 事件列表格式无效："+event);
+            ArrayNode kept=JSON.createArrayNode();
+            for(JsonNode entry:hooks.get(event)) {
+                if(!entry.isObject())throw new IOException("Hook 条目格式无效："+event);
+                if(!nested){if(!owned(entry.path("command").asText(""),platform))kept.add(entry);continue;}
+                if(!entry.path("hooks").isArray())throw new IOException("Hook 命令列表格式无效："+event);
+                ObjectNode group=((ObjectNode)entry).deepCopy();ArrayNode commands=JSON.createArrayNode();boolean removed=false;
+                for(JsonNode command:entry.get("hooks")) {
+                    if(owned(command.path("command").asText(""),platform))removed=true;else commands.add(command);
+                }
+                if(!removed||!commands.isEmpty()){group.set("hooks",commands);kept.add(group);}
+            }
+            if(kept.isEmpty())hooks.remove(event);else hooks.set(event,kept);
+        }
+        replacements.forEach((event,entry)->{
+            ArrayNode array=hooks.has(event)?(ArrayNode)hooks.get(event):JSON.createArrayNode();array.add(entry);hooks.set(event,array);
+        });
+        if(hooks.isEmpty())root.remove("hooks");else root.set("hooks",hooks);
+        return root;
+    }
+    boolean jsonRegistered(String text,String platform,Map<String,ObjectNode> expected,boolean nested)throws IOException {
+        ObjectNode root=json(text);Map<String,List<JsonNode>> found=new HashMap<>();
+        // Validate the same structure used for edits even when registrations are incomplete.
+        mergeJson(text,platform,Map.of(),nested);
+        if(platform.equals("Cursor")&&(!root.path("version").isIntegralNumber()||root.path("version").asInt()!=1))return false;
+        var events=root.path("hooks").fields();
+        while(events.hasNext()) {
+            var event=events.next();
+            for(JsonNode group:event.getValue())for(JsonNode item:nested?group.path("hooks"):List.of(group)) {
+                String command=item.path("command").asText("");
+                if(owned(command,platform)) {
+                    ObjectNode registration=((ObjectNode)group).deepCopy();
+                    if(nested)registration.set("hooks",JSON.createArrayNode().add(item));
+                    found.computeIfAbsent(event.getKey(),x->new ArrayList<>()).add(registration);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        return found.size()==expected.size()&&expected.entrySet().stream().allMatch(e->List.of(e.getValue()).equals(found.get(e.getKey())));
+    }
+    private static String stripBom(String s){return s.startsWith("\uFEFF")?s.substring(1):s;}
+    private static TomlParseResult toml(String text)throws IOException {
+        TomlParseResult result=Toml.parse(stripBom(text));
+        if(result.hasErrors())throw new IOException("TOML 配置无法解析，原文件未修改（"+result.errors().get(0).position()+"）。");
+        return result;
+    }
+    private static TomlArray hooks(TomlParseResult root)throws IOException {
+        if(!root.contains("hooks"))return null;
+        if(!root.isArray("hooks")||!root.getArray("hooks").containsTables())throw new IOException("Kimi hooks 必须是表数组。");
+        return root.getArray("hooks");
+    }
+    String mergeKimi(String text,String replacement)throws IOException {
+        var parsed=toml(text);var hooks=hooks(parsed);String[] lines=text.split("(?<=\n)",-1);Set<Integer> remove=new HashSet<>();
+        if(hooks!=null&&hooks.isEmpty()&&!replacement.isEmpty()) {
+            int line=parsed.inputPositionOf("hooks").line()-1;
+            if(!lines[line].strip().matches("hooks\\s*=\\s*\\[\\s*]\\s*(?:#.*)?"))throw new IOException("Kimi 空 hooks 数组使用复杂表达式，原文件未修改。");
+            remove.add(line);
+        }
+        if(hooks!=null)for(int i=0;i<hooks.size();i++) {
+            TomlTable hook=hooks.getTable(i);
+            if(!hook.isString("command")||!owned(hook.getString("command"),"Kimi"))continue;
+            int line=hooks.inputPositionOf(i).line()-1;
+            // Inline arrays/nested custom tables are deliberately not rewritten with regex.
+            if(!lines[line].strip().matches("\\[\\[\\s*hooks\\s*]]\\s*(?:#.*)?"))throw new IOException("Kimi 自有 Hook 使用复杂 TOML 表达式，无法安全迁移，原文件未修改。");
+            remove.add(line);
+            for(String key:hook.keySet()) {
+                int at=hook.inputPositionOf(key).line()-1;
+                TomlParseResult single=Toml.parse(stripBom(lines[at]));
+                if(single.hasErrors()||single.size()!=1||!single.contains(List.of(key))
+                        ||!Objects.equals(single.get(List.of(key)),hook.get(List.of(key))))
+                    throw new IOException("Kimi 自有 Hook 含多行或嵌套字段，无法安全迁移，原文件未修改。");
+                remove.add(at);
+            }
+        }
+        StringBuilder kept=new StringBuilder();
+        for(int i=0;i<lines.length;i++) {
+            String line=lines[i].strip();
+            if(!remove.contains(i)&&!line.equals(HookInstaller.KIMI_HOOK_BLOCK_START)&&!line.equals(HookInstaller.KIMI_HOOK_BLOCK_END))kept.append(lines[i]);
+        }
+        String result=kept+ (kept.length()>0&&!kept.toString().endsWith("\n")?"\n":"")+replacement;
+        validateKimiHooks(toml(result));return result;
+    }
+    private static void validateKimiHooks(TomlParseResult root)throws IOException {
+        var hooks=hooks(root);if(hooks==null)return;
+        for(int i=0;i<hooks.size();i++) {
+            var hook=hooks.getTable(i);
+            if(!hook.isString("event")||!KIMI_EVENTS.contains(hook.getString("event"))||!hook.isString("command")
+                    ||(hook.contains("matcher")&&!hook.isString("matcher"))
+                    ||(hook.contains("timeout")&&(!hook.isLong("timeout")||hook.getLong("timeout")<1||hook.getLong("timeout")>600)))
+                throw new IOException("Kimi 1.44.0 不支持 hooks["+i+"] 的事件或字段；第三方条目未修改。");
+        }
+    }
+    boolean kimiRegistered(String text,Map<String,String> expected)throws IOException {
+        var root=toml(text);validateKimiHooks(root);var hooks=hooks(root);if(hooks==null)return false;
+        Map<String,List<String>> found=new HashMap<>();
+        for(int i=0;i<hooks.size();i++) {
+            var hook=hooks.getTable(i);String command=hook.getString("command");
+            if(owned(command,"Kimi")) {
+                String event=hook.getString("event");
+                if(!hook.getString("matcher",()->"").isEmpty()||hook.getLong("timeout",()->30)!=(event.equals("PreToolUse")?20:10))return false;
+                found.computeIfAbsent(event,x->new ArrayList<>()).add(command);
+            }
+        }
+        return found.size()==expected.size()&&expected.entrySet().stream().allMatch(e->List.of(e.getValue()).equals(found.get(e.getKey())));
+    }
+    static boolean codexEnabled(String text)throws IOException {
+        var root=toml(text);return root.isBoolean("features.hooks")&&root.getBoolean("features.hooks");
+    }
+    static String enableCodex(String text)throws IOException {
+        var root=toml(text);String[] lines=text.split("(?<=\n)",-1);
+        if(root.contains("features.hooks")) {
+            if(!root.isBoolean("features.hooks"))throw new IOException("Codex features.hooks 必须是布尔值。");
+            if(root.getBoolean("features.hooks"))return text;
+            int at=root.inputPositionOf("features.hooks").line()-1;
+            if(!lines[at].strip().matches("hooks\\s*=\\s*false\\s*(?:#.*)?"))throw new IOException("Codex hooks 开关使用复杂表达式，无法安全修改。");
+            lines[at]=lines[at].replaceFirst("false","true");return String.join("",lines);
+        }
+        if(root.contains("features")) {
+            int at=root.inputPositionOf("features").line()-1;
+            if(!lines[at].strip().matches("\\[features]\\s*(?:#.*)?"))throw new IOException("Codex features 使用复杂表达式，无法安全修改。");
+            lines[at]=lines[at]+(lines[at].endsWith("\n")?"":"\n")+"hooks = true\n";
+            return String.join("",lines);
+        }
+        return text+"\n[features]\nhooks = true\n";
+    }
+}

-    private void generateCoreScript(Path hooksDir) throws Exception {

+    /** Only exact prior generated wrappers are migrated. User edits and all platform configuration stay untouched. */
+    public void migrateOwnedApprovalScripts() {
+        Path hooks=Paths.get(System.getProperty("user.home"),".ahakey","hooks");
+        if(!Files.isDirectory(hooks))return;
+        String[] names={CLAUDE_SCRIPT_NAME,CODEX_SCRIPT_NAME};
+        String[] known={"0d90c6a7e5fc68e97efb6cec57fda74cafba2699b351003663ff9ea1a37f9c30","aa5c340af397050f746d977a02ab8c4cf58b42c9d4bf4cd5dcd548835ee56075"};
+        for(int i=0;i<names.length;i++) {
+            Path file=hooks.resolve(names[i]);Path staging=null;
+            try {
+                if(!Files.isRegularFile(file) || Files.isSymbolicLink(file))continue;
+                byte[] before=Files.readAllBytes(file);
+                String hash=java.util.HexFormat.of().formatHex(java.security.MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(before));
+                if(!hash.equals(known[i]))continue;
+                staging=Files.createTempDirectory(hooks,"approval-migration-");
+                if(i==0)generateClaudeScript(staging);else generateCodexScript(staging);
+                if(!Arrays.equals(before,Files.readAllBytes(file)))throw new java.io.IOException("Hook changed during migration");
+                Files.write(hooks.resolve(names[i]+".wd007-"+java.util.UUID.randomUUID()+".bak"),before,java.nio.file.StandardOpenOption.CREATE_NEW);
+                Files.move(staging.resolve(names[i]),file,StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE,StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
+                log("已备份并迁移自有审批脚本："+names[i]);
+            } catch(Exception failure){log("审批脚本未迁移："+names[i]+"，"+failure.getMessage());}
+            finally{if(staging!=null)try{Files.deleteIfExists(staging.resolve(names[i]));Files.deleteIfExists(staging);}catch(Exception ignored){}}
+        }
+    }

+        try {
+            Files.createDirectories(hooksDir);
+            generateCoreScript(hooksDir);
+            generateClaudeScript(hooksDir);
+            generateCodexScript(hooksDir);
+            generateKimiScript(hooksDir);
+            generateCursorScript(hooksDir);
+            log("[安装] 已生成所有 Hook 脚本");

+    /**
+     * 安装指定平台的 Hook
+     */
+    public void install(String platform) {
+        installOrUpdate(platform);
+    }
+
+    public HookWriteResult installOrUpdate(String platform) {
+        log("[安装] 开始安装 " + platform + " Hook...");
+        try {
+            if(!isAppDetected(platform))return new HookWriteResult(false,"未检测到应用启动入口；配置目录本身不能证明应用已安装。");
+            if(platform.equals("Kimi")&&!kimiSupported())return new HookWriteResult(false,statusDetail(platform));
+            if(getHookStatus(platform)==HookStatus.LATEST)return new HookWriteResult(true,"脚本与注册已是最新，无需重复写入；客户端需重新加载配置。");
+            Map<Path,PlannedFile> plan=configurationPlan(platform,false);
+            Path scripts=scriptsDirectory();
+            plan.put(scripts.resolve(CORE_SCRIPT_NAME),planned(scripts.resolve(CORE_SCRIPT_NAME),coreScript()));
+            plan.put(scripts.resolve(scriptName(platform)),planned(scripts.resolve(scriptName(platform)),scriptContent(platform)));
+            applyPlan(plan);
+            HookStatus after=getHookStatus(platform);
+            if(after!=HookStatus.LATEST)return new HookWriteResult(false,"文件已备份并写入，但重检未就绪："+statusDetail(platform));
+            return new HookWriteResult(true,"已更新并验证脚本与注册；请重启对应客户端使配置生效。"+(platform.equals("Kimi")?"生命周期 Hook 已就绪；独立拨轮审批适配未在此项验证。":""));
+        } catch (Exception e) {
+            String reason = classifyFailure(e)+"："+(e.getMessage()==null?"请检查配置文件":e.getMessage());
+            log("[错误] " + platform + " 安装失败: " + reason + " - " + e.getMessage());
+            return new HookWriteResult(false, reason);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * 卸载指定平台的 Hook
+     */
+    public void uninstall(String platform) {
+        try{applyPlan(configurationPlan(platform,true));log("已移除 "+platform+" 的 AhaKey 注册，其他 Hook 保留。");}
+        catch(Exception failure){log("卸载未完成："+failure.getMessage());}
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * 检查指定平台的 Hook 是否已安装
+     */
+    public boolean isInstalled(String platform) {
+        try {
+            String text=readOptional(getHookConfigPath(platform));
+            HookConfigEditor editor=new HookConfigEditor(scriptsDirectory());
+            return platform.equals("Kimi")?editor.kimiRegistered(text,expectedCommands(platform))
+                    :editor.jsonRegistered(text,platform,registrations(platform),!platform.equals("Cursor"));
+        }catch(Exception invalid){return false;}
+    }
+
+    public HookStatus getHookStatus(String platform) {
+        try {
+            statusDetails.remove(platform);
+            if (!isAppDetected(platform)) {
+                boolean trace=Files.exists(getHookConfigPath(platform))||Files.isDirectory(getHookConfigPath(platform).getParent());
+                statusDetails.put(platform,trace?"仅发现配置痕迹，未检测到应用启动入口；不会据此安装 Hook。":"未检测到应用启动入口，请先安装对应应用后重新检测。");
+                return trace?HookStatus.CONFIG_ONLY:HookStatus.APP_NOT_FOUND;
+            }
+            if(platform.equals("Kimi")&&!kimiSupported())return HookStatus.UNSUPPORTED;
+            Path config=getHookConfigPath(platform);String text=readOptional(config);
+            HookConfigEditor editor=new HookConfigEditor(scriptsDirectory());
+            boolean registered;
+            if(platform.equals("Kimi")) {
+                editor.mergeKimi(text,""); // Reject invalid unrelated entries; owned obsolete events remain repairable.
+                try{registered=editor.kimiRegistered(text,expectedCommands(platform));}catch(IOException obsolete){registered=false;}
+            } else registered=editor.jsonRegistered(text,platform,registrations(platform),!platform.equals("Cursor"));
+            boolean scripts=readOptional(scriptsDirectory().resolve(scriptName(platform))).replace("\r\n","\n").equals(scriptContent(platform))
+                    &&readOptional(scriptsDirectory().resolve(CORE_SCRIPT_NAME)).replace("\r\n","\n").equals(coreScript());
+            if(platform.equals("Codex"))registered&=HookConfigEditor.codexEnabled(readOptional(config.resolveSibling("config.toml")));
+            statusDetails.put(platform,registered&&scripts?"脚本内容与正式配置中的事件注册一致；客户端需重新加载配置。":"脚本或正式事件注册缺失/不匹配，可显式安装或更新修复。");
+            return registered&&scripts?HookStatus.LATEST:Files.exists(config)?HookStatus.OUTDATED:HookStatus.NOT_INSTALLED;
+        } catch (Exception e) {
+            log("[错误] 检查 " + platform + " Hook 状态失败: " + e.getMessage());
+            statusDetails.put(platform,"配置检测失败："+e.getMessage()+" 请修复配置后重新检测。");
+            return HookStatus.ERROR;
+        }
+    }
+
+    // ==================== 脚本生成 ====================
+
+    private Path scriptsDirectory(){return Paths.get(System.getProperty("user.home"),".ahakey","hooks");}
+    private String scriptName(String platform){return switch(platform){case "Claude"->CLAUDE_SCRIPT_NAME;case "Cursor"->CURSOR_SCRIPT_NAME;case "Codex"->CODEX_SCRIPT_NAME;case "Kimi"->KIMI_SCRIPT_NAME;default->throw new IllegalArgumentException("未知平台："+platform);};}
+    private String scriptContent(String platform){return switch(platform){case "Claude"->claudeScript();case "Cursor"->cursorScript();case "Codex"->codexScript();case "Kimi"->kimiScript();default->throw new IllegalArgumentException("未知平台："+platform);};}
+    private String[][] events(String platform){return switch(platform){case "Claude"->CLAUDE_EVENTS;case "Cursor"->CURSOR_EVENTS;case "Codex"->CODEX_EVENTS;case "Kimi"->KIMI_EVENTS;default->throw new IllegalArgumentException("未知平台："+platform);};}
+    private Map<String,String> expectedCommands(String platform) {
+        Map<String,String> commands=new LinkedHashMap<>();
+        for(String[] event:events(platform))commands.put(event[0],buildHookCommand(scriptName(platform),event.length==3?event[1]:event[0]));
+        return commands;
+    }
+    private static String readOptional(Path path)throws IOException{return Files.exists(path)?Files.readString(path):"";}
+    private Map<String,ObjectNode> registrations(String platform) {
+            Map<String,ObjectNode> replacements=new LinkedHashMap<>();
+            for(String[] event:events(platform)) {
+                ObjectNode command=mapper.createObjectNode();command.put("command",expectedCommands(platform).get(event[0]));
+                command.put("timeout",Integer.parseInt(event[event.length-1]));
+                if(platform.equals("Cursor"))replacements.put(event[0],command);
+                else {
+                    command.put("type","command");ObjectNode group=mapper.createObjectNode();
+                    if(platform.equals("Claude"))group.put("matcher","");
+                    else if(event[0].equals("SessionStart"))group.put("matcher","startup|resume|clear");
+                    else if(!event[0].equals("Stop")&&!event[0].equals("UserPromptSubmit"))group.put("matcher","*");
+                    group.set("hooks",mapper.createArrayNode().add(command));replacements.put(event[0],group);
+                }
+            }
+        return replacements;
+    }
+    private record PlannedFile(byte[] before,byte[] after) {}
+    private static byte[] readBytes(Path path)throws IOException{return Files.exists(path)?Files.readAllBytes(path):null;}
+    private static String decode(byte[] bytes)throws IOException{return bytes==null?"":java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8.newDecoder().decode(java.nio.ByteBuffer.wrap(bytes)).toString();}
+    private static PlannedFile planned(Path path,String next)throws IOException{return new PlannedFile(readBytes(path),next.getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8));}
+    private Map<Path,PlannedFile> configurationPlan(String platform,boolean remove)throws Exception {
+        Path path=getHookConfigPath(platform);byte[] original=readBytes(path);String existing=decode(original);String next;
+        HookConfigEditor editor=new HookConfigEditor(scriptsDirectory());Map<Path,PlannedFile> plan=new LinkedHashMap<>();
+        if(platform.equals("Kimi"))next=editor.mergeKimi(existing,remove?"":buildKimiHookBlock());
+        else {
+            Map<String,ObjectNode> replacements=remove?Map.of():registrations(platform);
+            ObjectNode root=editor.mergeJson(existing,platform,replacements,!platform.equals("Cursor"));
+            if(platform.equals("Cursor")&&!remove)root.put("version",1);
+            next=mapper.writerWithDefaultPrettyPrinter().writeValueAsString(root)+"\n";
+        }
+        plan.put(path,new PlannedFile(original,next.getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8)));
+        if(platform.equals("Codex")&&!remove) {
+            Path toml=path.resolveSibling("config.toml");
+            byte[] oldToml=readBytes(toml);
+            plan.put(toml,new PlannedFile(oldToml,HookConfigEditor.enableCodex(decode(oldToml)).getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8)));
+            Path sidecar=path.resolveSibling(CODEX_SIDECAR_NAME);plan.put(sidecar,planned(sidecar,AHAKEY_HOOK_VERSION+"\n"));
+        }
+        return plan;
+    }
+    private static void atomicWrite(Path path,byte[] bytes)throws IOException {
+        Files.createDirectories(path.getParent());Path temporary=Files.createTempFile(path.getParent(),".ahakey-write-",".tmp");
+        try {
+            Files.write(temporary,bytes);
+            try{Files.move(temporary,path,StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE,StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);}
+            catch(java.nio.file.AtomicMoveNotSupportedException unsupported){Files.move(temporary,path,StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);}
+        }finally{Files.deleteIfExists(temporary);}
+    }
+    private void applyPlan(Map<Path,PlannedFile> plan)throws IOException {
+        List<Path> written=new ArrayList<>();
+        // Validate and back up every existing input before replacing any of them.
+        for(Path path:plan.keySet()) {
+            if(Files.isSymbolicLink(path))throw new IOException("不覆盖符号链接："+path.getFileName());
+            byte[] bytes=readBytes(path);
+            if(!Arrays.equals(bytes,plan.get(path).before()))throw new IOException("配置在生成更新方案后已被其他程序更改："+path.getFileName());
+            if(bytes!=null&&!Arrays.equals(bytes,plan.get(path).after()))Files.write(path.resolveSibling(path.getFileName()+".ahakey-"+UUID.randomUUID()+".bak"),bytes,java.nio.file.StandardOpenOption.CREATE_NEW);
+        }
+        try {
+            for(var entry:plan.entrySet()) {
+                Path path=entry.getKey();byte[] current=Files.exists(path)?Files.readAllBytes(path):null;
+                if(!Arrays.equals(entry.getValue().before(),current))throw new IOException("配置在更新期间已被其他程序更改："+path.getFileName());
+                if(!Arrays.equals(current,entry.getValue().after())){atomicWrite(path,entry.getValue().after());written.add(path);}
+            }
+        }catch(IOException failure) {
+            Collections.reverse(written);
+            for(Path path:written)try {
+                if(!Arrays.equals(Files.readAllBytes(path),plan.get(path).after()))throw new IOException("回滚前文件已被其他程序更改");
+                if(plan.get(path).before()==null)Files.delete(path);else atomicWrite(path,plan.get(path).before());
+            }catch(IOException rollback){failure.addSuppressed(rollback);log("更新未完成，请使用保留备份恢复 "+path.getFileName()+"："+rollback.getMessage());}
+            throw failure;
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void generateCoreScript(Path hooksDir) throws Exception {
+        Files.writeString(hooksDir.resolve(CORE_SCRIPT_NAME), coreScript());
+    }
+
+    private String coreScript() {
+        String content =
+            "# AhaKey Core - Auto-generated, do not edit\n" +
+            "# ahakey-hook-version: " + AHAKEY_HOOK_VERSION + "\n" +
+            "# Contains TCP connection logic, to be dot-sourced by platform-specific scripts\n" +
+            "try {\n" +
+            "    $stdinText = $null\n" +
             "    if ([Console]::IsInputRedirected) { $stdinText = [Console]::In.ReadToEnd() }\n" +
             "} catch { }\n" +
             "$payload = [ordered]@{ cmd = $EventName; ahakeyHookVersion = '" + AHAKEY_HOOK_VERSION + "'; cwd = (Get-Location).Path }\n" +
@@ -256,492 +328,223 @@
             "        $payload.hookInputRawBase64 = [Convert]::ToBase64String([Text.Encoding]::UTF8.GetBytes($stdinText))\n" +
             "    }\n" +
             "}\n" +
-            "$jsonLine = ($payload | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 20 -Compress)\n" +
-            "try {\n" +
-            "    $tcp = New-Object System.Net.Sockets.TcpClient\n" +
-            "    $tcp.Connect('127.0.0.1', " + currentPort() + ")\n" +
-            "    $writer = New-Object System.IO.StreamWriter($tcp.GetStream())\n" +
-            "    $writer.WriteLine($jsonLine)\n" +
-            "    $writer.Flush()\n" +
-            "    $reader = New-Object System.IO.StreamReader($tcp.GetStream())\n" +
-            "    $response = $reader.ReadLine()\n" +
-            "    $tcp.Close()\n" +
-            "} catch {\n" +
-            "    $response = $null\n" +
-            "}\n";
-        Files.write(scriptPath, content.getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
-    }

-    private void generateClaudeScript(Path hooksDir) throws Exception {
-        Path scriptPath = hooksDir.resolve(CLAUDE_SCRIPT_NAME);

+            "    $response = $null\n" +

+    private String claudeScript() {

+            "# ahakey-hook-version: " + AHAKEY_HOOK_VERSION + "\n" +
+            "param([Parameter(Position=0)][string]$EventName)\n" +
+            ". (Join-Path $env:USERPROFILE '.ahakey\\hooks\\ahakey-core.ps1')\n" +

+    protected Path findLauncher(String platform) {
+        String name=platform.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);Path home=Path.of(System.getProperty("user.home"));
+        List<Path> directories=new ArrayList<>(List.of(home.resolve(".local/bin"),home.resolve("AppData/Roaming/npm"),home.resolve("AppData/Local/Programs/"+name)));
+        String path=System.getenv("PATH");if(path!=null)for(String directory:path.split(java.util.regex.Pattern.quote(java.io.File.pathSeparator)))try{directories.add(Path.of(directory.replace("\"","")));}catch(RuntimeException ignored){}
+        for(Path directory:directories)for(String suffix:List.of(".exe",".cmd",".bat")) {
+            Path candidate=directory.resolve(name+suffix);if(Files.isRegularFile(candidate))return candidate;
+        }
+        if(platform.equals("Cursor")) {
+            for(var root:List.of(com.sun.jna.platform.win32.WinReg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER,com.sun.jna.platform.win32.WinReg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE))try {
+                String key="Software\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion\\Uninstall";
+                for(String child:com.sun.jna.platform.win32.Advapi32Util.registryGetKeys(root,key))try {
+                    String item=key+"\\"+child;
+                    if(!com.sun.jna.platform.win32.Advapi32Util.registryGetStringValue(root,item,"DisplayName").startsWith("Cursor"))continue;
+                    Path install=Path.of(com.sun.jna.platform.win32.Advapi32Util.registryGetStringValue(root,item,"InstallLocation"));
+                    for(Path candidate:List.of(install.resolve("Cursor.exe"),install.resolve("_/Cursor.exe")))if(Files.isRegularFile(candidate))return candidate;
+                }catch(RuntimeException ignored){}
+            }catch(RuntimeException|UnsatisfiedLinkError ignored){}
+        }
+        return null;
+    }
+    protected String kimiVersion()throws Exception {
+        Path launcher=findLauncher("Kimi");
+        if(launcher==null||!launcher.toString().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).endsWith(".exe"))return "";
+        Process probe=new ProcessBuilder(launcher.toString(),"--version").redirectErrorStream(true).start();
+        var output=new java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream();
+        Thread reader=new Thread(()->{try(var in=probe.getInputStream()){byte[] b=new byte[1024];int n;while((n=in.read(b))!=-1){if(output.size()+n<=8192)output.write(b,0,n);}}catch(IOException ignored){}},"kimi-version-read");reader.setDaemon(true);reader.start();
+        try {
+            if(!probe.waitFor(4,java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS))return "";
+            reader.join(500);if(probe.exitValue()!=0||reader.isAlive())return "";
+            var version=java.util.regex.Pattern.compile("(?i)kimi(?:[- ]cli)?(?:,?\\s+version)?[, :v]+(\\d+\\.\\d+\\.\\d+)").matcher(output.toString(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
+            return version.find()?version.group(1):"";
+        }finally{if(probe.isAlive())probe.destroyForcibly();}
+    }
+    private boolean kimiSupported()throws Exception {
+        String version=kimiVersion();boolean supported=version.equals("1.44.0");
+        if(!supported)statusDetails.put("Kimi","已检测启动入口，但"+(version.isBlank()?"无法确认 Kimi CLI 版本":"Kimi "+version+" 的 Hook 能力尚未确认")+"；当前仅验证 kimi-cli 1.44.0，不会写入未经确认的配置。");
+        return supported;
+    }
+
+    private String classifyFailure(Exception e) {
+        Throwable root = e;
+        while (root.getCause() != null) {
+            root = root.getCause();
+        }
+        String message = root.getMessage() == null ? "" : root.getMessage();
+        if (root instanceof java.nio.file.AccessDeniedException || message.contains("being used by another process")) {
+            return "文件不可写";
+        }
+        if (root instanceof com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonProcessingException) {
+            return "配置无法读取";
+        }
+        if (message.contains(".bak") || message.contains("copy")) {
+            return "备份失败";
+        }
+        return "未知错误";
+    }
+
+    private String buildKimiHookBlock() {
+        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
+        sb.append(KIMI_HOOK_BLOCK_START).append("\n");
+        sb.append("# Managed by AhaKey. Kimi CLI hooks run this installer with Kimi* event names.\n");
+        for (String[] ev : KIMI_EVENTS) {
+            sb.append("\n[[hooks]]\n");
+            sb.append("event = \"").append(ev[0]).append("\"\n");
+            sb.append("matcher = \"\"\n");
+            sb.append("command = \"").append(tomlEscape(buildHookCommand(KIMI_SCRIPT_NAME, ev[1]))).append("\"\n");
+            sb.append("timeout = ").append(ev[2]).append("\n");
+        }
+        sb.append("\n").append(KIMI_HOOK_BLOCK_END).append("\n");
+        return sb.toString();
+    }
+
+    private String tomlEscape(String s) {
+        return s.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace("\"", "\\\"");
+    }
+
+    private void log(String message) {
+        if (logger != null) {
+            logger.accept(message);
+        }
+    }
+}

### src/main/java/com/example/ahakey/service/KeySimulationService.java
--- start/src/main/java/com/example/ahakey/service/KeySimulationService.java
+++ current/src/main/java/com/example/ahakey/service/KeySimulationService.java
@@ -0,0 +1,73 @@
+package com.example.ahakey.service;
+
+import com.example.ahakey.model.KeyConfig;
+import java.util.ArrayList;
+import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
+import java.util.List;
+
+/** Executes an immutable editor snapshot. This class never accesses a device or changes focus. */
+public final class KeySimulationService {
+    public record Step(String action, int value) {}
+    public record Action(List<Step> steps) {
+        public Action { steps = List.copyOf(steps); }
+    }
+    @FunctionalInterface public interface Sender { void send(int hid, boolean down) throws Exception; }
+    @FunctionalInterface public interface Delay { void waitMillis(long millis) throws InterruptedException; }
+
+    public static Action snapshot(KeyConfig key) {
+        List<Step> steps = new ArrayList<>();
+        if (key.usesMacro()) {
+            for (int i = 0; i < key.getMacroStepCount(); i++)
+                steps.add(new Step(key.getMacroStepAction(i), key.getMacroStepParam(i)));
+        } else {
+            int code = key.getHidCode();
+            int[] bits = {0x200, 0x100, 0x400, 0x800, 0x2000, 0x1000, 0x4000, 0x8000};
+            List<Integer> keys = new ArrayList<>();
+            for (int i = 0; i < bits.length; i++) if ((code & bits[i]) != 0) keys.add(0xE0 + i);
+            if ((code & 255) != 0) keys.add(code & 255);
+            for (int hid : keys) steps.add(new Step("DOWN_KEY", hid));
+            for (int i = keys.size() - 1; i >= 0; i--) steps.add(new Step("UP_KEY", keys.get(i)));
+        }
+        if (steps.isEmpty()) throw new IllegalArgumentException("未设置动作，无法模拟。");
+        for (Step step : steps) {
+            if (step.value < 0 || step.value > 255 || !List.of("DOWN_KEY", "UP_KEY", "DELAY").contains(step.action))
+                throw new IllegalArgumentException("宏步骤无效。");
+        }
+        return new Action(steps);
+    }
+
+    public static void execute(Action action, Sender sender, Delay delay) throws Exception {
+        LinkedHashSet<Integer> pressed = new LinkedHashSet<>();
+        Exception failure = null;
+        try {
+            for (Step step : action.steps) {
+                if (Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted()) throw new InterruptedException("模拟已取消");
+                switch (step.action) {
+                    case "DELAY" -> delay.waitMillis(step.value * 3L);
+                    case "DOWN_KEY" -> {
+                        // A native failure may still have delivered the key; always attempt its release.
+                        pressed.add(step.value);
+                        sender.send(step.value, true);
+                    }
+                    case "UP_KEY" -> {
+                        // Do not release a key held by the user, outside this simulation.
+                        if (pressed.contains(step.value)) {
+                            sender.send(step.value, false);
+                            pressed.remove(step.value);
+                        }
+                    }
+                    default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException("未知宏步骤");
+                }
+            }
+        } catch (Exception e) { failure = e; }
+        finally {
+            var keys = new ArrayList<>(pressed);
+            for (int i = keys.size() - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
+                try { sender.send(keys.get(i), false); }
+                catch (Exception e) { if (failure == null) failure = e; else failure.addSuppressed(e); }
+            }
+        }
+        if (failure != null) throw failure;
+    }
+    private KeySimulationService() {}
+}

     private void sendPacket(DataOutputStream out, byte type, byte[] data) throws IOException {

### src/main/java/com/example/ahakey/service/connection/DeviceRecoveryRegistry.java
--- start/src/main/java/com/example/ahakey/service/connection/DeviceRecoveryRegistry.java
+++ current/src/main/java/com/example/ahakey/service/connection/DeviceRecoveryRegistry.java
@@ -0,0 +1,41 @@
+package com.example.ahakey.service.connection;
+
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.type.TypeReference;
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
+import java.io.IOException;
+import java.nio.file.*;
+import java.util.*;
+
+/** Durable uncertainty, keyed by exact transport identity. Restarting Studio is not device recovery. */
+public final class DeviceRecoveryRegistry {
+    private static final ObjectMapper JSON=new ObjectMapper();
+    private final Path file;
+    private final Map<String,Boolean> targets=new HashMap<>();
+    private boolean unreadable;
+    public DeviceRecoveryRegistry(Path file){
+        this.file=file;
+        try{if(Files.exists(file))targets.putAll(JSON.readValue(Files.readAllBytes(file),new TypeReference<Map<String,Boolean>>(){}));}
+        catch(Exception failure){unreadable=true;}
+    }
+    public synchronized boolean contains(String target){return unreadable||targets.containsKey(target);}
+    public synchronized boolean absenceObserved(String target){return !unreadable&&Boolean.TRUE.equals(targets.get(target));}
+    public synchronized void require(String target)throws IOException{
+        if(target==null||target.isBlank())return;targets.put(target,false);save();
+    }
+    public synchronized void observe(String transport,Set<String> present)throws IOException{
+        boolean changed=false;
+        for(String key:targets.keySet())if(key.startsWith(transport+":")&&!present.contains(key.substring(transport.length()+1))&&!targets.get(key)){targets.put(key,true);changed=true;}
+        if(changed)save();
+    }
+    public synchronized void confirmed(String target)throws IOException{
+        if(!absenceObserved(target))throw new IOException("尚未观察到该键盘真实离线");
+        Boolean previous=targets.remove(target);try{save();}catch(IOException failure){targets.put(target,previous);throw failure;}
+    }
+    private void save()throws IOException{
+        if(unreadable)throw new IOException("设备恢复记录不可读，写入保持禁用");
+        Files.createDirectories(file.toAbsolutePath().getParent());
+        Path next=Files.createTempFile(file.toAbsolutePath().getParent(),"recovery-",".tmp");
+        try{Files.write(next,JSON.writeValueAsBytes(targets));Files.move(next,file,StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE,StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);}
+        finally{Files.deleteIfExists(next);}
+    }
+}
