+package com.example.ahakey.service.connection;
+
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.node.ObjectNode;
+import java.io.*;
+import java.net.*;
+import java.nio.file.*;
+import java.security.MessageDigest;
+import java.util.*;
+import java.util.concurrent.*;
+import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;
+import java.util.function.Consumer;
+
+/** Versioned, private loopback IPC. No device selection/reconnection is implicit in startup. */
+public final class ManagedBridgeClient implements AutoCloseable {
+    private static final ObjectMapper JSON = new ObjectMapper();
+    private static final class Connection {
+        final Socket socket;
+        Process owner;
+        final ConcurrentMap<String, CompletableFuture<JsonNode>> pending = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
+        final ExecutorService writes = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor(r -> daemon(r, "bridge-writes"));
+        volatile String instance = "";
+        long eventSequence;
+        Connection(Socket socket) { this.socket = socket; }
+        void close() {
+            try { socket.close(); } catch (IOException ignored) { }
+            pending.values().forEach(f -> f.completeExceptionally(new IOException("桥连接中断")));
+            writes.shutdownNow();
+        }
+    }
+    private static Thread daemon(Runnable r, String name) { Thread t = new Thread(r, name); t.setDaemon(true); return t; }
+    private final AtomicLong sequence = new AtomicLong();
+    private final ExecutorService events = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor(r -> daemon(r, "bridge-events"));
+    private volatile Connection connection;
+    private volatile Process ownedProcess;
+    private volatile boolean closed;
+    private volatile Consumer<JsonNode> eventHandler = event -> {};
+    public void onEvent(Consumer<JsonNode> handler) { eventHandler = Objects.requireNonNull(handler); }
+    public boolean running() { Connection c = connection; return !closed && c != null && !c.socket.isClosed() && !c.instance.isEmpty(); }
+    public static Path applicationDirectory() throws Exception {
+        Path path = Path.of(ManagedBridgeClient.class.getProtectionDomain().getCodeSource().getLocation().toURI());
+        return Files.isRegularFile(path) ? path.getParent() : Path.of(System.getProperty("user.dir"));
+    }
+    public synchronized void start() throws Exception {
+        if (closed) throw new IOException("桥已关闭");
+        if (running()) return;
+        stopOwnedProcess(ownedProcess);
+        if (ownedProcess != null && ownedProcess.isAlive()) throw new IOException("旧桥进程尚未退出");
+        Path root = applicationDirectory();
+        Path executable = root.resolve("BLE_tcp_driver.exe");
+        Path manifest = root.resolve("bridge-managed.json");
+        if (!Files.isRegularFile(executable) || !Files.isRegularFile(manifest)) throw new IOException("随包蓝牙桥版本不匹配，请使用配套安装包。");
+        JsonNode identity = JSON.readTree(Files.readAllBytes(manifest));
+        String hash = HexFormat.of().formatHex(MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(Files.readAllBytes(executable)));
+        if (identity.path("protocolVersion").asInt() != 1 || !hash.equals(identity.path("sha256").asText()))
+            throw new IOException("蓝牙桥身份校验失败，未启动旧桥。");
+        int port;
+        try (ServerSocket reservation = new ServerSocket(0, 1, InetAddress.getLoopbackAddress())) { port = reservation.getLocalPort(); }
+        String token = UUID.randomUUID() + "-" + UUID.randomUUID();
+        ProcessBuilder builder = new ProcessBuilder(executable.toString(), "--studio", "--port", Integer.toString(port));
+        builder.directory(root.toFile());
+        builder.environment().put("AHAKEY_BRIDGE_TOKEN", token);
+        builder.redirectOutput(ProcessBuilder.Redirect.DISCARD); builder.redirectError(ProcessBuilder.Redirect.DISCARD);
+        Process launched = builder.start();
+        ownedProcess = launched;
+        Socket opened = null;
+        try {
+            long deadline = System.nanoTime() + TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(5);
+            while (!closed && opened == null && System.nanoTime() < deadline && launched.isAlive()) {
+                Socket attempt = new Socket();
+                try { attempt.connect(new InetSocketAddress("127.0.0.1", port), 200); opened = attempt; }
+                catch (IOException failure) { attempt.close(); Thread.sleep(50); }
+            }
+            if (closed || opened == null) throw new IOException("蓝牙桥启动超时或端口被占用。");
+            attach(opened);
+            ObjectNode hello = fields(); hello.put("ownerToken", token); hello.putArray("versions").add(1);
+            JsonNode reply = request("hello", hello, 2000);
+            if (reply.path("version").asInt() != 1 || reply.path("bridgeInstanceId").asText().isBlank()
+                    || !reply.path("capabilities").toString().contains("session-writes"))
+                throw new IOException("桥不支持当前连接协议。");
+            connection.instance = reply.path("bridgeInstanceId").asText();
+        } catch (Exception failure) {
+            if (opened != null) try { opened.close(); } catch (IOException ignored) { }
+            stopOwnedProcess(launched);
+            throw failure;
+        }
+    }
+    // Package seam for loopback tests; production calls only after verifying the bundled executable.
+    void attach(Socket opened) throws IOException {
+        if (closed) { opened.close(); throw new IOException("桥已关闭"); }
+        Connection old = connection;
+        if (old != null) old.close();
+        opened.setTcpNoDelay(true);
+        Connection next = new Connection(opened); next.owner=ownedProcess; connection = next;
+        daemon(() -> read(next), "managed-bridge-reader").start();
+    }
+    public JsonNode request(String op, ObjectNode fields, long timeoutMillis) throws Exception {
+        return request(connection, op, fields, timeoutMillis);
+    }
+    public Object ownerIdentity() { return connection; }
+    public void showWindow() throws Exception { start(); request("showWindow", 2000); }
+    public boolean ownerRetired(Object identity) {
+        Connection captured=(Connection)identity;
+        return captured==null || (captured.socket.isClosed() && (captured.owner==null || !captured.owner.isAlive()));
+    }
+    /** A failed release retires only the captured owned bridge, never a replacement. */
+    public void releaseSession(Object identity, ObjectNode fields) throws Exception {
+        Connection captured=(Connection)identity;
+        if(captured==null)return;
+        try { request(captured,"disconnect",fields,2000); }
+        catch(Exception failure) {
+            captured.close();stopOwnedProcess(captured.owner);
+            if(captured.owner!=null && captured.owner.isAlive())throw new IOException("旧桥会话尚未确认撤销",failure);
+        }
+    }
+    private JsonNode request(Connection captured, String op, ObjectNode fields, long timeoutMillis) throws Exception {
+        long deadline = System.nanoTime() + TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS.toNanos(timeoutMillis);
+        if (closed || captured == null || captured.socket.isClosed()) throw new IOException("蓝牙桥未连接");
+        String id = Long.toString(sequence.incrementAndGet());
+        ObjectNode request = fields.deepCopy();
+        request.put("version", 1).put("requestId", id).put("bridgeInstanceId", captured.instance).put("op", op);
+        byte[] body = JSON.writeValueAsBytes(request);
+        if (body.length > 16384) throw new IOException("桥请求过长");
+        CompletableFuture<JsonNode> result = new CompletableFuture<>(); captured.pending.put(id, result);
+        try {
+            captured.writes.execute(() -> {
+                try {
+                    if (captured != connection || captured.socket.isClosed() || System.nanoTime() >= deadline)
+                        throw new IOException("桥连接已变化或发送超时");
+                    OutputStream out = captured.socket.getOutputStream();
+                    out.write(0x10); out.write(body.length & 255); out.write(body.length >>> 8); out.write(body); out.flush();
+                } catch (Exception failure) { result.completeExceptionally(failure); }
+            });
+            long remaining = deadline - System.nanoTime();
+            if (remaining <= 0) throw new TimeoutException();
+            JsonNode reply = result.get(remaining, TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS);
+            if (!reply.path("ok").asBoolean()) throw new IOException("蓝牙桥：" + reply.path("code").asText("请求失败"));
+            return reply;
+        } catch (TimeoutException | InterruptedException failure) {
+            // Covers queueing and blocked writes as well as the response. Closing unblocks the writer.
+            captured.close();
+            if (failure instanceof InterruptedException) Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
+            throw new IOException("蓝牙桥请求已中止，连接已撤销：" + op, failure);
+        } finally { captured.pending.remove(id); }
+    }
+    public JsonNode request(String op, long timeoutMillis) throws Exception { return request(op, fields(), timeoutMillis); }
+    public static ObjectNode fields() { return JSON.createObjectNode(); }
+    private void read(Connection captured) {
+        try {
+            DataInputStream in = new DataInputStream(captured.socket.getInputStream());
+            while (!captured.socket.isClosed()) {
+                int type = in.readUnsignedByte(), length = in.readUnsignedByte() | (in.readUnsignedByte() << 8);
+                if (length < 2 || length > 16384 || (type != 0x90 && type != 0x91)) throw new IOException("无效桥封包");
+                byte[] bytes = new byte[length]; in.readFully(bytes);
+                JsonNode value = JSON.readTree(bytes);
+                if (value.path("version").asInt() != 1) throw new IOException("桥版本变化");
+                if (!captured.instance.isEmpty() && !captured.instance.equals(value.path("bridgeInstanceId").asText())) throw new IOException("桥实例变化");
+                if (type == 0x90) {
+                    var future = captured.pending.get(value.path("requestId").asText());
+                    if (future != null) future.complete(value);
+                } else {
+                    long next = value.path("eventSeq").asLong();
+                    if (next <= captured.eventSequence) continue;
+                    captured.eventSequence = next;
+                    dispatch(captured, value);
+                }
+            }
+        } catch (Exception failure) {
+            captured.close();
+            dispatch(captured, fields().put("event", "bridgeDisconnected").put("bridgeInstanceId", captured.instance));
+        }
+    }
+    private void dispatch(Connection captured, JsonNode event) {
+        try { events.execute(() -> { if (connection == captured && !closed) eventHandler.accept(event); }); }
+        catch (RejectedExecutionException ignored) { }
+    }
+    static void stopOwnedProcess(Process process) {
+        if (process == null || !process.isAlive()) return;
+        try {
+            if (process.waitFor(200, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)) return;
+            process.destroy();
+            if (!process.waitFor(200, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)) {
+                process.destroyForcibly(); process.waitFor(600, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
+            }
+        } catch (InterruptedException e) { process.destroyForcibly(); Thread.currentThread().interrupt(); }
+    }
+    public synchronized void restart() throws Exception {
+        Connection previous=connection;connection=null;
+        if(previous!=null)previous.close();stopOwnedProcess(ownedProcess);start();
+    }
+    public synchronized void stop() {
+        Connection previous=connection;connection=null;
+        if(previous!=null)previous.close();stopOwnedProcess(ownedProcess);
+    }
+    public void close() {
+        closed = true;
+        Connection captured = connection;
+        if (captured != null) captured.close();
+        events.shutdownNow();
+        stopOwnedProcess(ownedProcess);
+    }
+}

@@ -0,0 +1,27 @@
+package com.example.ahakey.service.connection;
+
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
+import java.io.IOException;
+import java.nio.file.*;
+
+/** A startup hint, never device identity, capability evidence, or an upload checkpoint. */
+public record RememberedBleTarget(String name, String address) {
+    private static Path path() { return Path.of(System.getProperty("user.home"), ".ahakey", "last-ble-target.json"); }
+    public static RememberedBleTarget load() {
+        try {
+            var node = new ObjectMapper().readTree(Files.readAllBytes(path()));
+            return new RememberedBleTarget(node.path("name").asText(), node.path("address").asText());
+        } catch (IOException | RuntimeException e) { return new RememberedBleTarget("", ""); }
+    }
+    public void save() throws IOException {
+        if (address == null || address.isBlank()) return;
+        Path target = path(); Files.createDirectories(target.getParent());
+        Path temporary = Files.createTempFile(target.getParent(), "ble-target-", ".tmp");
+        try {
+            new ObjectMapper().writeValue(temporary.toFile(), this);
+            try { Files.move(temporary, target, StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING); }
+            catch (AtomicMoveNotSupportedException e) { Files.move(temporary, target, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING); }
+        } finally { Files.deleteIfExists(temporary); }
+    }
+    public boolean matches(String candidateAddress) { return !address.isBlank() && address.equalsIgnoreCase(candidateAddress); }
+}

-        HookInstaller.HookStatus status,
-        Button action,

-    private void loadSleepWaitTime(ComboBox<SleepWaitTimeOption> options, Label status, BooleanProperty busy, AtomicInteger readSerialSource) {
-        if (!controller.isEffectivelyConnected()) {
-            status.setText(languageManager.getString("sleep-wait-time.not-connected"));
-            return;
-        }
-        int readSerial = readSerialSource.incrementAndGet();
-        logger.info("Sleep wait time UI read started: serial={}", readSerial);
-        busy.set(true);
-        status.setText(languageManager.getString("sleep-wait-time.reading"));
-        Thread timeoutThread = new Thread(() -> {
-            try {
-                Thread.sleep(10_000);
-            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
-                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
-                return;
-            }
-            Platform.runLater(() -> {
-                if (readSerialSource.get() == readSerial && busy.get()) {
-                    readSerialSource.incrementAndGet();
-                    logger.warn("Sleep wait time UI read timed out: serial={}", readSerial);
-                    options.getSelectionModel().clearSelection();
-                    status.setText(languageManager.getString("sleep-wait-time.read-timeout"));
-                    busy.set(false);
-                }
-            });
-        }, "sleep-wait-time-read-timeout");
-        timeoutThread.setDaemon(true);
-        timeoutThread.start();
-
-        Thread thread = new Thread(() -> {
-            try {
-                int minutes = controller.getBleManager().readSleepWaitTimeMinutes();
-                Platform.runLater(() -> {
-                    if (readSerialSource.get() != readSerial) {
-                        logger.info("Sleep wait time UI update ignored for stale read: serial={}, current={}", readSerial, readSerialSource.get());
-                        return;
-                    }
-                    try {
-                        SleepWaitTimeOption option = findSleepWaitTimeOption(options, minutes);
-                        if (option == null) {
-                            options.getSelectionModel().clearSelection();
-                            status.setText(languageManager.getString("sleep-wait-time.invalid-current"));
-                            logger.warn("Sleep wait time UI read returned unsupported value: serial={}, minutes={}", readSerial, minutes);
-                        } else {
-                            options.getSelectionModel().select(option);
-                            status.setText(formatSleepWaitTimeStatus("sleep-wait-time.current", option.label()));
-                            logger.info("Sleep wait time UI updated: serial={}, minutes={}, label={}", readSerial, minutes, option.label());
-                        }
-                    } finally {
-                        busy.set(false);
-                    }
-                });
-            } catch (Exception e) {
-                Platform.runLater(() -> {
-                    if (readSerialSource.get() != readSerial) {
-                        logger.info("Sleep wait time UI failure ignored for stale read: serial={}, current={}", readSerial, readSerialSource.get());
-                        return;
-                    }
-                    status.setText(languageManager.getString("sleep-wait-time.read-failed") + e.getMessage());
-                    logger.warn("Sleep wait time UI read failed: serial={}", readSerial, e);
-                    busy.set(false);
-                });
-            }
-        }, "sleep-wait-time-read");
-        thread.setDaemon(true);
-        thread.start();
-    }

+    public TopBar(StudioController controller, DeviceStatus deviceStatus,
+                  StudioState studioState, AgentManager agentManager) {
+        this.controller = controller;
+        this.deviceStatus = deviceStatus;
+        this.studioState = studioState;
+        this.agentManager = agentManager;
+        this.hookInstaller = new HookInstaller(
+            () -> controller.getHookDispatchPort(), this::addLog);
+        this.controller.setHookManagerOpener(this::showHookManagerDialog);
+        this.languageManager = LanguageManager.getInstance();
+        setSpacing(0);
+        setPadding(new Insets(0));
+        getStyleClass().add("top-bar");
+        initContent();
+        setupLanguageChangeListener();
+    }

+    private void initContent() {

+                Bindings.createStringBinding(deviceStatus::getSwitchTitle, deviceStatus.switchStateProperty(),deviceStatus.savedBindingsProperty(),deviceStatus.isConnectedProperty()),

+        ahaTypeToggle.getStyleClass().add("toggle-button");
+        ahaTypeToggle.textProperty().bind(Bindings.createStringBinding(
+            () -> studioState.ahaTypeEnabledProperty().get() ? "AhaType" : "AhaType",
+            studioState.ahaTypeEnabledProperty()
+        ));
+        ahaTypeToggle.selectedProperty().bindBidirectional(studioState.ahaTypeEnabledProperty());
+        ahaTypeToggle.selectedProperty().addListener((obs, oldValue, newValue) -> studioState.toggleAhaType(newValue));

+

+                agentManager.bluetoothOwnerProperty()

+            Bindings.createStringBinding(
+                () -> agentManager.isEditingConfiguration()
+                    ? languageManager.getString("config.status.editing-detail")
+                    : languageManager.getString("config.status.keyboard-detail"),

+            )

+
+        Button configModeButton = new Button();
+        configModeButton.getStyleClass().add("button-prominent");
+        configModeButton.textProperty().bind(Bindings.createStringBinding(controller::configurationModeButtonTitle,

+        configModeButton.setOnAction(event -> controller.handleConfigurationModeButton());

+    
+    /**
+     * 处理 BLE 驱动按钮点击
+     * - 如果 BLE_tcp_driver.exe 已运行，弹窗提示
+     * - 否则启动同级目录下的 BLE_tcp_driver.exe
+     */
+    private volatile boolean bleButtonProcessing = false;
+    private volatile long lastBleButtonClickTime = 0;
+    private static final long BLE_BUTTON_CLICK_DELAY_MS = 2000;
+    
+    private void handleBleButtonClick() { manageOwnedBridge(false); }
+    private void manageOwnedBridge(boolean stopOnly) {
+        if(bleButtonProcessing || !controller.normalDeviceActionAllowed())return;
+        bleButtonProcessing=true;
+        Thread worker=new Thread(()->{
+            try {controller.getBleManager().restartManagedBridge(stopOnly);Platform.runLater(()->showAlert("蓝牙桥",stopOnly?"已停止本软件持有的蓝牙桥。":"蓝牙桥已就绪，请点击连接选择键盘。"));}
+            catch(Exception failure){Platform.runLater(()->showAlert("蓝牙桥",failure.getMessage()));}
+            finally{bleButtonProcessing=false;}
+        },"bridge-owner");worker.setDaemon(true);worker.start();
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * 设置 BLE 按钮长按功能
+     * 长按5秒：停止本软件持有的蓝牙桥
+     * 短按：正常的 BLE 驱动启动/重启逻辑
+     */
+    private void setupBleButtonLongPress(Button bleButton) {
+        bleButton.setOnMousePressed(event -> {
+            bleButtonPressed = true;
+            bleLongPressTriggered = false;
+            
+            bleLongPressThread = new Thread(() -> {
+                try {
+                    Thread.sleep(5000);
+                    if (bleButtonPressed && !bleLongPressTriggered) {
+                        bleLongPressTriggered = true;
+                        Platform.runLater(() -> {
+                            forceKillAllBleProcesses();
+                        });
+                    }
+                } catch (InterruptedException e) {
+                    Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
+                }
+            }, "ble-longpress");
+            bleLongPressThread.start();
+        });
+        
+        bleButton.setOnMouseReleased(event -> {
+            bleButtonPressed = false;
+            if (bleLongPressThread != null) {
+                bleLongPressThread.interrupt();
+                bleLongPressThread = null;
+            }
+            
+            if (!bleLongPressTriggered) {
+                handleBleButtonClick();
+            }
+        });
+        
+        bleButton.setOnMouseExited(event -> {
+            if (bleButtonPressed) {
+                bleButtonPressed = false;
+                if (bleLongPressThread != null) {
+                    bleLongPressThread.interrupt();
+                    bleLongPressThread = null;
+                }
+            }
+        });
+    }
+    
+    /**
+     * 停止本软件持有的蓝牙桥
+     */
+    private void forceKillAllBleProcesses() { manageOwnedBridge(true); }
+
+    private void showAlert(String title, String content) {
+        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING);
+        alert.setTitle(title);
+        alert.setHeaderText(null);
+        alert.setContentText(content);
+        alert.showAndWait();
+    }
+    
+    /**
+     * 切换语音服务状态（启动/停止）
+     */
+    private void toggleVoiceService() {
+        if (voiceInputManager == null) {
+            setVoiceStatus("error", languageManager.getString("voice.status.voice-service-unavailable"));
+            return;
+        }
+        
+        if (voiceRunning) {
+            stopVoiceService();
+        } else {
+            startVoiceService();
+        }
+    }
+    
+    /**
+     * 启动语音服务
+     */
+    private void startVoiceService() {
+        voiceRunning = true;
+        updateVoiceButtonState();
+        setVoiceStatus("starting", languageManager.getString("voice.status.starting"));
+        
+        // 创建浮动通知窗口
+        if (floatingNotification == null) {
+            floatingNotification = new FloatingVoiceNotification();
+        }
+        
+        // 设置状态回调（同时更新UI和浮动通知）
+        voiceInputManager.setStatusCallback(status -> {
+            // 状态格式: "code:message"
+            String[] parts = status.split(":", 2);
+            String code = parts[0];
+            String message = parts.length > 1 ? parts[1] : code;
+            
+            Platform.runLater(() -> {
+                // 更新 TopBar 状态
+                setVoiceStatus(code, message);
+                
+                // 更新浮动通知
+                if (floatingNotification != null) {
+                    floatingNotification.updateStatus(code, message);
+                }
+            });
+        });
+        
+        // 启动语音输入管理器
+        voiceInputManager.startVoiceInput(result -> {
+            Platform.runLater(() -> {
+                voiceResultPreview.setText(result);
+            });
+        }, partialResult -> {
+            Platform.runLater(() -> {
+                voiceResultPreview.setText(partialResult);
+            });
+        });
+    }
+    
+    /**
+     * 停止语音服务
+     */
+    private void stopVoiceService() {
+        voiceRunning = false;
+        updateVoiceButtonState();
+        setVoiceStatus("stopping", languageManager.getString("voice.status.stopping"));
+        
+        // 关闭浮动通知窗口
+        if (floatingNotification != null) {
+            floatingNotification.close();
+            floatingNotification = null;
+        }
+        
+        if (voiceInputManager != null) {
+            voiceInputManager.stopVoiceInput();
+        }
+        
+        // 延迟更新状态
+        new Thread(() -> {
+            try {
+                Thread.sleep(500);
+                Platform.runLater(() -> {
+                    setVoiceStatus("stopped", languageManager.getString("voice.status.stopped"));
+                    voiceResultPreview.setText("");
+                });
+            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
+                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
+            }
+        }).start();
+    }
+    
+    /**
+     * 设置语音状态
+     */
+    private void setVoiceStatus(String status, String text) {
+        if (voiceStatusLamp != null) {
+            voiceStatusLamp.setStatus(status);
+        }
+        
+        if (voiceStatusLabel != null) {
+            voiceStatusLabel.setText(text);
+            
+            // 根据状态设置颜色
+            String color = switch (status) {
+                case "stopped", "idle" -> "#A7AFBA";           // 空闲状态 - 灰色
+                case "starting", "stopping", "processing", "recognizing" -> "#F5A623";  // 处理/识别中 - 橙色
+                case "recording" -> "#E74C3C";                 // 录音中 - 红色
+                case "ready" -> "#2ECC71";                     // 就绪 - 绿色
+                default -> "#E74C3C"; // error
+            };
+            voiceStatusLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: " + color + ";");
+        }
+    }
+    
+    /**
+     * 更新语音按钮状态
+     */
+    private void updateVoiceButtonState() {
+        if (voiceRecordButton == null) return;
+        
+        if (voiceInputManager == null || !voiceInputManager.isEnabled()) {
+            voiceRecordButton.setDisable(true);
+            voiceRecordButton.setText(languageManager.getString("button.voice-unavailable"));
+            setVoiceStatus("error", languageManager.getString("voice.status.voice-service-unavailable"));
+            return;
+        }
+        
+        voiceRecordButton.setDisable(false);
+        
+        if (voiceRunning) {
+            voiceRecordButton.getStyleClass().add("voice-recording");
+            voiceRecordButton.setText(languageManager.getString("button.stop-voice"));
+        } else {
+            voiceRecordButton.getStyleClass().remove("voice-recording");
+            voiceRecordButton.setText(languageManager.getString("button.start-voice"));
+        }
+    }
+
+    private VBox createStatusBox(
+        javafx.beans.value.ObservableValue<Boolean> isPositive,
+        javafx.beans.value.ObservableValue<String> title,
+        javafx.beans.value.ObservableValue<String> detail
+    ) {
+        VBox box = new VBox(1);
+        box.getStyleClass().add("status-box");
+
+        HBox row = new HBox(8);
+        Label dot = new Label();
+        dot.getStyleClass().add("status-dot");
+        dot.styleProperty().bind(Bindings.createStringBinding(
+            () -> "-fx-background-color: " + (isPositive.getValue() ? "#30d158" : "#0a84ff") + ";",
+            isPositive
+        ));
+
+        VBox text = new VBox(1);
+        Label titleLabel = new Label();
+        titleLabel.textProperty().bind(title);
+        titleLabel.getStyleClass().add("status-label");
+
+        Label detailLabel = new Label();
+        detailLabel.textProperty().bind(detail);
+        detailLabel.getStyleClass().add("status-detail");
+
+        text.getChildren().addAll(titleLabel, detailLabel);
+        row.getChildren().addAll(dot, text);
+        box.getChildren().add(row);
+        return box;
+    }
+
+    private void showDeviceInfoDialog() {
+        Stage dialog = new Stage();
+        dialog.initOwner(getScene().getWindow());
+        dialog.setTitle("设备管理");
+        dialog.setWidth(550);
+        dialog.setHeight(430);
+
+        ScrollPane scrollPane = new ScrollPane();
+        scrollPane.setFitToWidth(true);
+
+        VBox content = new VBox(12);
+        content.setPadding(new Insets(12));
+
+        VBox deviceCard = new VBox(8);
+        deviceCard.getStyleClass().add("dialog-card");
+        deviceCard.setPadding(new Insets(12));
+
+        Label deviceTitle = new Label(languageManager.getString("dialog.device-info"));
+        deviceTitle.getStyleClass().add("dialog-card-title");
+
+        HBox deviceRow1 = new HBox(16);
+        Label connStatus = new Label();
+        connStatus.getStyleClass().add("dialog-text");
+        connStatus.textProperty().bind(Bindings.createStringBinding(() ->
+            languageManager.getString("dialog.connection") + ": " + (this.deviceStatus.isConnected()
+                ? languageManager.getString("status.connected")
+                : languageManager.getString("status.disconnected")),
+            this.deviceStatus.isConnectedProperty()
+        ));
+        Label batteryStatus = new Label();
+        batteryStatus.getStyleClass().add("dialog-text");
+        batteryStatus.textProperty().bind(Bindings.createStringBinding(() ->
+            languageManager.getString("status.battery") + ": " + this.deviceStatus.getBatteryLevel() + "%",
+            this.deviceStatus.batteryLevelProperty()
+        ));
+        deviceRow1.getChildren().addAll(connStatus, batteryStatus);
+
+        HBox deviceRow2 = new HBox(16);
+        Label deviceName = new Label(languageManager.getString("dialog.device-name") + ": " + this.deviceStatus.getDisplayDeviceName());
+        deviceName.getStyleClass().add("dialog-text");
+        Label switchState = new Label();
+        switchState.getStyleClass().add("dialog-text");
+        switchState.textProperty().bind(Bindings.createStringBinding(() ->
+            languageManager.getString("status.switch") + ": " + this.deviceStatus.getSwitchTitle(),
+            this.deviceStatus.switchStateProperty()
+        ));
+        deviceRow2.getChildren().addAll(deviceName, switchState);
+
+        HBox versionRow = new HBox(16);
+        Label appVersion = new Label("上位机版本: " + getVersion());
+        appVersion.getStyleClass().add("dialog-text");
+        Label keyboardFirmware = new Label();
+        keyboardFirmware.getStyleClass().add("dialog-text");
+        keyboardFirmware.textProperty().bind(Bindings.createStringBinding(() ->
+            "键盘固件版本: " + this.deviceStatus.getDisplayFirmwareVersion(),
+            this.deviceStatus.firmwareCatalogKeyProperty(),
+            this.deviceStatus.firmwareMainProperty(),
+            this.deviceStatus.firmwareSubProperty()
+        ));
+        versionRow.getChildren().addAll(appVersion, keyboardFirmware);
+
+        SleepWaitTimeControl sleepWaitTimeControl = createSleepWaitTimeControl();
+        Button refreshDeviceInfoButton = new Button(languageManager.getString("dialog.refresh-device-info"));
+        BooleanProperty deviceInfoRefreshBusy = new SimpleBooleanProperty(false);
+        refreshDeviceInfoButton.setGraphic(Icons.refresh("14"));
+        refreshDeviceInfoButton.setTooltip(new Tooltip(languageManager.getString("dialog.refresh-device-info")));
+        refreshDeviceInfoButton.disableProperty().bind(Bindings.createBooleanBinding(
+            () -> !controller.isEffectivelyConnected() || deviceInfoRefreshBusy.get(),
+            this.deviceStatus.isConnectedProperty(),
+            deviceInfoRefreshBusy
+        ));
+        refreshDeviceInfoButton.setOnAction(event -> {
+            if (deviceInfoRefreshBusy.get()) {
+                return;
+            }
+            deviceInfoRefreshBusy.set(true);
+            controller.clearDeviceInfoDetailsForRefresh();
+            Thread thread = new Thread(() -> {
+                try {
+                    controller.readFirmwareCatalogKeyForDeviceInfo();
+                    controller.refreshDeviceStatusSnapshot();
+                    Platform.runLater(sleepWaitTimeControl::refresh);
+                } finally {
+                    Platform.runLater(() -> deviceInfoRefreshBusy.set(false));
+                }
+            }, "device-info-manual-refresh");
+            thread.setDaemon(true);
+            thread.start();
+        });
+
+        HBox deviceHeader = new HBox(10);
+        deviceHeader.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
+        Region deviceHeaderSpacer = new Region();
+        HBox.setHgrow(deviceHeaderSpacer, Priority.ALWAYS);
+        deviceHeader.getChildren().addAll(deviceTitle, deviceHeaderSpacer, refreshDeviceInfoButton);
+
+        deviceCard.getChildren().addAll(deviceHeader, deviceRow1, deviceRow2, versionRow, sleepWaitTimeControl.node());
+
+        HBox actionButtons = new HBox(10);
+        actionButtons.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_RIGHT);
+        Button connectBtn = new Button(languageManager.getString("button.connect"));
+        connectBtn.getStyleClass().add("button-connect");
+        connectBtn.disableProperty().bind(this.deviceStatus.isConnectedProperty());
+        connectBtn.setOnAction(event -> controller.userConnect());
+
+        Button disconnectBtn = new Button(languageManager.getString("button.disconnect"));
+        disconnectBtn.getStyleClass().add("button-disconnect");
+        disconnectBtn.disableProperty().bind(this.deviceStatus.isConnectedProperty().not());
+        disconnectBtn.setOnAction(event -> controller.userDisconnect());
+
+        Button closeBtn = new Button(languageManager.getString("dialog.close"));
+        closeBtn.setOnAction(event -> dialog.close());
+        actionButtons.getChildren().addAll(connectBtn, disconnectBtn, closeBtn);
+
+        content.getChildren().addAll(deviceCard, actionButtons);
+        scrollPane.setContent(content);
+
+        Scene scene = new Scene(scrollPane);
+        scene.getStylesheets().add(getClass().getResource("/style.css").toExternalForm());
+        dialog.setScene(scene);
+        dialog.show();
+    }
+
+    private void showHookManagerDialog() {
+        Stage dialog = new Stage();
+        dialog.initOwner(getScene().getWindow());
+        dialog.setTitle("AhaKey Hook 管理");
+        dialog.setWidth(560);
+        dialog.setHeight(620);
+
+        ScrollPane scrollPane = new ScrollPane();
+        scrollPane.setFitToWidth(true);
+
+        VBox content = new VBox(12);
+        content.setPadding(new Insets(12));
+        Map<String, VBox> cards = new LinkedHashMap<>();
+        Map<String, HookInstaller.HookStatus> statuses = new LinkedHashMap<>();
+        Map<String, Button> actionButtons = new LinkedHashMap<>();
+        BooleanProperty hookDialogBusy = new SimpleBooleanProperty(false);
+        VBox list = new VBox(10);
+        for (String name : List.of("Claude", "Cursor", "Codex", "Kimi")) {
+            VBox card = createHookManagerCard(
+                name,
+                HookInstaller.HookStatus.DETECTING,
+                statuses,
+                actionButtons,
+                hookDialogBusy,
+                null
+            );
+            cards.put(name, card);
+            list.getChildren().add(card);
+        }
+
+        Button refresh = new Button("重新检测");
+        refresh.setGraphic(Icons.refresh("14"));
+        refresh.disableProperty().bind(hookDialogBusy);
+        Runnable[] rescan = new Runnable[1];
+        rescan[0] = () -> {
+            hookDialogBusy.set(true);
+            actionButtons.clear();
+            for (Map.Entry<String, VBox> entry : cards.entrySet()) {
+                updateHookManagerCard(
+                    entry.getValue(),
+                    entry.getKey(),
+                    HookInstaller.HookStatus.DETECTING,
+                    statuses,
+                    actionButtons,
+                    hookDialogBusy,
+                    rescan[0]
+                );
+            }
+            Thread thread = new Thread(() -> {
+                Map<String, HookInstaller.HookStatus> scanned = new LinkedHashMap<>();
+                for (String name : cards.keySet()) {
+                    scanned.put(name, hookInstaller.getHookStatus(name));
+                }
+                Platform.runLater(() -> {
+                    statuses.clear();
+                    statuses.putAll(scanned);
+                    actionButtons.clear();
+                    for (Map.Entry<String, HookInstaller.HookStatus> entry : scanned.entrySet()) {
+                        updateHookManagerCard(
+                            cards.get(entry.getKey()),
+                            entry.getKey(),
+                            entry.getValue(),
+                            statuses,
+                            actionButtons,
+                            hookDialogBusy,
+                            rescan[0]
+                        );
+                    }
+                    hookDialogBusy.set(false);
+                });
+            }, "hook-manager-scan");
+            thread.setDaemon(true);
+            thread.start();
+        };
+        refresh.setOnAction(event -> rescan[0].run());
+
+        Label note1 = hookNote("安装或更新时，AhaKey 只会添加/替换自己的 Hook，不会删除你原本配置的其他 Hook。");
+        Label note2 = hookNote("当前多任务灯光支持 Codex / Cursor。");
+        HBox bottom = new HBox(10);
+        bottom.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_RIGHT);
+        Button closeBtn = new Button("关闭");
+        closeBtn.setOnAction(event -> dialog.close());
+        bottom.getChildren().addAll(refresh, closeBtn);
+
+        content.getChildren().addAll(list, note1, note2, bottom);
+        scrollPane.setContent(content);
+
+        Scene scene = new Scene(scrollPane);
+        scene.getStylesheets().add(getClass().getResource("/style.css").toExternalForm());
+        dialog.setScene(scene);
+        dialog.show();
+        rescan[0].run();
+    }
+
+    private Label hookNote(String text) {
+        Label label = new Label(text);
+        label.getStyleClass().add("group-note");
+        label.setWrapText(true);
+        return label;
+    }
+
+    private VBox createHookManagerCard(
+        String hookName,
+        HookInstaller.HookStatus status,
+        Map<String, HookInstaller.HookStatus> statuses,
+        Map<String, Button> actionButtons,
+        BooleanProperty hookDialogBusy,
+        Runnable rescan
+    ) {
+        VBox card = new VBox(8);
+        card.getStyleClass().add("dialog-card");
+        card.setPadding(new Insets(12));
+        updateHookManagerCard(card, hookName, status, statuses, actionButtons, hookDialogBusy, rescan);
+        return card;
+    }
+
+    private void updateHookManagerCard(
+        VBox card,
+        String hookName,
+        HookInstaller.HookStatus status,
+        Map<String, HookInstaller.HookStatus> statuses,
+        Map<String, Button> actionButtons,
+        BooleanProperty hookDialogBusy,
+        Runnable rescan
+    ) {
+        card.getChildren().clear();
+        statuses.put(hookName, status);
+        if (actionButtons != null) {
+            actionButtons.remove(hookName);
+        }
+
+        HBox titleRow = new HBox(8);
+        titleRow.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
+        Label title = new Label(hookName);
+        title.getStyleClass().add("dialog-card-title");
+        Region spacer = new Region();
+        HBox.setHgrow(spacer, Priority.ALWAYS);
+        Label statusLabel = new Label(hookStatusText(status));
+        statusLabel.getStyleClass().add(status == HookInstaller.HookStatus.LATEST ? "dialog-status-installed" : "dialog-status-uninstalled");
+        titleRow.getChildren().addAll(title, spacer, statusLabel);
+
+        Label description = hookNote(hookInstaller.statusDetail(hookName).isBlank()?hookDescription(status):hookInstaller.statusDetail(hookName));
+        Button action = new Button(hookActionText(status));
+        boolean canWrite = status == HookInstaller.HookStatus.NOT_INSTALLED || status == HookInstaller.HookStatus.OUTDATED;
+        if (hookDialogBusy == null) {
+            action.setDisable(!canWrite);
+        } else {
+            action.disableProperty().bind(Bindings.createBooleanBinding(
+                () -> hookDialogBusy.get() || !canWrite,
+                hookDialogBusy
+            ));
+        }
+        if (actionButtons != null) {
+            actionButtons.put(hookName, action);
+        }
+        if (canWrite) {
+            action.getStyleClass().add("button-install");
+            action.setOnAction(event -> runSingleHookWrite(hookName, status, action, actionButtons, hookDialogBusy, rescan));
+        }
+
+        card.getChildren().addAll(titleRow, description, action);
+        if(hookWriteMessages.containsKey(hookName))card.getChildren().add(hookNote(hookWriteMessages.get(hookName)));
+    }
+
+    private void runSingleHookWrite(
+        String hookName,
+        HookInstaller.HookStatus status,
+        Button action,
+        Map<String, Button> actionButtons,
+        BooleanProperty hookDialogBusy,
+        Runnable rescan
+    ) {
+        if (hookDialogBusy != null && hookDialogBusy.get()) {
+            return;
+        }
+        if (hookDialogBusy != null) {
+            hookDialogBusy.set(true);
+        }
+        if (actionButtons != null) {
+            actionButtons.values().forEach(button -> button.setMouseTransparent(true));
+        }
+        action.setText(status == HookInstaller.HookStatus.NOT_INSTALLED ? "安装中" : "更新中");
+        Thread thread = new Thread(() -> {
+            HookInstaller.HookWriteResult result = hookInstaller.installOrUpdate(hookName);
+            Platform.runLater(() -> {
+                if (actionButtons != null) {
+                    actionButtons.values().forEach(button -> button.setMouseTransparent(false));
+                }
+                hookWriteMessages.put(hookName,(result.success()?"成功：":"未完成：")+result.reason());
+                if(rescan!=null)rescan.run();
+                else if(hookDialogBusy!=null)hookDialogBusy.set(false);
+            });
+        }, "hook-manager-write-" + hookName);
+        thread.setDaemon(true);
+        thread.start();
+    }
+
+    private String hookStatusText(HookInstaller.HookStatus status) {
+        return switch (status) {
+            case DETECTING -> "检测中";
+            case APP_NOT_FOUND -> "未检测到应用";
+            case CONFIG_ONLY -> "仅配置痕迹";
+            case UNSUPPORTED -> "兼容性未确认";
+            case NOT_INSTALLED -> "未安装";
+            case OUTDATED -> "待更新";
+            case LATEST -> "最新";
+            case ERROR -> "异常";
+        };
+    }
+
+    private String hookActionText(HookInstaller.HookStatus status) {
+        return switch (status) {
+            case APP_NOT_FOUND, CONFIG_ONLY, UNSUPPORTED -> "不可安装";
+            case NOT_INSTALLED -> "安装 Hook";
+            case OUTDATED -> "更新 Hook";
+            case LATEST -> "已是最新";
+            case ERROR -> "异常";
+            case DETECTING -> "检测中";
+        };
+    }
+
+    private String hookDescription(HookInstaller.HookStatus status) {
+        return switch (status) {
+            case APP_NOT_FOUND -> "未检测到对应应用，暂不可安装 Hook";
+            case CONFIG_ONLY -> "仅发现配置目录，未检测到应用启动入口";
+            case UNSUPPORTED -> "当前应用版本的 Hook 能力尚未确认，不会写入配置";
+            case NOT_INSTALLED -> "未安装 Hook，安装后可支持 AhaKey 功能";
+            case OUTDATED -> "脚本或注册信息需更新；只替换 AhaKey 自有条目";
+            case LATEST -> "脚本和注册已就绪；客户端需重新加载配置";
+            case ERROR -> "配置检测失败，请检查卡片说明后重新检测";
+            case DETECTING -> "正在检测 Hook 状态";
+        };
+    }
+
+    private SleepWaitTimeControl createSleepWaitTimeControl() {
+        VBox box = new VBox(6);
+
+        Label title = new Label(languageManager.getString("sleep-wait-time.title"));
+        title.getStyleClass().add("dialog-text");
+
+        ComboBox<SleepWaitTimeOption> options = new ComboBox<>();
+        options.getItems().addAll(
+            new SleepWaitTimeOption(0, languageManager.getString("sleep-wait-time.never")),
+            new SleepWaitTimeOption(30, languageManager.getString("sleep-wait-time.30")),
+            new SleepWaitTimeOption(60, languageManager.getString("sleep-wait-time.60")),
+            new SleepWaitTimeOption(120, languageManager.getString("sleep-wait-time.120"))
+        );
+        options.setPrefWidth(140);
+
+        Label status = new Label();
+        status.getStyleClass().add("dialog-text");
+        status.setWrapText(true);
+        AtomicInteger readSerial = new AtomicInteger();
+
+        Label description = new Label(languageManager.getString("sleep-wait-time.description"));
+        description.getStyleClass().add("dialog-text");
+        description.setWrapText(true);
+
+        Button saveButton = new Button(languageManager.getString("sleep-wait-time.save"));
+        BooleanProperty busy = new SimpleBooleanProperty(false);
+        options.disableProperty().bind(Bindings.createBooleanBinding(
+            () -> !controller.isEffectivelyConnected() || busy.get(),
+            this.deviceStatus.isConnectedProperty(),
+            busy
+        ));
+        saveButton.disableProperty().bind(Bindings.createBooleanBinding(
+            () -> !controller.isEffectivelyConnected() || busy.get() || options.getSelectionModel().getSelectedItem() == null,
+            this.deviceStatus.isConnectedProperty(),
+            busy,
+            options.getSelectionModel().selectedItemProperty()
+        ));
+
+        HBox row = new HBox(8);
+        row.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
+        row.getChildren().addAll(title, options, saveButton);
+
+        saveButton.setOnAction(event -> saveSleepWaitTime(options, status, busy));
+        loadSleepWaitTime(options, status, busy, readSerial);
+
+        box.getChildren().addAll(row, description, status);
+        return new SleepWaitTimeControl(box, () -> loadSleepWaitTime(options, status, busy, readSerial));
+    }
+
+    private void loadSleepWaitTime(ComboBox<SleepWaitTimeOption> options, Label status, BooleanProperty busy, AtomicInteger readSerialSource) {
+        if (!controller.isEffectivelyConnected()) {
+            status.setText(languageManager.getString("sleep-wait-time.not-connected"));
+            return;
+        }
+        int readSerial = readSerialSource.incrementAndGet();
+        logger.info("Sleep wait time UI read started: serial={}", readSerial);
+        busy.set(true);
+        status.setText(languageManager.getString("sleep-wait-time.reading"));
+        Thread timeoutThread = new Thread(() -> {
+            try {
+                Thread.sleep(10_000);
+            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
+                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
+                return;
+            }
+            Platform.runLater(() -> {
+                if (readSerialSource.get() == readSerial && busy.get()) {
+                    readSerialSource.incrementAndGet();
+                    logger.warn("Sleep wait time UI read timed out: serial={}", readSerial);
+                    options.getSelectionModel().clearSelection();
+                    status.setText(languageManager.getString("sleep-wait-time.read-timeout"));
+                    busy.set(false);
+                }
+            });
+        }, "sleep-wait-time-read-timeout");
+        timeoutThread.setDaemon(true);
+        timeoutThread.start();
+
+        Thread thread = new Thread(() -> {
+            try {
+                int minutes = controller.getBleManager().readSleepWaitTimeMinutes();
+                Platform.runLater(() -> {
+                    if (readSerialSource.get() != readSerial) {
+                        logger.info("Sleep wait time UI update ignored for stale read: serial={}, current={}", readSerial, readSerialSource.get());
+                        return;
+                    }
+                    try {
+                        SleepWaitTimeOption option = findSleepWaitTimeOption(options, minutes);
+                        if (option == null) {
+                            options.getSelectionModel().clearSelection();
+                            status.setText(languageManager.getString("sleep-wait-time.invalid-current"));
+                            logger.warn("Sleep wait time UI read returned unsupported value: serial={}, minutes={}", readSerial, minutes);
+                        } else {
+                            options.getSelectionModel().select(option);
+                            status.setText(formatSleepWaitTimeStatus("sleep-wait-time.current", option.label()));
+                            logger.info("Sleep wait time UI updated: serial={}, minutes={}, label={}", readSerial, minutes, option.label());
+                        }
+                    } finally {
+                        busy.set(false);
+                    }
+                });
+            } catch (Exception e) {
+                Platform.runLater(() -> {
+                    if (readSerialSource.get() != readSerial) {
+                        logger.info("Sleep wait time UI failure ignored for stale read: serial={}, current={}", readSerial, readSerialSource.get());
+                        return;
+                    }
+                    status.setText(languageManager.getString("sleep-wait-time.read-failed") + e.getMessage());
+                    logger.warn("Sleep wait time UI read failed: serial={}", readSerial, e);
+                    busy.set(false);
+                });
+            }
+        }, "sleep-wait-time-read");
+        thread.setDaemon(true);
+        thread.start();
+    }
+
+    private void saveSleepWaitTime(ComboBox<SleepWaitTimeOption> options, Label status, BooleanProperty busy) {
+        SleepWaitTimeOption selected = options.getSelectionModel().getSelectedItem();
+        if (selected == null) {
+            return;
+        }
+        if (!controller.isEffectivelyConnected()) {
+            status.setText(languageManager.getString("sleep-wait-time.not-connected"));
+            return;
+        }
+        busy.set(true);
+        status.setText(languageManager.getString("sleep-wait-time.saving"));
+        Thread thread = new Thread(() -> {
+            try {
+                int readback = controller.getBleManager().setSleepWaitTimeMinutesAndVerify(selected.minutes());
+                Platform.runLater(() -> {
+                    SleepWaitTimeOption option = findSleepWaitTimeOption(options, readback);
+                    if (option != null) {
+                        options.getSelectionModel().select(option);
+                        status.setText(formatSleepWaitTimeStatus("sleep-wait-time.saved", option.label()));
+                    } else {
+                        status.setText(languageManager.getString("sleep-wait-time.invalid-current"));
+                    }
+                });
+            } catch (Exception e) {
+                Platform.runLater(() -> status.setText(languageManager.getString("sleep-wait-time.save-failed") + e.getMessage()));
+            } finally {
+                Platform.runLater(() -> busy.set(false));
+            }
+        }, "sleep-wait-time-save");
+        thread.setDaemon(true);
+        thread.start();
+    }
+
+    private SleepWaitTimeOption findSleepWaitTimeOption(ComboBox<SleepWaitTimeOption> options, int minutes) {
+        for (SleepWaitTimeOption option : options.getItems()) {
+            if (option.minutes() == minutes) {
+                return option;
+            }
+        }
+        return null;
+    }
+
+    private String formatSleepWaitTimeStatus(String key, String label) {
+        String pattern = languageManager.getString(key);
+        if (pattern == null || pattern.isBlank()) {
+            return label;
+        }
+        if (pattern.contains("%s")) {
+            return String.format(pattern, label);
+        }
+        return pattern + label;
+    }
+
+    private record SleepWaitTimeOption(int minutes, String label) {
+        @Override
+        public String toString() {
+            return label;
+        }
+    }
+
+    private record SleepWaitTimeControl(VBox node, Runnable refreshAction) {
+        void refresh() {
+            refreshAction.run();
+        }
+    }
+    
+    private void updateHookCardStatus(VBox card, String hookName, boolean isInstalled) {
+        for (var child : card.getChildren()) {
+            if (child instanceof Button button) {
+                if (isInstalled) {
+                    button.setText(languageManager.getString("hook.uninstall"));
+                    button.getStyleClass().remove("button-install");
+                    button.getStyleClass().add("button-uninstall");
+                    button.setOnAction(event -> uninstallHook(hookName));
+                } else {
+                    button.setText(languageManager.getString("hook.install"));
+                    button.getStyleClass().remove("button-uninstall");
+                    button.getStyleClass().add("button-install");
+                    button.setOnAction(event -> installHook(hookName));
+                }
+                break;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private VBox createHookCard(String hookName, boolean isInstalled) {
+        VBox card = new VBox(8);
+        card.getStyleClass().add("dialog-card");
+        card.setPadding(new Insets(12));
+
+        HBox titleRow = new HBox();
+        Label title = new Label(hookName + " Hook");
+        title.getStyleClass().add("dialog-card-title");
+        Region spacer1 = new Region();
+        HBox.setHgrow(spacer1, Priority.ALWAYS);
+        titleRow.getChildren().addAll(title, spacer1);
+
+        HBox statusRow = new HBox(8);
+        Label statusLabel = new Label(languageManager.getString("hook.installation-status") + ":");
+        statusLabel.getStyleClass().add("dialog-status-label");
+
+        Label statusValue = new Label(isInstalled ? languageManager.getString("hook.installed") : languageManager.getString("hook.not-installed"));
+        if (isInstalled) {
+            statusValue.getStyleClass().add("dialog-status-installed");
+        } else {
+            statusValue.getStyleClass().add("dialog-status-uninstalled");
+        }
+
+        Region spacer2 = new Region();
+        HBox.setHgrow(spacer2, Priority.ALWAYS);
+
+        Button installBtn = new Button(languageManager.getString("button.install"));
+        installBtn.getStyleClass().add("button-install");
+        installBtn.setOnAction(event -> {
+            installHook(hookName);
+            statusValue.setText(languageManager.getString("hook.installed"));
+            statusValue.getStyleClass().remove("dialog-status-uninstalled");
+            statusValue.getStyleClass().add("dialog-status-installed");
+        });
+
+        Button uninstallBtn = new Button(languageManager.getString("button.uninstall"));
+        uninstallBtn.getStyleClass().add("button-uninstall");
+        uninstallBtn.setOnAction(event -> {
+            uninstallHook(hookName);
+            statusValue.setText(languageManager.getString("hook.not-installed"));
+            statusValue.getStyleClass().remove("dialog-status-installed");
+            statusValue.getStyleClass().add("dialog-status-uninstalled");
+        });
+
+        statusRow.getChildren().addAll(statusLabel, statusValue, spacer2, installBtn, uninstallBtn);
+
+        card.getChildren().addAll(titleRow, statusRow);
+        return card;
+    }
+
+    // ==================== Hook 管理（委托给 HookInstaller） ====================
+
+    private boolean isHookInstalled(String hookName) {
+        return hookInstaller.isInstalled(hookName);
+    }
+
+    private void installHook(String hookName) {
+        hookInstaller.install(hookName);
+    }
+
+    private void uninstallHook(String hookName) {
+        addLog("[卸载] 开始卸载 " + hookName + " Hook...");
+        hookInstaller.uninstall(hookName);
+    }
+
+    private void addLog(String message) {
+        if (logArea != null) {
+            String timestamp = LocalDateTime.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("HH:mm:ss"));
+            logArea.appendText("[" + timestamp + "] " + message + "\n");
+            logArea.setScrollTop(Double.MAX_VALUE);
+        }
+    }
+    
+    private void showVersionDialog() {
+        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
+        alert.setTitle(languageManager.getString("dialog.version-title"));
+        alert.setHeaderText(null);
+        
+        String version = getVersion();
+        alert.setContentText(String.format(languageManager.getString("dialog.version-content"), version));
+        
+        alert.showAndWait();
+    }
+    
+    private String getVersion() {
+        return com.example.ahakey.update.InstalledAppIdentity.load().version();
+    }
+    
+    private void toggleLanguage() {
+        String newLang = languageManager.isChinese() ? "en" : "zh";
+        languageManager.switchLanguage(newLang);
+        
+        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.CONFIRMATION);
+        alert.setTitle(languageManager.getString("language-change-title"));
+        alert.setHeaderText(null);
+        alert.setContentText(languageManager.isChinese() 
+            ? languageManager.getString("language-change-chinese") 
+            : languageManager.getString("language-change-english"));
+        
+        ButtonType exitBtn = new ButtonType(languageManager.getString("dialog.exit-now"));
+        ButtonType laterBtn = new ButtonType(languageManager.getString("dialog.exit-later"));
+        
+        alert.getButtonTypes().setAll(exitBtn, laterBtn);
+        
+        Optional<ButtonType> result = alert.showAndWait();
+        if (result.isPresent() && result.get() == exitBtn) {
+            exitApplication();
+        }
+    }
+    
+    private void exitApplication() {
+        controller.requestApplicationExit();
+    }
+    
+    private void setupLanguageChangeListener() {
+        LanguageManager.LanguageChangeNotifier.addListener(() -> {
+            Platform.runLater(() -> {
+                if (languageMenuItem != null) {
+                    languageMenuItem.setText(languageManager.getLanguageToggleText());
+                }
+            });
+        });
+    }
+    
+    /**
+     * 语音状态指示灯组件
+     */
+    private class VoiceStatusLamp extends Canvas {
+        private String status = "stopped";
+        private double angle = 0;
+        private AnimationTimer timer;
+        private boolean isTimerRunning = false;
+        
+        public VoiceStatusLamp() {
+            super(16, 16);
+            timer = new AnimationTimer() {
+                @Override
+                public void handle(long now) {
+                    angle = (angle + 30) % 360;
+                    draw();
+                }
+            };
+        }
+        
+        public void setStatus(String status) {
+            this.status = status != null ? status : "stopped";
+            if (status.equals("starting") || status.equals("stopping") || status.equals("processing")) {
+                if (!isTimerRunning) {
+                    timer.start();
+                    isTimerRunning = true;
+                }
+            } else {
+                timer.stop();
+                isTimerRunning = false;
+                angle = 0;
+            }
+            draw();
+        }
+        
+        private void draw() {
+            GraphicsContext gc = getGraphicsContext2D();
+            gc.clearRect(0, 0, getWidth(), getHeight());
+            
+            if (status.equals("stopped")) {
+                // 灰色空心圆
+                gc.setStroke(javafx.scene.paint.Color.web("#8A9099"));
+                gc.setLineWidth(1.6);
+                gc.strokeOval(2.0, 2.0, getWidth() - 4, getHeight() - 4);
+            } else if (status.equals("starting") || status.equals("stopping") || status.equals("processing")) {
+                // 旋转动画
+                gc.setStroke(javafx.scene.paint.Color.web("#5C6470"));
+                gc.setLineWidth(1.6);
+                gc.strokeOval(2.0, 2.0, getWidth() - 4, getHeight() - 4);
+                
+                gc.setStroke(javafx.scene.paint.Color.web("#F5A623"));
+                gc.setLineWidth(2.2);
+                gc.setLineCap(javafx.scene.shape.StrokeLineCap.ROUND);
+                
+                double startAngle = -angle * Math.PI / 180;
+                double arcLength = -120 * Math.PI / 180;
+                gc.strokeArc(2.0, 2.0, getWidth() - 4, getHeight() - 4, startAngle, arcLength, javafx.scene.shape.ArcType.OPEN);
+            } else if (status.equals("ready")) {
+                // 绿色实心圆
+                gc.setFill(javafx.scene.paint.Color.web("#2ECC71"));
+                gc.fillOval(2.0, 2.0, getWidth() - 4, getHeight() - 4);
+            } else {
+                // 红色实心圆（error）
+                gc.setFill(javafx.scene.paint.Color.web("#E74C3C"));
+                gc.fillOval(2.0, 2.0, getWidth() - 4, getHeight() - 4);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}

+package com.example.ahakey.displayconfig;

+import com.example.ahakey.model.OledResourceState;

+import com.example.ahakey.util.OLEDFrameEncoder;
+import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
