+    static final class Firmware {
+        final String key; final List<byte[]> requests=new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();
+        volatile boolean dropIdentity; volatile int metadataState;
+        int sleep=5,mode=0,brightness=35;byte[] action={4};
+        final byte[] route=K1Route.ordinary(0x73,new byte[]{4}).bytes();
+        byte[] routeStage=new byte[102],resource=new byte[0];long routeCrc,resourceCrc;int resourceAt,resourceFrames,resourceEntry;boolean committed;
+        byte[] sideReply={0,0,2,2,0,2,0};
+        Firmware(String key){this.key=key;}
+        synchronized byte[] answer(byte[] f){
+            requests.add(f.clone());int op=f[2]&255;byte[] p={0};
+            if(op==0x9F){if(dropIdentity)return null;p=key.isEmpty()?new byte[]{0}:("\0"+key).getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.US_ASCII);}
+            else if(op==0)p=new byte[]{80,1,1,0,(byte)mode,0,0,(byte)brightness};
+            else if(op==0x95){if(f.length==7)sleep=(f[3]&255)|((f[4]&255)<<8);else p=new byte[]{0,(byte)sleep,(byte)(sleep>>8)};}
+            else if(op==0x92)mode=f[3]&255;
+            else if(op==0x85)brightness=f[3]&255;
+            else if(op==0x73 && f[3]==0x73)action=Arrays.copyOfRange(f,6,f.length-2);
+            else if(!key.isEmpty()) {
+                if(op==0x8E && f[3]==0)brightness=f[4]&255;
+                if(op==0x8A){var b=ByteBuffer.wrap(f).order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN);resourceEntry=f[3]&255;resource=new byte[b.getInt(4)];resourceFrames=b.getShort(8)&65535;resourceCrc=Integer.toUnsignedLong(b.getInt(10));resourceAt=0;committed=false;}
+                if(op==0x8B){int at=ByteBuffer.wrap(f).order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN).getInt(4),n=f[8]&255;check(at==resourceAt,"OLED chunk contiguous offset");System.arraycopy(f,9,resource,at,n);resourceAt+=n;}
+                if(op==0x8C){check(resourceAt==resource.length&&K1RouteProtocol.crc(resource)==resourceCrc,"OLED complete byte stream CRC");committed=true;}
+                if(op==0x87){p=new byte[6+action.length];p[1]=f[3];p[2]=f[4];p[3]=0x73;p[4]=(byte)action.length;System.arraycopy(action,0,p,5,action.length);}
+                if(op==0x97){if(f.length==5)p=sideReply;else{sideReply=new byte[f.length-3];sideReply[1]=0;sideReply[2]=2;System.arraycopy(f,4,sideReply,3,f.length-6);}}
+                if(op==0x88){int kind=f[3]&255;boolean valid=committed&&(f[4]&255)==resourceEntry&&metadataState==0;
+                    p=switch(kind){case 0->new byte[]{0,0,(byte)brightness};case 1->new byte[]{0,1,f[4],f[5],1};case 2->new byte[]{0,2,f[4],(byte)(valid?1:0),(byte)(valid?2:metadataState)};case 3->K1RouteProtocol.fields(13).put((byte)0).put((byte)3).put(f[4]).putShort((short)(valid?resourceFrames:0)).putInt(valid?resource.length:0).putInt(valid?(int)resourceCrc:0).array();default->new byte[]{1};};}
+                if(op==0x9E){
+                    int sub=f[4]&255;var input=ByteBuffer.wrap(f).order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN);int nonce=input.getInt(5);
+                    if(sub==0)p=K1RouteProtocol.fields(9).put((byte)0).put((byte)1).put((byte)0).putInt(nonce).put((byte)3).put((byte)4).array();
+                    else if(sub==0x10){int offset=f[10]&255,count=f[11]&255;p=K1RouteProtocol.fields(15+count).put((byte)0).put((byte)1).put((byte)sub).putInt(nonce).put(f[9]).put((byte)offset).putShort((short)102).putInt((int)K1RouteProtocol.crc(route)).put(route,offset,count).array();}
+                    else if(sub==0x20){int token=input.getInt(12);routeStage=new byte[102];routeCrc=Integer.toUnsignedLong(input.getInt(8));p=K1RouteProtocol.fields(13).put((byte)0).put((byte)1).put((byte)sub).putInt(token).putInt(token).putShort((short)0).array();}
+                    else if(sub==0x21){int offset=input.getShort(10)&65535,count=f[12]&255;System.arraycopy(f,13,routeStage,offset,count);p=K1RouteProtocol.fields(11).put((byte)0).put((byte)1).put((byte)sub).putInt(nonce).put(f[9]).putShort((short)(offset+count)).put((byte)count).array();}
+                    else if(sub==0x22){check(K1RouteProtocol.crc(routeStage)==routeCrc,"K1 staged CRC validated");System.arraycopy(routeStage,0,route,0,102);p=K1RouteProtocol.fields(12).put((byte)0).put((byte)1).put((byte)sub).putInt(nonce).put(f[9]).putInt((int)routeCrc).array();}
+                }
+            }
+            return AhaKeyProtocol.buildFrame(f[2],p);
+        }
+        long count(int op){return requests.stream().filter(f->(f[2]&255)==op).count();}
+    }

+            var reply=ManagedBridgeClient.fields().put("version",1).put("bridgeInstanceId","fixture").put("requestId",request.path("requestId").asText()).put("ok",true);
+            byte[] notify=null;

+            switch(request.path("op").asText()){
+                case "enumerateConnected"->{reply.put("snapshotId","snapshot");var array=reply.putArray("candidates");for(String id:List.of("A","B"))array.addObject().put("targetId",id).put("name","AhaKey").put("address","MAC-"+id);}

+        }}catch(EOFException|SocketException end){}catch(Exception error){error.printStackTrace();}}

+            fx(()->{c.getK1RouteService().refresh();return null;});
+            check(fx(()->c.getStudioState().k1Routes().capability.get()==K1RouteState.Capability.LEGACY&&!c.getStudioState().k1Routes().confirmed(0)),"legacy K1 editable layer never fabricates observed config");
+            fx(()->{var s=c.getStudioState();s.getKeyConfig(StudioPart.KEY1).setHidCode(7);s.markDirty(StudioPart.KEY1);s.displayConfig().editBrightness(44);s.displayConfig().editEffect(0,0,3);var method=StudioController.class.getDeclaredMethod("syncAllModesAfterK1",boolean.class);method.setAccessible(true);method.invoke(c,false);return null;});
+            await(()->f.firmware.count(4)>0,"actual controller retains legacy save endpoint");
+            check(f.firmware.requests.stream().anyMatch(r->r[2]==0x73&&r[3]==0x73&&r[5]==0&&r[6]==7),"controller sent legacy K1 action");
+            check(f.firmware.count(0x84)==1&&f.firmware.count(0x85)==1,"controller saves only edited legacy lights");
+            check(f.firmware.count(0x73)==1,"one legacy edit never clears the other fifteen unknown keys or descriptions");
+            long g=f.connectionGeneration();try(var binding=f.bindGeneration(g)){f.disconnect();}
+            check(!fx(()->c.getDeviceStatus().isConnected()),"bound-generation disconnect clears actual UI");
+        }finally{fx(()->{c.shutdown();return null;});}
+    }
+    static void failures()throws Exception{
+        System.setProperty("user.home",Files.createTempDirectory("r6-failure-").toString());
+        java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger ready=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger();
+        var callback=new BleManager.BleCallback(){public void onConnected(){ready.incrementAndGet();}public void onDisconnected(){}public void onStatusReceived(DeviceStatus s){}public void onError(String s){System.out.println(s);}};
+        try(var f=new Fixture("x1-c582-hw1-p1-1.0.1-r001",false,callback)){
+            var registry=(DeviceRecoveryRegistry)get(f,"recoveryTargets");registry.require("USB:A");registry.observe("USB",Set.of());f.firmware.metadataState=1;
+            f.connectSelected(new DeviceTarget("USB","A","AhaKey"));await(()->!f.isScanning(),"writing metadata failure finishes");
+            check(!f.isConnected()&&ready.get()==0&&registry.contains("USB:A"),"recovery failure retains record and never calls onConnected");
+            f.firmware.metadataState=0;registry.observe("USB",Set.of());f.connectA();check(ready.get()==1&&!registry.contains("USB:A"),"explicit retry after all19 idle succeeds");
+        }
+        for(String key:List.of("unknown-catalog","x1-c582-hw1-p1-1.0.1-r001"))try(var f=new Fixture(key,false,CALLBACK)){
+            f.connectA();f.firmware.dropIdentity=key.startsWith("x1");
+            try{f.readFirmwareCatalogKey();if(f.firmware.dropIdentity)throw new AssertionError("timeout accepted");}catch(IOException e){check(f.firmware.dropIdentity,"only deliberate timeout fails read");}
+            check(!f.capabilities().legacyWrites()&&!f.isDisplayAdmitted(),"unknown identity or timeout never becomes legacy/full");
+        }
+        for(boolean ble:new boolean[]{false,true})try(var f=new Fixture("",ble,CALLBACK)){
+            var registry=(DeviceRecoveryRegistry)get(f,"recoveryTargets");String target=(ble?"BLE:":"USB:")+"A";registry.require(target);registry.observe(ble?"BLE":"USB",Set.of());
+            f.connectA();check(f.isLegacySaveResultUnknown()&&registry.contains(target)&&f.firmware.count(0x88)==0,"legacy recovery restores command communication, retains unknown save record, no fabricated88");
+            f.sendCommandExpecting(AhaKeyProtocol.setKeyMapping(0,0,new byte[]{8}),(byte)0x73);
+            try{f.writeData(new byte[]{1});throw new AssertionError("unknown legacy resource state was writable");}catch(IOException expected){checks++;}
+            f.disconnect();f.connectA();check(registry.contains(target)&&f.isLegacySaveResultUnknown(),"legacy reconnect repeats live proof and retains uncertainty history");
+        }
+    }
+    static void lightingTargets()throws Exception{
+        fx(()->{
+            StudioState s=new StudioState();s.selectDevice("BLE:A");
+            BleManager legacy=new BleManager(CALLBACK){public FirmwareCapabilities capabilities(){return FirmwareCapabilities.LEGACY;}};
+            try(var service=new DisplayConfigService(s,legacy,new OledResourceStore(Files.createTempDirectory("r6-lights-")))){
+                service.capabilityChanged();var state=s.displayConfig();

+                state.editBrightness(44);state.editEffect(0,0,3);s.selectDevice("USB:B#new");service.connectionChanged();state.readBrightness(80);state.readEffect(0,0,16);
+                check(state.brightness.get()==80&&state.effect(0,0)==16&&!state.brightnessDirty()&&!state.effectDirty(0,0),"B displays B readback, not A draft");
+                s.selectDevice("BLE:A");check(state.brightness.get()==44&&state.effect(0,0)==3&&state.brightnessDirty()&&state.effectDirty(0,0),"returning A retains A user edits");
+                state.readEffect(0,1,12);state.invalidateLightingObservations();
+                check(state.effect(0,1)==-1&&!state.effectKnownOrEdited(0,1)&&state.brightness.get()==44&&state.brightnessKnownOrEdited(),"same-address new generation drops clean facts but retains explicit edits");
+                s.selectDevice("BLE:C");s.updateKeyDescription(StudioPart.KEY2,"only description");
+                var commands=DeviceSyncService.legacyEditedCommands(s);check(commands.size()==2&&commands.get(0).data()[3]==0x75,"description-only legacy edit never clears unknown action");
+                s.selectDevice("BLE:D");check(DeviceSyncService.legacyEditedCommands(s).isEmpty(),"new target placeholders generate no legacy writes");
+            }finally{legacy.shutdownTransport();}
+            return null;
+        });
+    }
+    public static void main(String[] args)throws Exception{
+        Platform.startup(()->{});Platform.setImplicitExit(false);
+        try{for(String key:List.of("x1-c582-hw1-p1-1.0.1-r001","x1-c582-hw2-p1-1.0.1-r001",""))for(boolean ble:new boolean[]{false,true})matrix(key,ble);controllerLegacy();failures();lightingTargets();
+            System.out.println("PASS "+checks+" firmware matrix assertions. Offline only, no hardware acceptance.");
+        }finally{Platform.exit();}
+    }

+    static void concurrentEdits(Installed installer,Path home)throws Exception {
+        var make=HookInstaller.class.getDeclaredMethod("configurationPlan",String.class,boolean.class);make.setAccessible(true);
+        var apply=HookInstaller.class.getDeclaredMethod("applyPlan",Map.class);apply.setAccessible(true);
+        Path config=installer.getHookConfigPath("Claude");Files.writeString(config,"{\"external\":1}");
+        Object plan=make.invoke(installer,"Claude",false);String external="{\"external\":2}";Files.writeString(config,external);
+        try{apply.invoke(installer,plan);throw new AssertionError("stale plan overwrote external changes");}catch(java.lang.reflect.InvocationTargetException expected){}
+        check(Files.readString(config).equals(external),"planning-to-commit race preserves external edits");

+        Object multi=make.invoke(installer,"Codex",false);Files.writeString(toml,"model='external'\n");
+        try{apply.invoke(installer,multi);throw new AssertionError("stale TOML plan accepted");}catch(java.lang.reflect.InvocationTargetException expected){}
+        check(!Files.exists(codex)&&Files.readString(toml).equals("model='external'\n"),"all multi-file snapshots checked before first write");
+        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked") Map<Path,Object> rollback=(Map<Path,Object>)make.invoke(installer,"Claude",false);
+        var planned=Class.forName("com.example.ahakey.service.HookInstaller$PlannedFile").getDeclaredConstructor(byte[].class,byte[].class);planned.setAccessible(true);
+        Path blocker=home.resolve("not-a-directory");Files.writeString(blocker,"unchanged");
+        rollback.put(blocker.resolve("fail.txt"),planned.newInstance(null,"fail".getBytes()));
+        try{apply.invoke(installer,rollback);throw new AssertionError("write failure expected");}catch(java.lang.reflect.InvocationTargetException expected){}
+        check(Files.readString(config).equals(external),"later file failure restores earlier config and leaves backup");
+    }
+    static void kimi(Installed installer,Path home)throws Exception {
+        Path config=installer.getHookConfigPath("Kimi");Files.createDirectories(config.getParent());
+        String foreign="# Keep my comment\n[[hooks]]\nevent='Stop'\ncommand='foreign-command'\ntimeout=14\n\n[other]\nvalue='preserve exactly'\n";
+        String old="[[hooks]]\nevent='PermissionRequest'\ncommand='powershell -File \""+home.resolve(".ahakey/hooks/ahakey-hook.ps1").toString().replace("\\","/")+"\" KimiPermissionRequest'\ntimeout=10\n";
+        Files.writeString(config,old+foreign);
+        check(installer.getHookStatus("Kimi")==HookInstaller.HookStatus.OUTDATED,"unsupported owned legacy event remains repairable");
+        check(installer.installOrUpdate("Kimi").success(),"Kimi migrates canonical config and current consumer events");
+        String first=Files.readString(config);check(first.contains(foreign)&&!first.contains("PermissionRequest"),"foreign TOML bytes survive while owned invalid event removed");
+        check(installer.getHookStatus("Kimi")==HookInstaller.HookStatus.LATEST,"Kimi scanner verifies generated registration");
+        check(installer.installOrUpdate("Kimi").success()&&Files.readString(config).equals(first),"second same-version update writes nothing");
+        Path evidence=Path.of(System.getProperty("wd007.artifacts",home.resolve("schema-evidence").toString()));Files.createDirectories(evidence);
+        Files.writeString(evidence.resolve("generated-kimi.toml"),first,StandardOpenOption.CREATE_NEW);
+        System.out.println("GENERATED_KIMI_CONFIG="+evidence.resolve("generated-kimi.toml"));
+        Files.writeString(config,first.replace("timeout = 20","timeout = 19"));check(installer.getHookStatus("Kimi")!=HookInstaller.HookStatus.LATEST,"Kimi wrong timeout is not latest");
+        check(installer.installOrUpdate("Kimi").success(),"repair incorrect registered timeout");
+        installer.uninstall("Kimi");check(Files.readString(config).contains(foreign)&&!Files.readString(config).contains("KimiPreToolUse"),"Kimi removal preserves foreign TOML");
+        Files.writeString(config,"hooks = []\n# untouched\n[other]\nvalue='keep'\n");
+        check(installer.installOrUpdate("Kimi").success()&&Files.readString(config).contains("# untouched\n[other]\nvalue='keep'"),"initial empty hook array can be installed safely");
+        String invalid="[[hooks]]\nevent='PermissionRequest'\ncommand='foreign-command'\n";Files.writeString(config,invalid);
+        check(!installer.installOrUpdate("Kimi").success()&&Files.readString(config).equals(invalid),"invalid foreign Kimi event is reported, never deleted");
+        installer.version="9.0.0";check(installer.getHookStatus("Kimi")==HookInstaller.HookStatus.UNSUPPORTED&&!installer.installOrUpdate("Kimi").success(),"unknown Kimi capability cannot be certified or installed");
+    }
+    public static void main(String[] args)throws Exception {
+        Path home=Files.createTempDirectory("wd007-hook-management-");System.setProperty("user.home",home.toString());
+        Installed installer=new Installed();
+        Path canonical=home.resolve(".claude/settings.json");Files.createDirectories(canonical.getParent());

+        Files.writeString(canonical,mixed);

+        }
+        check(installer.getHookConfigPath("Claude").equals(canonical),"Claude uses actual settings entry");

+        check(installer.getHookStatus("Claude")!=HookInstaller.HookStatus.LATEST,"version marker alone cannot certify invalid core");

+        System.out.println("PASS explicit Hook update/rescan, registration, core integrity and mixed ownership");

+package com.example.ahakey.service;

+import com.example.ahakey.app.StudioController;

+
+/** Actual management window, scans and asynchronous button handlers, isolated installer home. */
+public final class Wd007HookUiChecks {
+    static List<Node> nodes(Node node){var result=new ArrayList<Node>();result.add(node);if(node instanceof ScrollPane s)result.addAll(nodes(s.getContent()));else if(node instanceof Parent p)for(Node child:p.getChildrenUnmodifiable())result.addAll(nodes(child));return result;}
+    static void until(Callable<Boolean> condition,String message)throws Exception {
+        long end=System.nanoTime()+TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(8);
+        while(System.nanoTime()<end){if(fx(condition)){check(true,message);return;}Thread.sleep(20);}throw new AssertionError(message);
+    }
+    static VBox card(Stage dialog,String platform){return (VBox)nodes(dialog.getScene().getRoot()).stream().filter(n->n instanceof VBox box&&box.getStyleClass().contains("dialog-card")&&nodes(box).stream().anyMatch(c->c instanceof Label l&&l.getText().equals(platform))).findFirst().orElseThrow();}
+    static boolean label(VBox card,String text){return nodes(card).stream().anyMatch(n->n instanceof Label l&&l.getText().contains(text));}
+    static Button action(VBox card){return (Button)nodes(card).stream().filter(n->n instanceof Button).findFirst().orElseThrow();}
+    public static void main(String[] args)throws Exception {
+        Path home=Files.createTempDirectory("wd007-hook-ui-");System.setProperty("user.home",home.toString());
+        Platform.startup(()->{});Platform.setImplicitExit(false);
+        var installer=new Wd007HookManagementChecks.Installed();
+        StudioController c=fx(()->new StudioController(Wire::new,ble->new KimiAhaKeyBridge(ble,0),false));
+        try {
+            TopBar bar=fx(()->new TopBar(c,c.getDeviceStatus(),c.getStudioState(),c.getAgentManager()));
+            var field=TopBar.class.getDeclaredField("hookInstaller");field.setAccessible(true);field.set(bar,installer);
+            Stage parent=fx(()->{Stage s=new Stage();s.setScene(new Scene(bar));s.show();return s;});
+            var open=TopBar.class.getDeclaredMethod("showHookManagerDialog");open.setAccessible(true);
+            for(int round=0;round<2;round++) {
+                Stage dialog=fx(()->{open.invoke(bar);return (Stage)Window.getWindows().stream().filter(w->w!=parent&&w.isShowing()).findFirst().orElseThrow();});
+                until(()->!label(card(dialog,"Claude"),"检测中"),"scan completes");
+                check(fx(()->Window.getWindows().stream().filter(Window::isShowing).count()==2),"opening manager never creates modal update prompt");
+                if(round==0)for(String platform:List.of("Claude","Kimi")) {
+                    fx(()->{Button button=action(card(dialog,platform));check(!button.isDisabled(),"explicit install enabled");button.fire();return null;});
+                    until(()->label(card(dialog,platform),"最新")&&label(card(dialog,platform),"成功："),"actual button finishes with persistent success and latest "+platform);
+                    check(installer.getHookStatus(platform)==HookInstaller.HookStatus.LATEST,"UI success matches real registration");
+                }
+                else check(fx(()->label(card(dialog,"Claude"),"最新")&&label(card(dialog,"Kimi"),"最新")),"reopen remains latest");
+                fx(()->{dialog.close();return null;});
+            }
+            Path broken=installer.getHookConfigPath("Claude");Files.writeString(broken,"{}");
+            Stage dialog=fx(()->{open.invoke(bar);return (Stage)Window.getWindows().stream().filter(w->w!=parent&&w.isShowing()).findFirst().orElseThrow();});
+            until(()->!action(card(dialog,"Claude")).isDisabled(),"update available for missing registration");

+package com.example.ahakey.service;
+
+import com.example.ahakey.protocol.*;
+import com.example.ahakey.model.connection.DeviceTarget;
+import java.nio.file.*;
+import java.util.*;
+import java.util.concurrent.*;
+import static com.example.ahakey.service.Wd007BehaviorChecks.*;
+
+/** Missing-tail replay through the actual scheduler, reassembler and recovery registry. */
+public final class Wd007ReadFailureChecks {
+    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
+        System.setProperty("user.home",Files.createTempDirectory("wd007-read-failure-").toString());
+        Wire wire=new Wire();var pool=Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor();
+        try {
+            wire.connect();await(wire::isConnected,"memory connected");wire.held=0x9e;
+            long generation=wire.connectionGeneration();
+            byte[] request=K1RouteProtocol.request(0x10,K1RouteProtocol.fields(7).putInt(123).put((byte)0).put((byte)0).put((byte)42).array());
+            long at=System.nanoTime();
+            var result=pool.submit(()->{try{wire.k1RouteTransaction(generation,()->wire.exchangeK1Route(request,generation));return false;}catch(Exception expected){System.out.println("READ_FAILURE="+expected);return true;}});
+            await(()->wire.heldRequest!=null,"read sent");Memory old=wire.heldMemory;
+            byte[] response=new byte[62];response[0]=(byte)0xaa;response[1]=(byte)0xbb;response[2]=(byte)0x9e;response[4]=1;response[5]=0x10;response[6]=123;response[12]=102;response[60]=(byte)0xcc;response[61]=(byte)0xdd;
+            for(int offset=0;offset<60;offset+=20)old.receive.accept(Arrays.copyOfRange(response,offset,offset+20));
+            check(result.get(18,TimeUnit.SECONDS),"incomplete response must fail");
+            System.out.println("TIMEOUT_MS="+TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.toMillis(System.nanoTime()-at)+" connected="+wire.isConnected()+" writeUncertain="+wire.isDisplayWriteUncertain());
+            var registry=new com.example.ahakey.service.connection.DeviceRecoveryRegistry(Path.of(System.getProperty("user.home"),".ahakey","device-recovery.json"));
+            check(!registry.contains("USB:A"),"read-only timeout must not persist unknown-write quarantine");
+            check(!wire.isConnected()&&wire.connectionGeneration()!=generation,"failed read retires session before accepting more commands");
+            int sent=wire.frames.size();try{wire.sendCommand(AhaKeyProtocol.queryFirmwareCatalogKey());throw new AssertionError("failed session reused");}catch(java.io.IOException expected){}
+            check(wire.frames.size()==sent,"no implicit retry or new command in failed session");
+            wire.held=-1;wire.connectSelected(new DeviceTarget("USB","A","A"));await(wire::isConnected,"explicit reconnect without power cycle");
+            long next=wire.connectionGeneration();old.receive.accept(Arrays.copyOfRange(response,60,62));old.answer(AhaKeyProtocol.queryDeviceStatus());
+            check(wire.connectionGeneration()==next&&wire.getCachedStatus().getSwitchState()==-1,"late old tail/status cannot enter new session");
+            check(wire.readFirmwareCatalogKey().equals("x1-c582-hw1-p1-1.0.0-r001"),"fresh read succeeds without durable quarantine");
+            System.out.println("PASS missing tail retires read session, isolates stale callbacks and permits explicit reconnect");
+        } finally {pool.shutdownNow();wire.shutdownTransport();}
+    }
+}

+package com.example.ahakey.service;
+
+import com.example.ahakey.app.StudioController;
+import com.example.ahakey.model.connection.DeviceTarget;
+import com.example.ahakey.service.connection.DeviceRecoveryRegistry;
+import javafx.application.Platform;
+import java.nio.file.Files;
+import java.util.concurrent.*;
+import static com.example.ahakey.service.Wd007BehaviorChecks.*;
+
+public final class Wd007RecoveryControllerChecks {
+    static <T>T fx(Callable<T> action)throws Exception{FutureTask<T> task=new FutureTask<>(action);Platform.runLater(task);return task.get(5,TimeUnit.SECONDS);}
+    public static void main(String[] args)throws Exception{
+        System.setProperty("user.home",Files.createTempDirectory("wd007-recovery-controller-").toString());Platform.startup(()->{});Platform.setImplicitExit(false);
+        Wire[] wire=new Wire[1];StudioController controller=fx(()->new StudioController(cb->wire[0]=new Wire(cb),ble->new KimiAhaKeyBridge(ble,0),false));
+        try{
+            Wire w=wire[0];DeviceRecoveryRegistry registry=(DeviceRecoveryRegistry)field(w,"recoveryTargets");registry.require("USB:A");registry.observe("USB",java.util.Set.of());
+            w.held=0x88;w.connectSelected(new DeviceTarget("USB","A","A"));await(()->w.heldMemory!=null,"recovery holds first metadata probe");
+            check(w.frames.equals(java.util.List.of("A:159","A:136")),"only identity plus recovery metadata before controller initialization");
+            try{w.readFirmwareCatalogKey();throw new AssertionError("ordinary reader bypassed recovery");}catch(java.io.IOException expected){}
+            check(w.isDisplayAdmitted()&&w.isDisplayWriteUncertain()&&!w.refreshAutoApproval(),"ordinary reader cannot poison probe identity or approve");
+            check(!fx(()->controller.getDeviceStatus().isConnected()),"onConnected initialization is held until recovery proof");
+            w.held=-1;w.heldMemory.answer(w.heldRequest);
+            await(()->!w.isDisplayWriteUncertain(),"metadata proof clears recovery");
+            await(()->w.frames.contains("A:135"),"actual controller input initialization starts after proof");
+            var observed=java.util.List.copyOf(w.frames);int firstInput=observed.indexOf("A:135");
+            check(observed.subList(0,firstInput).stream().filter(s->s.equals("A:136")).count()==19,"all 19 metadata records precede ordinary key readback");
+            check(!registry.contains("USB:A"),"successful proof persisted");
+            System.out.println("PASS "+checks+" actual controller quarantine reconnect checks");
+        }finally{fx(()->{controller.shutdown();return null;});Platform.exit();}
+    }
+}

+package com.example.ahakey.service;
+
+import com.example.ahakey.model.connection.DeviceTarget;
+import com.example.ahakey.protocol.AhaKeyProtocol;
+import java.io.*;
+import java.nio.file.*;
+import java.util.*;
+import java.util.concurrent.*;
+import static com.example.ahakey.service.Wd007BehaviorChecks.*;
+
+/** Real admission, owned-release, observation and generated wrapper paths; memory devices only. */
+public final class Wd007SafetyChecks {
+    static void set(Object object,String name,Object value)throws Exception{var f=BleManager.class.getDeclaredField(name);f.setAccessible(true);f.set(object,value);}
+    static Wire connected()throws Exception{
+        System.setProperty("user.home",Files.createTempDirectory("wd007-safety-").toString());
+        Wire w=new Wire();w.connectSelected(new DeviceTarget("USB","A","A"));await(w::isConnected,"fixture connected");return w;
+    }
+    static void queued(boolean timeout)throws Exception{
+        Wire w=connected();var pool=Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2);try{
+            w.held=0x73;
+            var firstThread=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<Thread>();
+            var first=pool.submit(()->{firstThread.set(Thread.currentThread());try{w.sendCommand(AhaKeyProtocol.buildFrame((byte)0x73,new byte[]{0}));return false;}catch(IOException expected){return true;}});
+            await(()->w.heldMemory!=null,"first physical request held");
+            // Physical transmission returns before firmwareExchange reacquires its lock and awaits
+            // the ACK. Starting the second tryLock caller in that gap can legitimately reject it.
+            await(()->Arrays.stream(firstThread.get().getStackTrace()).anyMatch(frame->frame.getMethodName().equals("awaitNanos")),"first request entered actual ACK condition wait");
+            var thread=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<Thread>();
+            var second=pool.submit(()->{thread.set(Thread.currentThread());try{w.sendCommand(AhaKeyProtocol.queryDeviceStatus());return false;}catch(IOException expected){return true;}});
+            await(()->thread.get()!=null&&thread.get().getState()==Thread.State.TIMED_WAITING,"second request entered ACK wait queue");
+            if(!timeout)w.cancelDeviceOperation();
+            check(first.get(18,TimeUnit.SECONDS)&&second.get(2,TimeUnit.SECONDS),"both pending operations fail after "+(timeout?"timeout":"cancel"));
+            w.heldMemory.answer(w.heldRequest);
+            check(w.frames.equals(List.of("A:115")),"queued request never reaches physical sender, even with late ACK");
+            check(!w.refreshAutoApproval(),"quarantine cannot auto approve");
+        }finally{w.shutdownTransport();pool.shutdownNow();}
+    }
+    static void releaseAndObservation()throws Exception{
+        Wire w=connected();try{
+            String attachment=w.draftIdentity();w.observeUsbCandidates(Set.of("A"));
+            // Emulate a failed release being safely retired by the actual managed client (null means never had a child).
+            set(w,"bridgeSession","dead-session");set(w,"activeTarget",new DeviceTarget("BLE","old","A"));
+            w.disconnect();w.candidates=List.of("A","B");w.connectSelected(new DeviceTarget("USB","B","B"));
+            await(()->w.isConnected()&&w.targetIdentity().equals("USB:B"),"dead owned bridge does not permanently block USB B");
+            w.observeUsbCandidates(Set.of("A","B"));
+            w.connectSelected(new DeviceTarget("USB","A","A"));await(()->w.isConnected()&&w.targetIdentity().equals("USB:A"),"manual return same attachment");
+            check(!w.draftIdentity().equals(attachment),"new USB configuration session never reuses identity based only on same path");
+            w.observeUsbCandidates(Set.of("B"));check(!w.isConnected(),"physical remove disconnects without fallback");
+            w.observeUsbCandidates(Set.of("A","B"));check(!w.isConnected(),"new insertion while disconnected never opens");
+            w.connectSelected(new DeviceTarget("USB","A","replacement"));await(w::isConnected,"replacement explicitly selected");
+            check(!w.draftIdentity().equals(attachment),"no-serial same-path replacement has distinct draft identity");
+            check(UsbHidTransport.configurationPaths(List.of("hid#vid_413c&pid_2107&mi_01#a","hid#vid_413c&pid_2107&mi_00#a","hid#vid_other&pid_1")).equals(List.of("hid#vid_413c&pid_2107&mi_01#a")),"only AhaKey configuration interface is a candidate");
+            var registry=(com.example.ahakey.service.connection.DeviceRecoveryRegistry)field(w,"recoveryTargets");registry.require("USB:A");
+            w.enumerationFails=true;try{w.pollUsbCandidates();throw new AssertionError("failed enumeration accepted");}catch(IllegalStateException expected){}
+            check(!registry.absenceObserved("USB:A"),"enumeration failure cannot establish absence");
+        }finally{w.shutdownTransport();}
+    }
+    static void hooks()throws Exception{
+        Wire w=connected();HookDispatchServer server=new HookDispatchServer(w,0);try{
+            w.readFirmwareCatalogKey();
+            server.start();var f=HookDispatchServer.class.getDeclaredField("serverSocket");f.setAccessible(true);int port=((java.net.ServerSocket)f.get(server)).getLocalPort();
+            new HookInstaller(port,s->{}).generateAllScripts();
+            Path home=Path.of(System.getProperty("user.home"));
+            for(String state:List.of("auto","custom","unknown","written-awaiting-readback")){
+                w.confirmSavedBindings(side(true),w.connectionGeneration());w.position=0;
+                if(state.equals("custom"))w.position=1;
+                if(state.equals("unknown"))w.getCachedStatus().clearSavedBindings();
+                if(state.equals("written-awaiting-readback"))w.writeSideSwitchConfig(side(false));
+                for(String event:List.of("PermissionRequest","CodexPreToolUse","CodexPermissionRequest")){
+                    String name=event.startsWith("Codex")?"codex":"claude";
+                    Path log=home.resolve(state+"-"+event+".json");
+                    var builder=new ProcessBuilder("powershell.exe","-NoProfile","-NonInteractive","-ExecutionPolicy","Bypass","-File",home.resolve(".ahakey/hooks/ahakey-"+name+".ps1").toString(),event);
+                    builder.environment().put("USERPROFILE",home.toString());builder.redirectErrorStream(true).redirectOutput(log.toFile());
+                    Process process=builder.start();try{process.getOutputStream().write("{}".getBytes());process.getOutputStream().close();check(process.waitFor(8,TimeUnit.SECONDS),"generated wrapper terminates");check(process.exitValue()==0,"wrapper exit success");}finally{if(process.isAlive())process.destroyForcibly();}
+                    String result=Files.readString(log).trim();
+                    check(state.equals("auto")?result.contains("\"behavior\":\"allow\""):result.equals("{}"),"actual generated "+event+" "+state+" => native policy");
+                }
+                if(state.equals("written-awaiting-readback"))check(!w.getCachedStatus().isAutoApproval(),"write ACK alone cannot restore approval facts");
+            }
+        }finally{server.stop();w.shutdownTransport();}
+    }
+    public static void main(String[] args)throws Exception{queued(false);queued(true);releaseAndObservation();hooks();System.out.println("PASS "+checks+" WD007 safety checks; wrappers executed only in temporary USERPROFILE");}

+package com.example.ahakey.service;
+
+import com.example.ahakey.firmware.*;
+import com.example.ahakey.model.*;
+import com.example.ahakey.model.connection.DeviceTarget;
+import com.example.ahakey.protocol.*;
+import java.io.IOException;
+import java.nio.*;
+import java.nio.file.Files;
+import java.util.*;
+import java.util.concurrent.*;
+import java.util.concurrent.atomic.*;
+import static com.example.ahakey.service.Wd007BehaviorChecks.*;
+
+/** Real BLE scheduler/notification path; only native transmission is replaced by an in-memory peripheral. */
+public final class Wd007TransferChecks {
+    static final class Peripheral extends BleManager {
+        final List<byte[]> sent=new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();
+        final List<Integer> slices=new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();
+        final K1Route route=K1RouteProtocolChecks.Wire.consumer(0xe9);
+        final CountDownLatch held=new CountDownLatch(1),release=new CountDownLatch(1);
+        volatile String fault="";int delayMs,chunks,commits,acknowledged;byte[] uploaded;long crc;
+        Peripheral(boolean usb)throws Exception {
+            super(CALLBACK);
+            DeviceMaintenanceChecks.field(this,"isConnected",true);getCachedStatus().setConnected(true);
+            DeviceMaintenanceChecks.field(this,"activeTarget",new DeviceTarget(usb?"USB":"BLE","memory","memory"));
+            DeviceMaintenanceChecks.field(this,"firmwareIdentity",new FirmwareIdentity(0,1,DeviceStatus.IdentityState.SUPPORTED,FirmwareCatalog.CATALOG));
+            if(usb)DeviceMaintenanceChecks.field(this,"usbTransport",new DeviceMaintenanceChecks.MemoryUsb());
+        }
+        @Override public void queryStatus(){}
+        void notify(long generation,byte[] frame)throws Exception {
+            var receive=BleManager.class.getDeclaredMethod("onSessionNotify",long.class,byte[].class);receive.setAccessible(true);
+            for(int offset=0;offset<frame.length;offset+=20)receive.invoke(this,generation,Arrays.copyOfRange(frame,offset,Math.min(frame.length,offset+20)));
+        }
+        @Override protected void transmitFirmware(UsbHidTransport usb,String session,String target,long generation,byte[] frame,boolean data)throws Exception {
+            check(!data,"resource upload uses framed acknowledged chunks");sent.add(frame.clone());int opcode=frame[2]&255;
+            var fields=ByteBuffer.wrap(frame).order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN);
+            if(opcode==0x9e) {
+                int nonce=fields.getInt(5),mode=frame[9]&255,offset=frame[10]&255,count=frame[11]&255;
+                slices.add(count);byte[] bytes=route.bytes();
+                var reply=K1RouteProtocol.fields(12+count).putInt(nonce+(fault.equals("nonce")?1:0))
+                        .put((byte)(mode+(fault.equals("mode")?1:0))).put((byte)(offset+(fault.equals("offset")?1:0)))
+                        .putShort((short)102).putInt((int)K1RouteProtocol.crc(bytes)+(fault.equals("crc")?1:0)).put(bytes,offset,count).array();
+                notify(generation,K1RouteProtocolChecks.response(0x10,0,reply));return;
+            }
+            if(opcode==0x8a){uploaded=new byte[fields.getInt(4)];crc=Integer.toUnsignedLong(fields.getInt(10));}
+            else if(opcode==0x8b) {
+                chunks++;int offset=fields.getInt(4),count=frame[8]&255;
+                check(offset==acknowledged&&count>0&&count<=48,"contiguous stop-and-wait chunk offsets");
+                System.arraycopy(frame,9,uploaded,offset,count);
+                if(chunks==2&&!fault.isEmpty()) {
+                    held.countDown();
+                    if(fault.equals("native-hang")) {
+                        // Simulates a driver ignoring interruption; test cleanup explicitly releases it.
+                        while(release.getCount()>0)try{release.await();}catch(InterruptedException ignored){}
+                    } else return; // Transport wrote but notification was lost.
+                }
+                if(delayMs>0)Thread.sleep(delayMs);
+                acknowledged+=count;
+            } else if(opcode==0x8c){commits++;check(acknowledged==uploaded.length&&K1RouteProtocol.crc(uploaded)==crc,"commit only after exact bytes and CRC");}
+            else throw new AssertionError("Unexpected opcode "+opcode);
+            notify(generation,AhaKeyProtocol.buildFrame(frame[2],new byte[]{0}));
+        }
+    }
+    static void routeReads()throws Exception {
+        for(boolean usb:new boolean[]{false,true}) {
+            Peripheral p=new Peripheral(usb);
+            try(K1RouteService service=new K1RouteService(new StudioState(),p)) {
+                K1Route read=p.k1RouteTransaction(0,()->service.read(2,0));
+                check(read.equals(p.route),"full route reconstructed with CRC");
+                check(p.slices.equals(usb?List.of(42,42,18):List.of(20,20,20,20,20,2)),"transport slice sizes "+p.slices);
+                for(String fault:List.of("nonce","mode","offset","crc")){
+                    p.fault=fault;
+                    try{p.k1RouteTransaction(0,()->service.read(2,0));throw new AssertionError("accepted "+fault);}catch(IOException expected){}
+                }
+            }finally{p.shutdownTransport();}
+        }
+    }
+    static OledResourceState.Asset asset(int frames) {
+        byte[] bytes=new byte[frames*AhaKeyProtocol.OLED_FRAME_SLOT_SIZE];
+        for(int f=0;f<frames;f++)for(int i=0;i<AhaKeyProtocol.OLED_FRAME_BYTES;i++)bytes[f*AhaKeyProtocol.OLED_FRAME_SLOT_SIZE+i]=(byte)(i*31+f);
+        return OledResourceState.Asset.fromBytes(bytes);
+    }
+    static void completeLongUpload()throws Exception {
+        Peripheral p=new Peripheral(false);p.delayMs=3;var asset=asset(12);AtomicBoolean recorded=new AtomicBoolean();AtomicInteger progress=new AtomicInteger();long at=System.nanoTime();
+        try {
+            OledUploadService.uploadResource(p,0,1,asset,percent->{},()->recorded.set(true),detail->{
+                check(detail.acknowledgedBytes()>progress.getAndSet(detail.acknowledgedBytes()),"progress strictly follows acknowledged payload");
+                check(detail.acknowledgedBytes()==p.acknowledged,"UI progress never exceeds ACKed bytes");
+            });
+            long elapsed=TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.toMillis(System.nanoTime()-at);
+            check(recorded.get()&&p.commits==1&&Arrays.equals(asset.bytes(),p.uploaded),"all actual image bytes reach one confirmed commit");
+            check(elapsed>15000&&!p.isDisplayWriteUncertain(),"healthy progressing upload may exceed one response timeout");
+            System.out.println("LONG_UPLOAD bytes="+asset.bytes().length+" chunks="+p.chunks+" elapsedMs="+elapsed+" injectedAckDelayMs=3");
+        }finally{p.release.countDown();p.shutdownTransport();}
+    }
+    static void interruptedUpload(String action)throws Exception {
+        Peripheral p=new Peripheral(false);p.fault=action.equals("native-hang")?action:"missing-ack";
+        var pool=Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor();
+        try {
+            var result=pool.submit(()->{try{OledUploadService.uploadResource(p,0,1,asset(1),x->{},()->{});return false;}catch(OledUploadService.UploadFailure expected){return !expected.commitUncertain();}});
+            check(p.held.await(3,TimeUnit.SECONDS),"second data chunk entered "+action);long at=System.nanoTime();
+            if(action.equals("cancel"))p.cancelDeviceOperation(0);
+            if(action.equals("disconnect"))p.disconnect();
+            check(result.get(action.equals("missing-ack")?18:4,TimeUnit.SECONDS),"unfinished upload returns without commit "+action);
+            long elapsed=TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.toMillis(System.nanoTime()-at);
+            if(action.equals("cancel")||action.equals("disconnect"))check(elapsed<1500,"cancel/revoke bound "+action);
+            if(action.equals("native-hang"))check(elapsed<3500,"native write deadline");
+            check(p.chunks==2&&p.commits==0,"no continuation/retry/commit after "+action);
+            var registry=new com.example.ahakey.service.connection.DeviceRecoveryRegistry(java.nio.file.Path.of(System.getProperty("user.home"),".ahakey","device-recovery.json"));
+            check(registry.contains("BLE:memory"),"unknown upload remains durably quarantined "+action);
+            p.release.countDown();p.notify(0,AhaKeyProtocol.buildFrame((byte)0x8b,new byte[]{0}));
+            check(p.sent.size()==3,"late ACK cannot restart upload");
+            System.out.println("UPLOAD_FAILURE action="+action+" elapsedMs="+elapsed+" chunks="+p.chunks+" commits="+p.commits);
+        }finally{p.release.countDown();pool.shutdownNow();p.shutdownTransport();}
+    }
+    public static void main(String[] args)throws Exception {
+        System.setProperty("user.home",Files.createTempDirectory("wd007-transfer-").toString());
+        routeReads();completeLongUpload();
+        for(String action:List.of("cancel","disconnect","native-hang","missing-ack"))interruptedUpload(action);
+        System.out.println("PASS actual route/upload scheduler and payload checks="+checks+"; memory peripheral only, no GATT hardware");
+    }
+}

+package com.example.ahakey.service;
+
+import com.example.ahakey.app.StudioController;
+import com.example.ahakey.model.*;
+import com.example.ahakey.model.connection.DeviceTarget;
+import com.example.ahakey.protocol.AhaKeyProtocol;
+import com.example.ahakey.service.connection.DeviceRecoveryRegistry;
+import javafx.application.Platform;
+import java.nio.file.Files;
+import java.util.*;
+import java.util.concurrent.*;
+import static com.example.ahakey.service.Wd007BehaviorChecks.*;
+import static com.example.ahakey.service.Wd007RecoveryControllerChecks.fx;
+
+/** Native reader's terminal seam through actual coordinator/controller; no device or enumeration calls. */
+public final class Wd007UsbTerminationChecks {
+    static final class ReaderProbe extends UsbHidTransport {void failed(){readerTerminatedUnexpectedly();}}
+    static Runnable callback(UsbHidTransport transport)throws Exception{
+        var field=UsbHidTransport.class.getDeclaredField("disconnectHandler");field.setAccessible(true);return (Runnable)field.get(transport);
+    }
+    static void connected(StudioController controller,Wire w)throws Exception{
+        w.frames.clear();w.connectSelected(new DeviceTarget("USB","A","same path, no serial"));
+        await(w::isConnected,"manual configuration connect");
+        readback(controller,w);
+    }
+    static void readback(StudioController controller,Wire w)throws Exception{
+        long end=System.nanoTime()+TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(5);
+        while(System.nanoTime()<end){
+            if(w.frames.stream().filter(s->s.equals("A:135")).count()>=16&&fx(()->!controller.getStudioState().inputReadbackInProgressProperty().get()&&!controller.getStudioState().k1Routes().busy.get()))return;
+            Thread.sleep(10);
+        }
+        throw new AssertionError("controller did not complete readback");
+    }
+    static void atomicTermination()throws Exception{
+        var workers=Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2);
+        try{for(int i=0;i<40;i++){
+            ReaderProbe probe=new ReaderProbe();var calls=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger();probe.onUnexpectedDisconnect(calls::incrementAndGet);var start=new CountDownLatch(1);
+            var close=workers.submit(()->{start.await();probe.cancelPendingIo();return null;});
+            var fail=workers.submit(()->{start.await();probe.failed();return null;});start.countDown();close.get(2,TimeUnit.SECONDS);fail.get(2,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
+            check(calls.get()==(probe.terminatedUnexpectedly()?1:0),"atomic cause and notification agree under simultaneous close/failure "+i);
+            boolean cause=probe.terminatedUnexpectedly();probe.cancelPendingIo();probe.failed();
+            check(probe.terminatedUnexpectedly()==cause,"terminal cause cannot be overwritten "+i);
+        }}finally{workers.shutdownNow();}
+    }
+    public static void main(String[] args)throws Exception{
+        var calls=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger();ReaderProbe probe=new ReaderProbe();probe.onUnexpectedDisconnect(calls::incrementAndGet);probe.failed();probe.failed();
+        check(calls.get()==1,"unexpected terminal step reports once");
+        ReaderProbe closing=new ReaderProbe();closing.onUnexpectedDisconnect(calls::incrementAndGet);closing.close();closing.failed();
+        check(calls.get()==1,"intentional cancellation does not report unexpected termination");
+        atomicTermination();
+        System.setProperty("user.home",Files.createTempDirectory("wd007-usb-termination-").toString());Platform.startup(()->{});Platform.setImplicitExit(false);
+        Wire[] wires=new Wire[1];StudioController controller=fx(()->new StudioController(cb->wires[0]=new Wire(cb),ble->new KimiAhaKeyBridge(ble,0),false));
+        var pool=Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor();
+        try{
+            Wire w=wires[0];connected(controller,w);String a=w.draftIdentity();
+            fx(()->{controller.getStudioState().getKeyConfig(ModeSlot.MODE0,StudioPart.KEY2).setHidCode(31);controller.getStudioState().markDirty(StudioPart.KEY2);return null;});
+            w.pollUsbCandidates();
+            check(a.equals(w.draftIdentity())&&fx(()->controller.getStudioState().getKeyConfig(ModeSlot.MODE0,StudioPart.KEY2).getHidCode()==31),"current USB session retains dirty draft during unchanged observation");
+            var drafts=StudioState.class.getDeclaredField("deviceDrafts");drafts.setAccessible(true);
+            w.disconnect();check(!w.isConnected(),"explicit disconnect stays disconnected");
+            w.readbackHid=7;connected(controller,w);String manualReplacement=w.draftIdentity();
+            check(!a.equals(manualReplacement)&&fx(()->controller.getStudioState().getKeyConfig(ModeSlot.MODE0,StudioPart.KEY2).getHidCode()==7&&!controller.getStudioState().isDirty(StudioPart.KEY2)),"same-path B after intentional disconnect reads B actual configuration without A draft");
+            check(w.candidates.equals(List.of("A"))&&fx(()->((Map<?,?>)drafts.get(controller.getStudioState())).containsKey(a)),"no observed absence or serial; A draft remains separately preserved");
+            fx(()->{controller.getStudioState().getKeyConfig(ModeSlot.MODE0,StudioPart.KEY2).setHidCode(32);controller.getStudioState().markDirty(StudioPart.KEY2);return null;});
+            w.disconnect();w.readbackHid=6;connected(controller,w);
+            check(!manualReplacement.equals(w.draftIdentity())&&fx(()->controller.getStudioState().getKeyConfig(ModeSlot.MODE0,StudioPart.KEY2).getHidCode()==6&&!controller.getStudioState().isDirty(StudioPart.KEY2)&&((Map<?,?>)drafts.get(controller.getStudioState())).containsKey(manualReplacement)),"each explicit USB reconnect reads actual values and preserves old draft without automatic reuse");
+            check(!((DeviceRecoveryRegistry)field(w,"recoveryTargets")).absenceObserved("USB:A"),"intentional reconnect does not manufacture physical absence");
+            Memory old=w.opened.get("A");Runnable late=callback(old);old.failReader();
+            check(!w.isConnected()&&w.getCachedStatus().getSavedBindings()==null,"reader terminal synchronously revokes current connection and approval facts");
+            fx(()->null);check(!fx(()->controller.getDeviceStatus().isConnected()),"actual controller shows disconnected after reader failure");
+            check(w.candidates.equals(List.of("A")),"enumeration remained identical throughout fast replacement");
+            w.pollUsbCandidates();check(!w.isConnected(),"same-path observation never auto reconnects failed reader");
+            w.readbackHid=7;connected(controller,w);String b=w.draftIdentity();long generation=w.connectionGeneration();
+            check(!a.equals(b)&&fx(()->controller.getStudioState().getKeyConfig(ModeSlot.MODE0,StudioPart.KEY2).getHidCode()==7&&!controller.getStudioState().isDirty(StudioPart.KEY2)),"same-path replacement B displays actual readback, not A dirty draft");
+            check(fx(()->((Map<?,?>)drafts.get(controller.getStudioState())).containsKey(a)),"A draft record remains preserved");
+            late.run();check(w.isConnected()&&w.connectionGeneration()==generation&&w.draftIdentity().equals(b),"late old-reader callback cannot disconnect or retire B");
+            fx(()->{controller.getStudioState().getKeyConfig(ModeSlot.MODE0,StudioPart.KEY2).setHidCode(44);controller.getStudioState().markDirty(StudioPart.KEY2);return null;});
+            Memory queuedReader=w.opened.get("A");
+            var commandLock=(java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantLock)field(w,"commandLock");
+            Thread reader=new Thread(queuedReader::failReader,"fixture-reader-terminal");
+            String replacementIdentity;long replacementGeneration;
+            commandLock.lock();try{
+                reader.start();
+                await(()->queuedReader.terminatedUnexpectedly()&&commandLock.hasQueuedThread(reader),"reader cause latched while callback waits on coordinator lock");
+                w.frames.clear();w.readbackHid=8;
+                long intent=((java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong)field(w,"connectionIntent")).incrementAndGet();
+                var open=BleManager.class.getDeclaredMethod("openSelected",DeviceTarget.class,long.class);open.setAccessible(true);
+                open.invoke(w,new DeviceTarget("USB","A","same path race replacement"),intent);
+                replacementIdentity=w.draftIdentity();replacementGeneration=w.connectionGeneration();
+                check(!replacementIdentity.equals(b)&&w.isConnected(),"replacement consumes old unexpected cause before callback can run");
+            }finally{commandLock.unlock();}
+            reader.join(2000);check(!reader.isAlive(),"late reader callback completed after replacement");readback(controller,w);
+            check(w.isConnected()&&w.connectionGeneration()==replacementGeneration&&w.draftIdentity().equals(replacementIdentity),"late queued callback cannot alter new generation or attachment");
+            check(fx(()->controller.getStudioState().getKeyConfig(ModeSlot.MODE0,StudioPart.KEY2).getHidCode()==8&&!controller.getStudioState().isDirty(StudioPart.KEY2)&&((Map<?,?>)drafts.get(controller.getStudioState())).containsKey(b)),"actual controller reads replacement value and retains prior dirty draft separately");
+            check(!((DeviceRecoveryRegistry)field(w,"recoveryTargets")).absenceObserved("USB:A"),"queued I/O failure is not physical absence evidence");
+            w.held=0x73;var pending=pool.submit(()->{try{w.sendCommand(AhaKeyProtocol.buildFrame((byte)0x73,new byte[]{0}));return false;}catch(java.io.IOException expected){return true;}});
+            await(()->w.heldMemory!=null,"pending B write held for terminal failure");w.opened.get("A").failReader();
+            check(pending.get(2,TimeUnit.SECONDS),"reader failure aborts current outstanding command");
+            DeviceRecoveryRegistry registry=(DeviceRecoveryRegistry)field(w,"recoveryTargets");
+            check(registry.contains("USB:A")&&!registry.absenceObserved("USB:A"),"I/O failure quarantines uncertain write without claiming physical absence");
+            w.pollUsbCandidates();check(!registry.absenceObserved("USB:A")&&!w.isConnected(),"unchanged complete enumeration does not clear quarantine or reconnect");
+            w.held=-1;w.connectSelected(new DeviceTarget("USB","A","same path"));await(()->!w.isScanning()&&!w.isConnected(),"manual reconnect without recovery evidence remains disconnected");
+            check(registry.contains("USB:A")&&!w.refreshAutoApproval()&&!fx(()->controller.getDeviceStatus().isConnected()),"reader failure retains recovery registration and cannot re-admit writes, UI connection or AUTO");
+            System.out.println("PASS "+checks+" IR05 reader/coordinator/controller checks; stable enumeration, no real HID");
+        }finally{pool.shutdownNow();fx(()->{controller.shutdown();return null;});Platform.exit();}
+    }

+package com.example.ahakey.service;
+
+import com.example.ahakey.App;
+import com.example.ahakey.app.StudioController;
+import com.example.ahakey.model.OledResourceState;
+import javafx.application.Platform;
+import javafx.scene.Scene;
+import javafx.scene.layout.Pane;
+import javafx.stage.*;
+import java.nio.file.Files;
+import java.util.concurrent.*;
+import static com.example.ahakey.service.Wd007BehaviorChecks.*;
+
+/** Production X and menu-exit handlers; only final process termination and USB are replaced. */
+public final class Wd007WindowExitChecks {
+    static class Probe extends App {final CountDownLatch exited=new CountDownLatch(1);@Override protected void exitRuntime(){exited.countDown();}}
+    static <T>T fx(Callable<T> call)throws Exception{var done=new CompletableFuture<T>();Platform.runLater(()->{try{done.complete(call.call());}catch(Throwable e){done.completeExceptionally(e);}});return done.get(8,TimeUnit.SECONDS);}
+    static void set(Object value,String name,Object data)throws Exception{var field=App.class.getDeclaredField(name);field.setAccessible(true);field.set(value,data);}
+    static void invoke(App app,String name)throws Exception{var method=App.class.getDeclaredMethod(name);method.setAccessible(true);method.invoke(app);}
+    public static void main(String[] args)throws Exception{
+        System.setProperty("user.home",Files.createTempDirectory("wd007-exit-").toString());
+        Platform.startup(()->Platform.setImplicitExit(false));
+        Wire wire=new Wire();
+        try{
+            StudioController controller=fx(()->new StudioController(cb->wire,KimiAhaKeyBridge::new,false));
+            wire.connectSelected(new com.example.ahakey.model.connection.DeviceTarget("USB","A","A"));await(wire::isConnected,"exit fixture connected");wire.readFirmwareCatalogKey();
+            var service=controller.getDisplayConfigService();
+            await(()->service.state().ready.get(),"local library ready");
+            Probe app=new Probe();Stage stage=fx(()->{
+                service.connectionChanged();var current=service.state().current().replace(OledResourceState.Asset.fromBytes(new byte[28672]));service.state().put(current);
+                Stage window=new Stage();window.setScene(new Scene(new Pane(),320,160));set(app,"controller",controller);set(app,"primaryStage",window);invoke(app,"installWindowCloseHandler");
+                controller.setApplicationExit(()->{try{invoke(app,"shutdownApplication");}catch(Exception e){throw new RuntimeException(e);}});window.show();return window;
+            });
+            wire.held=0x8B;fx(()->{service.upload(service.state().current());return null;});await(()->wire.heldRequest!=null,"real display upload awaits chunk ACK");
+            fx(()->{stage.fireEvent(new WindowEvent(stage,WindowEvent.WINDOW_CLOSE_REQUEST));return null;});
+            long end=System.nanoTime()+TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(2);while(fx(stage::isShowing)&&System.nanoTime()<end)Thread.sleep(5);
+            check(!fx(stage::isShowing)&&controller.getStudioState().uploadingOledProperty().get(),"X hides to tray and preserves pending upload");
+            long at=System.nanoTime();fx(()->{controller.requestApplicationExit();return null;});
+            check(fx(()->true),"FX responsive during exit cleanup");check(app.exited.await(6,TimeUnit.SECONDS),"actual menu exit reaches termination within bounded cleanup");
+            check(TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.toMillis(System.nanoTime()-at)<6000,"exit deadline measured");
+            check(wire.frames.stream().noneMatch(f->f.endsWith(":140")),"exit does not COMMIT unfinished upload");
+            System.out.println("PASS actual X/upload/menu-exit checks="+checks+"; final System.exit stubbed, no device");
+        }finally{fx(()->{for(Window w:java.util.List.copyOf(Window.getWindows()))w.hide();return null;});Platform.exit();}
+    }

+package com.example.ahakey.service.connection;
+
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.*;
+import java.io.*;
+import java.net.*;
+import java.util.concurrent.*;
+import java.util.concurrent.atomic.*;
+
+/** Real loopback framing plus blocked-stream fixtures; never starts the bridge or a device. */
+public final class ManagedBridgeClientChecks {
+    static final ObjectMapper JSON=new ObjectMapper();
+    static int checks;
+    public static final class StubbornProcess extends Process {
+        public volatile boolean alive=true;
+        public int destroys;
+        public OutputStream getOutputStream(){return OutputStream.nullOutputStream();}
+        public InputStream getInputStream(){return InputStream.nullInputStream();}
+        public InputStream getErrorStream(){return InputStream.nullInputStream();}
+        public int waitFor(){if(alive)throw new IllegalStateException("still alive");return 0;}
+        public boolean waitFor(long timeout,TimeUnit unit){return !alive;}
+        public int exitValue(){if(alive)throw new IllegalThreadStateException();return 0;}
+        public void destroy(){destroys++;}
+        public Process destroyForcibly(){destroys++;return this;}
+        public boolean isAlive(){return alive;}
+    }
+    static void check(boolean value,String name){if(!value)throw new AssertionError(name);checks++;}
+    static JsonNode read(Socket s)throws Exception{
+        DataInputStream in=new DataInputStream(s.getInputStream());check(in.readUnsignedByte()==0x10,"request envelope");
+        int n=in.readUnsignedByte()|in.readUnsignedByte()<<8;return JSON.readTree(in.readNBytes(n));
+    }
+    static void send(Socket s,int type,JsonNode node)throws Exception{
+        byte[] b=JSON.writeValueAsBytes(node);var out=s.getOutputStream();out.write(type);out.write(b.length&255);out.write(b.length>>8);out.write(b);out.flush();
+    }
+    static class BlockedSocket extends Socket {
+        final CountDownLatch entered=new CountDownLatch(1),released=new CountDownLatch(1);
+        volatile boolean shut;
+        @Override public void setTcpNoDelay(boolean ignored){}
+        @Override public boolean isClosed(){return shut;}
+        @Override public void close(){shut=true;released.countDown();}
+        void block()throws IOException{try{released.await();}catch(InterruptedException e){Thread.currentThread().interrupt();}throw new IOException("closed");}
+        @Override public InputStream getInputStream(){return new InputStream(){public int read()throws IOException{block();return -1;}};}
+        @Override public OutputStream getOutputStream(){return new OutputStream(){public void write(int b)throws IOException{entered.countDown();block();}};}
+    }
+    public static void main(String[] args)throws Exception{
+        try(var client=new ManagedBridgeClient();var listener=new ServerSocket(0,1,InetAddress.getLoopbackAddress());var local=new Socket("127.0.0.1",listener.getLocalPort());var peer=listener.accept()){
+            client.attach(local);
+            var pool=Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2);
+            try{
+                var first=pool.submit(()->client.request("first",1000));JsonNode a=read(peer);
+                var second=pool.submit(()->client.request("second",1000));JsonNode b=read(peer);
+                send(peer,0x90,ManagedBridgeClient.fields().put("version",1).put("bridgeInstanceId","one").put("requestId",b.path("requestId").asText()).put("ok",true).put("value",2));
+                send(peer,0x90,ManagedBridgeClient.fields().put("version",1).put("bridgeInstanceId","one").put("requestId",a.path("requestId").asText()).put("ok",true).put("value",1));
+                check(first.get().path("value").asInt()==1 && second.get().path("value").asInt()==2,"correlation survives out of order replies");
+            }finally{pool.shutdownNow();}
+        }
+        try(var client=new ManagedBridgeClient()){
+            BlockedSocket socket=new BlockedSocket();client.attach(socket);long at=System.nanoTime();
+            try{client.request("blocked",120);throw new AssertionError("deadline missing");}catch(IOException expected){}
+            check(TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.toMillis(System.nanoTime()-at)<800,"blocked write has total deadline");
+            check(socket.shut,"deadline closes owning socket");
+        }
+        try(var client=new ManagedBridgeClient();var listener=new ServerSocket(0,2,InetAddress.getLoopbackAddress())){
+            var entered=new CountDownLatch(1);var release=new CountDownLatch(1);var delivered=new AtomicInteger();
+            client.onEvent(event->{if(event.path("event").asText().equals("hold")){entered.countDown();try{release.await();}catch(InterruptedException e){Thread.currentThread().interrupt();}}else delivered.incrementAndGet();});
+            try(var a=new Socket("127.0.0.1",listener.getLocalPort());var pa=listener.accept();var b=new Socket("127.0.0.1",listener.getLocalPort());var pb=listener.accept()){
+                client.attach(a);send(pa,0x91,ManagedBridgeClient.fields().put("version",1).put("eventSeq",1).put("event","hold"));
+                check(entered.await(1,TimeUnit.SECONDS),"event queue deliberately held");
+                pa.close();Thread.sleep(30);client.attach(b);release.countDown();Thread.sleep(70);
+                check(delivered.get()==0,"queued old disconnect cannot reach new connection");
+                send(pb,0x91,ManagedBridgeClient.fields().put("version",1).put("eventSeq",1).put("event","new"));
+                long end=System.nanoTime()+TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(1);while(delivered.get()==0&&System.nanoTime()<end)Thread.sleep(5);
+                check(delivered.get()==1,"new connection owns independent event sequence");
+            }finally{release.countDown();}
+        }
+        try(var client=new ManagedBridgeClient()){
+            BlockedSocket old=new BlockedSocket();client.attach(old);Object identity=client.ownerIdentity();old.close();
+            BlockedSocket replacement=new BlockedSocket();client.attach(replacement);
+            client.releaseSession(identity,ManagedBridgeClient.fields().put("sessionId","A"));
+            check(!replacement.shut,"failed old release cannot close new bridge connection");
+            client.releaseSession(identity,ManagedBridgeClient.fields().put("sessionId","A"));
+            check(!replacement.shut,"retired release is repeatable, no exceptional barrier");
+        }
+        System.out.println("PASS "+checks+" managed IPC behavior checks; loopback only");
+    }
+}

+package com.example.ahakey.service;
+
+import java.io.*;
+import java.net.*;
+import java.lang.reflect.Field;
+import java.util.Arrays;
+
+/** Isolated loopback ports only; never calls connect(), HID discovery, or the application. */
+public final class KimiBridgeLifecycleCheck {
+    private static int checks;
+    private static final class Transport extends BleManager {
+        long generation = 1;
+        boolean connected = true;
+        boolean usb = true;
+        Transport() { super(null);getCachedStatus().setConnected(true);getCachedStatus().setSavedBindings(Wd007BehaviorChecks.side(true));getCachedStatus().setSwitchState(0); }
+        @Override public boolean refreshAutoApproval(){return connected&&getCachedStatus().isAutoApproval();}
+        @Override public long connectionGeneration() { return generation; }
+        @Override public boolean isConnected() { return connected; }
+        @Override public boolean isUsbConnected() { return usb; }
+        @Override public void connect() { throw new AssertionError("Hardware forbidden"); }
+        @Override protected boolean isUsbDevicePresent() { throw new AssertionError("HID forbidden"); }
+    }
+    private static Object field(Object value, String name) throws Exception {
+        Field field = value.getClass().getDeclaredField(name);
+        field.setAccessible(true);
+        return field.get(value);
+    }
+    private static int port(KimiAhaKeyBridge bridge) throws Exception {
+        return ((ServerSocket) field(field(bridge, "session"), "listener")).getLocalPort();
+    }
+    private static void check(boolean condition, String message) {
+        if (!condition) throw new AssertionError(message);
+        checks++;
+    }
+    private static Socket client(int port) throws Exception {
+        Socket socket = new Socket("127.0.0.1", port);
+        socket.setSoTimeout(2000);
+        return socket;
+    }
+    private static byte[] query(Socket client, int type) throws Exception {
+        client.getOutputStream().write(new byte[]{(byte)type, 0, 0});
+        DataInputStream in = new DataInputStream(client.getInputStream());
+        int response = in.readUnsignedByte();
+        int length = in.readUnsignedByte() | in.readUnsignedByte() << 8;
+        check(response == (type == 3 ? 0x82 : 0x83), "unchanged response type");
+        return in.readNBytes(length);
+    }
+    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
+        if(args.length==2 && args[0].equals("consumer-probe")) {
+            Transport fixture=new Transport();
+            switch(args[1]) {
+                case "swapped-auto" -> {fixture.getCachedStatus().setSavedBindings(Wd007BehaviorChecks.side(false));fixture.getCachedStatus().setSwitchState(1);}
+                case "custom" -> fixture.getCachedStatus().setSwitchState(1);
+                case "unknown" -> fixture.getCachedStatus().clearSavedBindings();
+                case "disconnected" -> fixture.connected=false;
+                case "auto" -> {}
+                default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException();
+            }
+            KimiAhaKeyBridge probe=new KimiAhaKeyBridge(fixture,0);try{probe.start();System.out.println("KIMI_PROBE_PORT="+port(probe));System.out.flush();System.in.read();}finally{probe.close();}return;
+        }
+        Transport transport = new Transport();
+        KimiAhaKeyBridge bridge = new KimiAhaKeyBridge(transport, 0);
+        try {
+            bridge.connectionChanged();
+            for (int round = 0; round < 30; round++) {
+                int oldPort = port(bridge);
+                try (Socket oldClient = client(oldPort)) {
+                    check(Arrays.equals(query(oldClient, 3), new byte[]{1,0,0,1}), "status payload");
+                    bridge.prepareForConnection();
+                    oldClient.setSoTimeout(200);
+                    check(oldClient.getInputStream().read() == -1, "stop closes accepted clients immediately");
+                    // Same-generation late connect callback cannot reclaim the BLE port.
+                    bridge.connectionChanged();
+                    check(field(bridge,"session") == null, "late USB callback blocked while switching");
+                    try (ServerSocket bleListener = new ServerSocket()) {
+                        bleListener.setReuseAddress(true);
+                        bleListener.bind(new InetSocketAddress("127.0.0.1", oldPort));
+                        transport.generation++;
+                        transport.usb = false;
+                        bridge.connectionChanged();
+                        check(field(bridge,"session") != null, "BLE now uses Java compatibility listener too");
+                    }
+                    transport.generation++;
+                    transport.usb = true;
+                    bridge.connectionChanged();
+                    Object newSession = field(bridge,"session");
+                    bridge.connectionChanged(); // delayed disconnect notification reconciles current USB
+                    check(field(bridge,"session") == newSession, "late disconnect cannot stop current USB");
+                    try (Socket fresh = client(port(bridge))) {
+                        query(fresh, 3);
+                        byte[] info = query(fresh, 4);
+                        check(info.length == 10 && info[6] == 0, "USB reconnect preserves Kimi auto state");
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            transport.connected = false;
+            bridge.connectionChanged();
+            check(field(bridge,"session") == null, "disconnect stops service");
+            bridge.stop(); bridge.stop();
+            transport.connected = true;
+            transport.generation++;
+            bridge.connectionChanged();
+            bridge.close();
+            bridge.connectionChanged(); bridge.start();
+            check(field(bridge,"session") == null, "shutdown rejects late start");
+        } finally { bridge.close(); }
+        try (ServerSocket occupied = new ServerSocket(0,10,InetAddress.getLoopbackAddress())) {
+            int busyPort = occupied.getLocalPort();
+            KimiAhaKeyBridge retry = new KimiAhaKeyBridge(transport, busyPort);
+            try {
+                retry.start();
+                check(field(retry,"session") == null, "occupied external port not taken over");
+                occupied.close();
+                retry.start();
+                try (Socket socket = client(busyPort)) { query(socket, 3); query(socket, 4); }
+            } finally { retry.close(); }
+        }
+        System.out.println("PASS " + checks + " Kimi checks; 30 USB/BLE/USB rounds; ephemeral ports only");
+    }
 }
